### [1] [An Exhaustive Census of Main-Diagonal Symmetric Costas Arrays of Orders 37-42](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28690)
*Bogdan Dumitru,Cristian Budala*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文通过穷举枚举确定了37到42阶所有主对角线对称的Costas阵列，发现所有阵列均属于已知的有限域构造，不存在零星阵列；同时开发了一个随机对合的碰撞模型，首次两项碰撞指数为n²/18+n^{3/2}/360。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Costas阵列在雷达、声呐和通信系统中具有重要意义。主对角线对称的Costas阵列（称为对称Costas阵列）是其中的重要子类，此前已知到36阶的普查结果，但37-42阶的情况未知。确定是否存在不符合已知构造规律的"零星"对称Costas阵列是核心问题。

Method: 采用穷举枚举法对37-42阶的所有主对角线对称Costas阵列进行全面搜索。同时，开发了一个基于随机对合的碰撞模型，从解析上推导了碰撞指数的前两项（n²/18+n^{3/2}/360，余项为O(n)），并使用经验数据对低阶模型和丛聚修正进行校准。

Result: 37阶有4个对称Costas阵列，38阶为0个，39阶16个，40阶2个，41阶12个，42阶4个。所有阵列均属于已知的有限域构造：37-40阶为F_41上的Lempel阵列及其角删减/增广，41阶为F_43上的Lempel阵列，42阶为Rickard-Golomb族中的两个角增广及一个反向补对。结合此前到36阶的普查，结果表明24-42阶不存在零星主对角线对称Costas阵列。

Conclusion: 新的六个普查结果支持碰撞模型的预测，即零星对称Costas阵列的期望数量快速下降。这为"所有对称Costas阵列最终都可由已知有限域构造生成"提供了进一步证据，同时所提出的碰撞模型也为理解这类组合结构的出现概率提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: We determine by exhaustive enumeration all main-diagonal symmetric Costas arrays of orders 37 through 42. There are 4 arrays of order 37, none of order 38, 16 of order 39, 2 of order 40, 12 of order 41, and 4 of order 42. All belong to known finite-field constructions. The arrays at orders 37-40 are Lempel arrays over $\mathbb{F}_{41}$ or their corner deletions and augmentations; the twelve arrays at order 41 are the Lempel arrays over $\mathbb{F}_{43}$; and the order-42 census consists of two corner augmentations and a reverse-complement pair in the Rickard-Golomb family. Combined with the published census through order 36, these results show that no sporadic main-diagonal symmetric Costas array occurs at orders 24-42. We also develop a collision model for random involutions. The first two terms of its collision exponent are derived analytically, $n^2/18+n^{3/2}/360$, with an $O(n)$ remainder. The lower-order model and clumping correction are empirical. The six new censuses are consistent with the model's prediction of a rapidly declining expected sporadic population.

</details>


### [2] [Riordan array representation of recursive polynomial sequences, orthogonal polynomial sequences, and $d$-orthogonal polynomial sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28834)
*Tian-Xiao He*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究多项式序列的系数矩阵与Riordan阵列之间的关系。对于二阶递推给出完整刻画，对任意阶递推给出必要条件和完全分解判据。更进一步，建立了适用于任意正交多项式序列(OPS)的production-matrix框架：任意OPS的系数矩阵的production matrix总是编码三项递推的三对角Jacobi矩阵，且A⁻¹首列给出矩序列。最后将该框架推广到d-正交多项式序列，证明其系数矩阵可逆、production matrix为(d+2)-带状的下Hessenberg矩阵并编码(d+2)项递推，且逆矩阵前d列恢复定义向量泛函的矩序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前Riordan阵列方法仅适用于系数矩阵可表示为Riordan阵列的特殊多项式序列，而如Legendre多项式等具有非恒定递推系数的序列无法用该方法刻画。作者希望建立一个统一的production-matrix框架，将Riordan阵列方法推广到任意正交多项式序列乃至d-正交多项式序列，从而获得更一般的结构化刻画。

Method: 1) 对二阶递推给出Riordan阵列表示的完整特征刻画；对ℓ≥3阶递推给出必要条件及完全分解判据。2) 引入production matrix P_{A⁻¹}=ASA⁻¹，证明对任意OPS其总是编码三项递推的三对角Jacobi矩阵，且A⁻¹首列即为矩序列。3) 以Legendre（非Riordan型）和Chebyshev（Riordan型）多项式验证框架，并对广义Gegenbauer-Humbert OPS给出系数矩阵及其逆的显式闭式。4) 将框架推广到d-正交多项式序列，利用production matrix的带状结构（(d+2)-banded下Hessenberg）对应(d+2)项递推，并通过逆矩阵前d列恢复矩序列，建立d-正交性与(d+1)-Hessenberg广义Riordan阵列的对应。

Result: 给出了二阶递推的完整Riordan阵列表征、高阶递推的必要条件和完全分解判据；建立了适用于所有OPS的production-matrix框架，统一了Riordan型与非Riordan型情况；对d-正交多项式序列证明了系数矩阵可逆性、production matrix的带状结构及其与(d+1)-Hessenberg广义Riordan阵列的等价性，将经典d=1三对角对应推广到higher d。

Conclusion: 论文建立的production-matrix框架将Riordan阵列方法从特殊情形推广为适用于任意正交多项式序列乃至d-正交多项式序列的普适工具，一方面统一了该领域的经典结果，另一方面为高阶正交性提供了新的结构化视角。

Abstract: We study when a polynomial sequence $\{p_n(x)\}$ satisfying a linear homogeneous recurrence with polynomial coefficients admits an ordinary Riordan array as its coefficient matrix. For second-order recurrences $p_{n+2}(x)=a(x)p_{n+1}(x)+b(x)p_n(x)$, we give a complete characterization. We extend this to a necessary condition and a full factorization criterion for recurrences of arbitrary order $\ell \geq 3$.
  We then develop a production-matrix framework for arbitrary orthogonal polynomial sequences (OPS), not restricted to the Riordan-array-type case: for any lower-triangular invertible coefficient matrix $A$ of an OPS, the production matrix $P_{A^{-1}}=ASA^{-1}$ is always the tridiagonal Jacobi matrix encoding the three-term recurrence, and the first column of $A^{-1}$ always gives the moment sequence -- even when, as for the Legendre polynomials, the recurrence coefficients are non-constant and no Riordan array representation of the coefficient matrix exists. We illustrate this with Legendre and Chebyshev polynomials as, respectively, non-Riordan-type and Riordan-type examples, and give an explicit closed-form coefficient matrix and its inverse for the generalized Gegenbauer--Humbert OPS. Finally, we extend the framework to $d$-orthogonal polynomial sequences, showing that the coefficient matrix of a $d$-orthogonal sequence is always invertible, that its associated production matrix is $(d+2)$-banded (lower Hessenberg) and encodes the corresponding $(d+2)$-term recurrence, and that the first $d$ columns of the inverse matrix recover the moment sequences of the defining vector functional -- identifying $d$-orthogonality with $(d+1)$-Hessenberg generalized Riordan arrays and extending the classical $d=1$ tridiagonal correspondence.

</details>


### [3] [Braess' Paradox in Uniform Affine Grid Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28938)
*Andy Lu,Steven J. Miller*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了有向矩形网格交通网络中的Braess悖论，该悖论指增加一条边反而增加总通行时间。通过类比电网给出来确定网格中添加弦后总通行时间变化的界限，给出了弦引发悖论的充要条件，计算了100×100以内网格中这类弦的精确比例（发现此类弦稀少，且在长宽比约为2:1时比例最大），改进了Braess比的上界使其仅依赖网格维度（正方形上逼近1.207，细长网格上为4/3），并证明任何使比率最大的弦必须具有零延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Braess悖论是网络中反直觉的现象：添加边反而使总通行时间增加。已有研究对一般网络给出了Braess比的4/3上界，但对具体结构（如有向矩形网格）中悖论发生的条件、频率以及更精确的上界缺乏深入理解。本文旨在对矩形网格这一交通网络的重要模型进行系统性刻画。

Method: 利用与电网的类比（将拥塞网络的流量分配映射到电阻网络分析），推导出添加弦后总通行时间变化的界。通过充要条件刻画和弦分类，利用数值/组合方法计算其精确比例。通过网格维度依赖的分析推导出Braess比的新上界。

Result: 给出了弦引发悖论的充要条件；计算了维度至多100的所有网格中悖论引发弦的精确比例，该比例很小且长宽比2:1附近最大；将Braess比的上界从统一的4/3改进为仅依赖网格维度的上界，正方形网格上逼近约1.207，薄网格上保持为4/3；证明比率最大化的弦必为零延迟。

Conclusion: 在矩形网格中，Braess悖论相关弦是稀少的，且其发生具有明确的结构条件。网格纵横比显著影响悖论发生的频率；改进的上界说明矩形网格的Braess比明显小于一般网络的4/3上界，且零延迟弦在最大化悖论效应中起着关键作用。

Abstract: Braess' Paradox is the phenomenon in which adding an edge to a congestion network increases total travel time. We study the paradox in directed rectangular grids where every edge shares the latency function $\ell(x)=ax+b$ and an added chord has latency $\ell_{*}(x) = cx+d$, where $a > 0$ and $b,c,d \ge 0$. Using an analogy with electrical networks, we bound the change in total travel time. We then give a necessary and sufficient condition for a chord to induce the paradox for some choice of nonnegative coefficients and compute the exact proportion of such chords in all grids with dimensions at most $100$. Such chords are scarce, and the fraction is maximized near an aspect ratio of $2:1$. We then improve the established $4/3$ upper bound on the Braess Ratio to one depending only on the grid dimensions, approaching $1.207$ on squares and $4/3$ on thin grids. Finally, we prove any ratio-maximizing chord must have zero latency.

</details>


### [4] [A Chvátal--Erdős type condition for supereulerian digraphs with $α_{2}=4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29101)
*Zirui Liu,Jin Yan,Jia Zhou*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文刻画了满足α₂(D)=4的强连通有向图的超欧拉性，证明当λ(D)≥2且α₂(D)=4时，强连通有向图D是超欧拉图当且仅当D不属于一个由2-弧强连通有向图组成的例外族H；同时证明所有满足α₂(D)=4且λ(D)≥3的强连通有向图都是超欧拉图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超欧拉有向图（包含生成闭迹的有向图）是图论中的一个重要研究对象，其与弧连通度、有向哈密顿圈等经典问题密切相关。此前已有关于α₂(D)较小取值（如α₂(D)≤2）情形的超欧拉性刻画，本文旨在将这一刻画推广到α₂(D)=4的情形，进一步完善超欧拉有向图的结构理论。

Method: 本文通过证明一个充分必要条件来刻画超欧拉性：设D为强连通有向图且α₂(D)=4，若λ(D)≥2，则D是超欧拉的当且仅当D不属于某个由2-弧强连通有向图构成的例外族H。此外，还证明了当λ(D)≥3时，所有满足α₂(D)=4的强连通有向图均为超欧拉图。证明方法基于有向图的弧连通度、不诱导2-圈的顶点集等结构分析。

Result: 主要结果有两个：(1) 强连通有向图D满足α₂(D)=4且λ(D)≥2时，D是超欧拉的当且仅当D∉H，其中H是一个具有α₂(D)=4的2-弧强连通有向图的例外族；(2) 所有满足α₂(D)=4且λ(D)≥3的强连通有向图都是超欧拉图。

Conclusion: 本文完整刻画了α₂(D)=4情形下的超欧拉性临界条件，给出了2-弧连通情形下的完整分类（唯一例外族H）以及3-弧连通情形下的充分条件，深化了对超欧拉有向图与弧连通度之间关系的理解。

Abstract: A digraph is \textbf{supereulerian} if it contains a spanning closed trail. Let $α_2(D)$ denote the maximum cardinality of a vertex set inducing no 2-cycle. In this paper, we characterize supereulerianity in a strong digraph $D$ with $α_2(D)=4$ by proving that a strong digraph $D$ with $α_2(D)=4$ and $λ(D)\ge 2$ is supereulerian if and only if $D$ does not belong to an exceptional family $\mathcal H$ of $2$-arc-strong digraphs with $α_2(D)=4$. Furthermore, every strong digraph satisfying $α_2(D)=4$ and $λ(D)\geq3$ is supereulerian.

</details>


### [5] [Cluster Geometry of Universal Schubert Polynomials I: Geometric Bases and Schubert Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29125)
*Jiarui Fei*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究Fulton通用Schubert多项式作为上三角幺模群上的正则函数，建立了其与三角形簇结构、抛物Bruhat区间以及几何基元（generic、canonical、Mirković-Vilonen）之间的深刻联系，证明了在特定条件下Schubert多项式在共轭作用下的齐次性与对应canonical元素等价，并给出了具体的反例分类结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fulton的通用Schubert多项式是Schubert演算与表示理论交叉领域的核心对象，其在三角形簇结构下的组合与几何性质尚未被系统刻画。理解其齐次性、几何基元之间的关系以及相关转移矩阵的结构，对于建立Schubert多项式与量子群、丛代数及箭图表示之间的联系至关重要。

Method: 利用上三角幺模群的三角簇结构为每个置换分配Schubert g-向量；通过仿射单模变换论证其凸包与笛卡尔积单纯形的等价性；借助楼梯箭图Grassmannian层状结构将根次纤维实现为抛物Bruhat区间；结合齐次性准则、Λ_Q-刚性条件以及转移矩阵的支撑成分分析，对几何基元进行系统比较。

Result: 证明Schubert g-向量的凸包单模等价于Δ_1×⋯×Δ_{N-1}；明确刻画了Schubert多项式在对角共轭下齐次的充分必要条件（等价于对应的canonical元素）；建立了齐次性与Λ_Q-刚性的蕴含关系；分类了几何到Schubert转移矩阵的单位列，确定了PBW到几何和码到Schubert转移矩阵的支撑成分；并构造了一个S_10中的置换实例，展示三种几何基元在此情形下互不相同。

Conclusion: 该工作系统地建立了Schubert多项式的组合结构（g-向量、Bruhat区间）与其几何性质（齐次性、几何基元）之间的完整对应关系，揭示了Fulton标准基本模在多种基下的结构异同，为后续研究Schubert演算与箭图表示的深层联系提供了新工具与具体反例。

Abstract: We study Fulton's universal Schubert polynomials $\mathfrak S_w(c)$ as regular functions on the upper unitriangular group $U_N$. The standard triangular cluster structure associates a Schubert $\sf g$-vector to every permutation. Their convex hull is unimodularly equivalent to $Δ_1\times\cdots\timesΔ_{N-1}$, and their root-degree fibers are parabolic Bruhat intervals realized by the strata of staircase quiver Grassmannians. The geometric (i.e., generic, canonical, and Mirković--Vilonen) elements indexed by these vectors form integral bases of Fulton's standard-elementary module. We prove that $\mathfrak S_w(c)$ is homogeneous under diagonal conjugation if and only if it is the corresponding canonical element, and that homogeneity of $\mathfrak S_w(c)$ implies $Λ_Q$-rigidity of $Z_{\sf g_w}$. We also classify simultaneously the unit columns of the geometric-to-Schubert transitions, determine support components of the PBW-to-geometric and code-to-Schubert transitions, and exhibit a permutation $w\in S_{10}$ for which the three geometric basis elements are distinct.

</details>


### [6] [On $(1,2^4)$ and $(1,2^5)$-packing edge-coloring of sparse subcubic graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29163)
*Seog-Jin Kim,Xujun Liu,Boyan Xu*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了亚立方平面图的(1^j,2^k)-打包边染色问题,证明了存在有限的最小正整数k1和k2,使得围长至少为k1/k2的亚立方平面图分别具有(1,2^5)-和(1,2^4)-打包边染色,并给出5≤k1≤12和6≤k2≤16的界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hocquard等人发现一个围长为3的亚立方平面图不具有(1,2^5)-打包边染色,并猜想所有亚立方平面图具有(1,2^6)-打包边染色。此外,对任意固定正整数k,存在围长为k的亚立方平面图不具有(1,2^3)-打包边染色。因此自然需要研究对于较大围长的亚立方平面图,是否存在更大阶数的(1,2^k)-打包边染色,特别是确定使得所有满足围长条件的亚立方平面图都可染色的最小围长阈值k1和k2。

Method: 作者通过构造分析和证明方法研究亚立方平面图中围长与打包边染色数之间的关系。针对(1,2^5)-打包边染色,给出了围长阈值k1的下界5和上界12;针对(1,2^4)-打包边染色,给出了围长阈值k2的下界6和上界16。其中下界通过构造反例图获得,上界通过证明围长满足条件的图必定可染来获得。

Result: 证明了k1和k2都是有限的,且具体得到5≤k1≤12和6≤k2≤16。

Conclusion: 亚立方平面图中,围长对打包边染色可行性有显著影响。本文给出了两个关键围长阈值k1和k2的具体有限范围,为进一步精化这些阈值提供了基础。

Abstract: A $(1^j,2^k)$-packing edge-coloring of a graph $G$ is a partition of the edge set $E(G)$ into $j$ matchings and $k$ induced matchings. Hocquard, Lajou, and Lu\v zar found a subcubic planar graph of girth $3$ that has no $(1,2^5)$-packing edge-coloring and also conjectured that every subcubic planar graph has a $(1,2^6)$-packing edge-coloring. We also notice that for every fixed positive integer $k$ there exists a subcubic planar graph with girth $k$ that is not $(1,2^3)$-packing edge-colorable.
  It is natural to consider what is the minimum positive integer $k_1$ such that every subcubic planar graph with girth at least $k_1$ is $(1,2^5)$-packing edge-colorable. Furthermore, we also consider what is the minimum positive integer $k_2$ such that every subcubic planar graph with girth at least $k_2$ is $(1,2^4)$-packing edge-colorable. In this paper, we show both $k_1$ and $k_2$ are finite, and in fact $5 \le k_1 \le 12$ and $6 \le k_2 \le 16$.

</details>


### [7] [Resolvable quantum Latin squares with maximal cardinality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29201)
*Yuyuan Zhang,Haitao Cao*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了可解量子拉丁方（RQLS）的存在性问题，利用完全映射和将经典奇异直积构造推广到量子领域，确定了具有最大基数的RQLS(n)的存在性，仅余17个可能的例外情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子拉丁方是经典拉丁方在量子信息论中的推广，在量子纠错、量子纠缠和量子门分解等领域有重要应用。可解量子拉丁方要求存在n个两两不相交的横截，其存在性问题此前尚未完全解决，本文旨在系统地确定最大基数RQLS(n)的存在性。

Method: 本文采用两种关键技术：(1) 利用完全映射（complete mappings）来构造RQLS中的不相交横截；(2) 将经典拉丁方的奇异直积构造方法推广到量子拉丁方领域，通过这种推广的构造方法来生成新的RQLS。

Result: 通过上述构造方法，本文确定了最大基数RQLS(n)的存在性，将所有可能的情况归约到仅剩17个未知例外（possible exceptions），大幅缩小了开放问题的范围。

Conclusion: 本文在可解量子拉丁方的构造理论上取得了实质性进展，将经典组合设计中的工具成功拓展到量子领域，为日后完全解决RQLS的存在性问题奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: A resolvable quantum Latin square of order $n$, denoted by $\text{RQLS}(n)$, is a quantum Latin square admitting $n$ pairwise disjoint transversals. By employing complete mappings and extending the classical singular direct product construction to the quantum setting, we determine the existence of maximal-cardinality $\text{RQLS}(n)$s with $17$ possible exceptions.

</details>


### [8] [Cyclically Compatible Deformations of the Braid Arrangement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29203)
*Yanru Chen,Ang Li,Suijie Wang*

Abstract: We introduce and study equivariant analytic spectral invariants associated with the Açıkmeşe lift of a graph with self-loops $G_S$. We introduce the equivariant heat character and establish a trace-norm stability estimate. Abstract: A de Bruijn polyomino has colored cells and includes exactly one instance of each possible coloring of another polyomino with those colors. As a step toward finding these, we introduce the problem of finding the smallest polyominoes containing at least $N$ translated copies of the polyomino $p$, allowing overlaps. We say these are $(p,N)$-dense and have size $a_{p,N}$. For certain pairs of polyominoes $p$ and $q$ of equal size, we show there are polyomino transformations relating $a_{p,N}$ and $a_{q,N}$. Using these transformations, we identify classes of polyominoes which share the sequence $(a_{p,N})_{N=1}^\infty$. We give closed forms for $(a_{p,N})_{N=1}^\infty$ for most polyominoes with up to five cells. Leveraging these results we introduce novel de Bruijn polyominoes, as well as other colored polyforms with de Bruijn-like properties.

Abstract: We develop a method for evaluating finite and infinite series involving Catalan numbers by specializing alternating binomial identities at half-integer parameters. Abstract: We resolve three conjectures proposed by Tokushige in 2013 about cross $t$-intersecting families of subsets and integer sequences. For $0<p<1$ and $\mathcal F\subseteq2^{[n]}$, define the $p$-biased measure by $μ_p(\mathcal F)=\sum_{F\in\mathcal F}p^{|F|}(1-p)^{n-|F|}$. Two families $\mathcal F_1,\mathcal F_2\subseteq2^{[n]}$ are cross $t$-intersecting if $|F_1\cap F_2|\geq t$ for every $F_1\in\mathcal F_1$ and $F_2\in\mathcal F_2$.   this completely resolves Tokushige's measure conjecture. We also prove that every pair whose measure product is close to the maximum must be close to a common $t$-star. We further obtain a more general result in which a separate agreement requirement is imposed for each possible value. Abstract: For a finite graph, its energy is the sum of the absolute values of its adjacency eigenvalues. The unique energy-maximising divisor set is the checkerboard set consisting of all divisors p raised to the power i times q raised to the power j, where i ranges from zero to 2r, j ranges from zero to 2s plus 1, and i plus j is even, and we obtain an explicit closed formula for the corresponding maximum energy. In particular, for q at least 5, when r and s are both equal to 1, our theorem establishes the conjectured maximality of Roldan's checkerboard divisor set and recovers his closed-form energy formula. For a vertex $v$ and a multiset $\mathcal{H}$ of graphs, the \textit{suspension} $\mathcal{H} + v$ of $\mathcal{H}$ is the graph obtained by connecting the vertex $v$ to all vertices of $H$ for each $H \in \mathcal{H}$. \ar(n,\mathcal{H}_{k+1}) $
for $k\ge 1$, $r\ge 2$ and sufficiently large $n$. This result unifies and generalizes a result of Liu et al. (arXiv:2411.08475) concerning the friendship graph, and a result of Lu et al. (arXiv:2507.13165) on the intersecting cliques.

Abstract: Motivated by the concept of polarity introduced by G. Birkhoff for a binary relation on a set, we introduce a concept of orthogonality in a poset with $0$. We present constructions of orthogonally adjoint pairs in pseudocomplemented posets. If a given operator $f$ is an order-isomorphism of a pseudocomplemented poset satisfying some natural properties then the corresponding adjoint $g$ can be described explicitly. Abstract: Harwit and Sloane conjectured that every nonsingular entrywise-nonnegative matrix $A\in\mathbb R^{n\times n}$ satisfies $\|A^{-1}\|_F\ge 2n(n+1)^{-1}\|A\|_{\max}^{-1}$, with equality precisely for positive multiples of $S$-matrices. Starting from the structural identities in Frankel--Urschel Lemma 2.1, we derive an exact global defect budget and combine binary rounding with Gram projection. A refined ten-row obstruction handles every even $n\ge66$; a finite exact calculation handles $4\le n\le64$, $n\ne6$; and a separate multi-column energy argument treats $n=6$. The order-two case follows from a direct calculation. The new even-dimensional proof has been formalized in Lean 4, with Frankel--Urschel Lemma 2.1 as its sole external mathematical input. Abstract: We classify Schur positivity in two families of symmetric functions $f_n^T$ introduced by Sundaram. For even $n\ge18$, it fails exactly when $n\in T$ and $n/2\notin T$; then $s_{(1^n)}$ indexes the unique negative Schur coefficient, equal to $-1$. Finally, for $k=q^s$ with $q$ prime, $s\ge2$, $k\mid n$, and $n>k$, we derive major-index residue inequalities and classify all equality cases. Abstract: We introduce $L$-functions associated with characters of the Jacobian of a finite graph, as a graph-theoretic analogue of the $L$-functions arising from unramified coverings of algebraic curves. Abstract: We study classical pattern avoidance in canon permutations from two complementary points of view. First, for arbitrary alphabet size, we resolve three conjectures of Laudone by giving canonical structural decompositions.

A relabeling reduction and a six-class lattice-word theorem show that each fixed-underlying-permutation component has cardinality \[ 0,\qquad 1,\qquad C_k,\qquad B_k,\qquad Q_k,\qquad\text{or}\qquad T_k \] where $C_k$ denote the $k$-th Catalan number, $T_k=f^{(k,k,k)}$ is the unrestricted rectangular-tableau number, $B_k$ counts three-row lattice words in which every $1$ precedes every $3$, and $Q_k$ counts $321$-avoiding three-row lattice words. The sixty-four forbidden sets yield twelve enumerative formulas. Abstract: Anti-Latin squares were introduced in connection with non-linear secure network coding, and the extremal problem for large mutually orthogonal families is motivated by that setting. The upper bound is obtained by passing through balanced matrices, while the lower bound is given by a deterministic permutation argument. For all $d\ge 8$, and also for the exceptional order $d=6$, the upper bound is shown to be attainable by a general probabilistic construction. Abstract: Perfect state transfer on graphs has attracted extensive attention due to its application in quantum information and quantum computation. Explicit characterizations of connection sets admitting perfect state transfer in Cayley graphs are rare and, so far, are known only for a few abelian Cayley graphs. In this paper, we characterize the conjugation-closed connection sets of connected Cayley graphs over dihedral groups that admit perfect state transfer. By applying Ramanujan sums, Möbius inversion, and arguments based on the $p$-adic exponential valuation of rational numbers, we convert the eigenvalue constraints imposed by perfect state transfer into explicit structural conditions on the connection set. TL;DR: 本文研究了禁止偏序（forbidden poset）在最大反-Ramsey（maximal anti-Ramsey）问题中的类比，引入了参数 ar_m(n,m,P) 和 ar*_m(n,m,P)，即最小颜色数 k 使得存在大小为 m 的族 F⊆2^[n] 及染色 F→[k]，其中所有弱/强 P 的拷贝均为彩虹色。论文在两种 m 的量级下确定了这些参数的渐近行为：当 m=2^n 时对所有偏序 P 完全确定；当 m=Θ(binomial(n,floor(n/2))) 时，对每个连通偏序 P 和整数 k，中间 k 层布尔格的彩虹染色需要 Θ(n^{m_{P,k}}) 或 Θ(n^{m^*_{P,k}}) 种颜色。论文还针对一类无穷树偏序证明了所提出的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者推广了Burr、Erdős、Graham和Sós在图中引入的最大反-Ramsey问题到禁止偏序的设定中，旨在研究在保持所有禁止结构拷贝为彩虹色的前提下所需的最少颜色数，并揭示其与经典poset free族的极大尺寸La(n,P)之间的深层联系。

Method: 论文引入了 ar_m(n,m,P) 和 ar*_m(n,m,P) 两个参数，分别在弱拷贝和强拷贝的意义下定义。作者在两种重要量级下研究这些参数：(1) m=2^n（全空间）情形，对所有偏序 P 完全确定渐近值；(2) m=Θ(binomial(n,floor(n/2)))（中等规模）情形，考虑布尔格的中间 k 层，将所需颜色数归结为 Θ(n^{m_{P,k}}) 或 Θ(n^{m^*_{P,k}}) 的多项式阶。对于树偏序，作者证明了 m_{T,k}=m^*_{T,k}，并提出一个关于树偏序的猜想，然后对一类无穷树偏序证明了该猜想在 ar_m 方面的成立。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 当 m=2^n 时，对所有偏序 P，ar_m 和 ar*_m 的渐近值被完全确定；(2) 对于任意连通偏序 P 和整数 k，中间 k 层布尔格染色需要 Θ(n^{m_{P,k}})（弱拷贝）或 Θ(n^{m^*_{P,k}})（强拷贝）种颜色；(3) 对任意树偏序 T，m_{T,k}=m^*_{T,k}；(4) 提出关于树偏序的猜想（当 m≥(k-1+ε)binomial(n,floor(n/2)) 时，ar_m, ar*_m=Ω(n^{m_{T,k}})），并在一类无穷树偏序上证明了该猜想中 ar_m 的部分。

Conclusion: 本文建立了禁止偏序集最大反-Ramsey问题的基本框架，在 m=2^n 和 m=Θ(binomial(n,floor(n/2))) 两个关键量级下给出了完整或部分的渐近刻画，并通过树偏序的猜想揭示了问题的本质复杂性。相关猜想（特别是强拷贝情形）仍留作开放问题，为后续研究指明了方向。

Abstract: We study the forbidden poset analog of the maximal anti-Ramsey problem introduced for graphs by Burr, Erd\H os, Graham, and Sós. For integers $m\le 2^n$ and poset $P=(P,\preceq)$, we introduce $\mathrm{ar_m}(n,m,P)$ (and $\mathrm{ar^*_m}(n,m,P)$) to denote the minimum integer $k$ such that there exists a family $\mathcal{F}\subseteq 2^{[n]}$ with $|\mathcal{F}|=m$ and a coloring $\mathcal{F}\rightarrow [k]$ with all weak (strong) copies of $P$ being rainbow. As long as there exist $P$-free families of size $m$, these parameters equal 1. It is known that the largest size $La(n,P)$ ($La^*(n,P)$) of weak (strong) $P$-free families has order of magnitude $Θ(\binom{n}{\lfloor n/2\rfloor})$ unless $P$ is the antichain $A_k$ on $k$ elements. In this paper we study $\mathrm{ar_m}(n,m,P)$ and $\mathrm{ar^*_m}(n,m,P)$ in two regimes of $m$. We determine the asymptotics of these parameters for all posets $P$ when $m=2^n$. We also consider the case $m=Θ(\binom{n}{\lfloor n/2\rfloor})$. It is shown that for any connected poset $P$ and integer $k$, there exist integers $m_{P,k}$ and $m^*_{P,k}$ such that to color the middle $k$ layers of the Boolean lattice with all weak or strong copies of $P$ being rainbow, one needs $Θ(n^{m_{P,k}})$ or $Θ(n^{m^*_{P,k}})$ colors. For tree posets $T$, one has $m_{T,k}=m^*_{T,k}$. We conjecture that for any tree poset $T$, and positive real $\varepsilon$, $\mathrm{ar_m}(n,m,T),\mathrm{ar^*_m}(n,m,T)=Ω(n^{m_{T,k}})$ holds provided $m\ge (k-1+\varepsilon)\binom{n}{\lfloor n/2\rfloor}$. We prove our conjecture on $\mathrm{ar_m}(n,m,T)$ for an infinite class of tree posets.

</details>

Abstract: Jaramillo-Velez recently introduced edge codes, a new class of toric evaluation codes constructed from the edges of a (hyper)graph $\mathcal{H}$. In the case that $\mathcal{H}$ is a tree, Jaramillo-Velez computed both the minimum distance and the weight distribution of the associated code. In this paper, we study edge codes associated to unicyclic graphs. Our most striking result is that computing the parameters of these codes is subtle in the case that the induced cycle has an even length because these values will depend on certain conditions regarding the length of the cycle and the size of the base field.

</details>

Method: 下界通过将路径的大小-Ramsey数结果嵌入偶数环获得；上界则构造性地设计了一种图构造方案（图中嵌入偶数环），通过改进的图构造和概率/组合方法将上界从指数级常数O(k³⁴)优化到O(k²log k)，该常数与k的平方乘对数阶一致。

Conclusion: 偶数环的k色大小-Ramsey数的阶已完全确定，为Θ(k²log k)·n。该结果同时表明常数因子与k的对数相关，而非此前上界显示的k的高次幂，代表了该问题的一个决定性进展。

Abstract: We determine the $k$-colour size--Ramsey number of even cycles up to absolute constant factors. For every $k\ge2$ and every even $n\ge100\log k$, \[ \widehat R_k(C_n)=Θ(k^2\log k)n. \] The lower bound follows from the corresponding result of Beke, Li and Sahasrabudhe for paths, while our upper bound improves the previous best estimate $O(k^{34}n)$ of Javadi, Kohayakawa and Miralaei.

</details>


### [39] [On high-girth layered graphs of positive Turán density in a hypercube](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30544)
*Maria Axenovich,Marko Pejić*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究超立方体中的Turán密度问题。作者证明了对于任意给定的围长g≥3，都存在一个分层图（layered graph），其围长至少为g，且在超立方体中的Turán密度至少为1/2。这一结果将之前已知的具有正Turán密度的分层图从仅包含6或10长环的图，推广到了一类具有任意大围长的图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对于一般图H，确定其在超立方体中的Turán密度π_□(H)是一个开放问题。Conlon找到了一类Turán密度为零的图，但具有正Turán密度的情况鲜有研究。此前仅知道包含长度为6或10的环的分层图具有正Turán密度，自然的问题是是否存在其他类型的分层图也具有正Turán密度，特别是具有大围长的分层图。

Method: 作者构造了一类分层图，其围长可以任意大（至少为g）。利用超立方体的分层结构——即通过交替选取边层（edge-layer）来获得下界——证明了这类分层图在超立方体中的Turán密度至少为1/2。这利用了非可嵌入单一边层的图在取交替边层时不会出现同构子图的构造策略。

Result: 对于任意给定的围长g≥3，存在一个分层图其围长至少为g，并且该图在超立方体中的Turán密度至少为1/2。这突破了此前仅限包含6或10长环图形的限制，证明了一类具有任意大围长的分层图也具有正Turán密度。

Conclusion: 该结果表明，具有正Turán密度的分层图远不止之前已知的少数情况，存在一个包含任意大围长的分层图族，其Turán密度至少为1/2。这为理解超立方体中Turán密度的完整图景提供了新的见解，也表明分层图中的正Turán密度现象比预想的更为普遍。

Abstract: For a graph $H$, let $\operatorname{ex}(Q_n, H)$ be the largest number of edges in a subgraph of the hypercube $Q_n$ of dimension $n$ that contains no subgraph isomorphic to $H$. The Turán density of $H$ in a hypercube, denoted $π_\square(H)$, is defined as $\lim_{n\rightarrow \infty} \operatorname{ex}(Q_n, H)/|E(Q_n)|$. Determining $π_\square(H)$ remains a widely open question for general $H$. Conlon found a large class of graphs with zero Turán density in a hypercube. In this note, we address the case when $π_{\square} (H)>0$. If a graph $H$ is not embeddable in an edge-layer of a hypercube, then $π_{\square} (H)\geq 1/2$, as can be seen by taking every other edge layer of $Q_n$. Among the layered graphs, the only ones known to have positive Turán density in a hypercube are graphs containing cycles of length $6$ or $10$. We show that, for every $g \geq 3$, there is a layered graph of girth at least $g$ whose Turán density in a hypercube is at least $1/2$.

</details>


### [40] [Second Order Zarankiewicz Number](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30555)
*Johan Löfberg,Liqun Qi*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文引入二阶Zarankiewicz数z_2(m,n)和递归线参数z_RL(m,n)，建立了从BSR(m,n)到z(m,n)的无条件层级不等式链，并通过多种显式构造证明二阶框架严格强于弱框架（如5×4情形13>12、10×3情形20>19、7×7情形31>28），最后在完全图关联族上证明两者之间存在Ω(N³)的立方渐近分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究建立了一阶框架（weak framework）下Zarankiewicz数的若干层参数，但二阶矩（SOS rank）框架与弱框架之间的精确关系尚不清楚。作者希望引入二阶版本的Zarankiewicz数，并考察其与弱框架各层参数之间的间隙是否只存在于有限维，还是在渐近意义下也持续存在。

Method: 作者定义二阶Zarankiewicz数z_2(m,n)（不可约双重简单双二次型最大SOS秩）以及递归线参数z_RL(m,n)，后者通过保留条件(S)、排除(W1)、(W2)、(W2')并强化递归矩形证书(RW3⁺)获得。利用显式不可约形式的构造（5×4、10×3、7×7以及完全图关联族）结合精确搜索与贪心搜索来证明各层参数的严格分离，并通过添加互补对检查来形式化(RW3⁺)机制。

Result: 建立了BSR(m,n)≥z_2(m,n)≥z_RL(m,n)≥z_wL(m,n)≥z(m,n)的无条件层级。具体结果：(1) 5×4不可约形式SOS秩13>12=z_wL(5,4)；(2) z_RL(10,3)≥20>19=z_wL(10,3)，反驳了三列等式猜想；(3) 7×7上z_RL(7,7)≥31>28=z_wL(7,7)；(4) 完全图关联族上z_2(组合数(N,2),N)-z_wL≥(1/16-o(1))N³，证明了渐近立方分离。

Conclusion: 二阶Zarankiewicz框架与弱框架之间的间隙不仅是有限维现象，而是在完全图关联族上具有Ω(N³)的渐近分离，说明二阶框架捕获了弱框架遗漏的实质信息。文中提出的(RW3⁺)机制在被证明严格弱于字面(W3)的同时，能够在公共弱环境类上给出更强的下界。

Abstract: We introduce the \emph{second order Zarankiewicz number} $z_2(m,n)$, defined as the maximum SOS rank of an $m \times n$ irreducible double simple biquadratic form. Abstract: By a simple homogenization process, one may turn a $q$-analog into a symmetric $(q,t)$-analog. A more intricate non-homogeneous extension is also developed. This also leads naturally to new constructions and conjectures. We further explore other avenues of investigations, included graded and equivariant $γ$-positivity and $γ$-anti-positivity (also known as alternatingly $γ$-positive).

  τ(G(n,p))=\frac{n-a}{a}+o(1) \] with high probability. Moreover, we show that there is a sequence $n_m\to\infty$ such that with high probability, \[
  τ(G(n_{m},p))
  \le\frac{n_{m}-a}a-\frac{1}{a}. \]

</details>

Abstract: We study the partition regular properties of topological groups, proving several extensions of Hindman theorem where monochromatic sets of finite sums are required to satisfy additional topological constraints. Conclusion: 本文将圆法成功推广到双权重变量的加权射影空间超曲面计数问题，给出了渐近公式成立时变量维数需满足的显式条件，并通过局部密度正性建立了积分Hasse原理的充分条件。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce several novel approaches utilizing the circle method to obtain the asymptotic formula for the number of integral points of bounded height lying on a hypersurface in a weighted projective space.
  Let $F(\mathbf{x} ; \mathbf{y})$ be a given weighted form of degree $d$ in variables $\mathbf{x} \in \mathbb{R}^{s_1}$ and $\mathbf{y} \in \mathbb{R}^{s_2}$, where variables $\mathbf{x}$ and $\mathbf{y}$ have weights $w_1$ and $w_2$ with $w_1<w_2,\left(w_1, w_2\right)=1$, and $d>w_1 w_2$. Write $$ R_F(P):=\#\left\{(\mathbf{x} ; \mathbf{y}) \in \mathbb{Z}^{s_1+s_2}: F(\mathbf{x} ; \mathbf{y})=0,|\mathbf{x}| \leq P^{w_1 / d},|\mathbf{y}| \leq P^{w_2 / d}\right\} . $$ In particular, we show that whenever $$ s_1+s_2-σ_F>\left(1+\frac{w_2}{w_1}\right) \frac{d}{w_1} 2^{d / w_1}, $$ where $σ_F$ is the dimension of the affine singular locus of $F$, the quantity $R_F(P)$ has the expected asymptotic formula, that is $$ R_F(P)=c P^{s_1 w_1 / d+s_2 w_2 / d-1}+o\left(P^{s_1 w_1 / d+s_2 w_2 / d-1}\right), $$ where $c$ is the product of local densities. Furthermore, the constant $c$ is positive whenever $F(\mathbf{x} ; \mathbf{y})=0$ has a nonsingular solution over $\mathbb{R}$ and $\mathbb{Q}_p$ for every prime $p$. As a corollary, we verify the integral Hasse principle for the quasi-smooth hypersurface defined by $F(\mathbf{x} ; \mathbf{y})=0$ in a weighted projective space of sufficiently large dimensions.

</details>

Abstract: Let $E/K$ be an elliptic curve over a number field $K$, and let $[C]\in H^1(K,E(\overline{K}))$ be a homogeneous space under $E$. Suppose that $C$ has period $n$ and index $d$. Abstract: We study supersingular isogeny graphs of rank-two Drinfeld modules over $A=\mathbb{F}_q[T]$. For distinct finite primes $\mathfrak p$ and $\mathfrak q$ of $A$, we prove that the graph in characteristic $\mathfrak p$, with edges given by cyclic $\mathfrak q$-isogenies, is connected. The proof combines Gekeler's ideal-class correspondence with strong approximation to realize the graph as a quotient of the Bruhat--Tits tree of the homothety classes of $A_\mathfrak q$-lattices in $(\mathbb{F}_q(T))_\mathfrak q^2$. We also introduce the completeness number $E(\mathfrak p)$, the least integer such that the graph is complete for every prime $\mathfrak q\neq\mathfrak p$ with $\operatorname{deg}\mathfrak q\geq E(\mathfrak p)$, and derive explicit upper bounds using the Ramanujan--Petersson bound for the eigenvalues of operators associated to Brandt matrices over function fields. In particular, if $d=\operatorname{deg}\mathfrak p$, then $E(\mathfrak p)\leq 2d+2$, with sharper bounds depending on $q$ and the parity of $d$. This confirms a conjecture of Micheli and Papikian that the graph becomes complete once $\operatorname{deg}\mathfrak q$ is sufficiently large relative to $\operatorname{deg}\mathfrak p$. We further obtain parity-dependent lower bounds and prove that $E(\mathfrak p)\geq d+\log_q d-K$ for an absolute constant $K>0$ and sufficiently large odd $d$, thereby refuting the previously suggested bound $E(\mathfrak p)\leq d+1$. Finally, we prove a criterion yielding an algorithm to compute $E(\mathfrak p)$.

Abstract: In this second chapter of the $\textit{Arithmetic of critical values}$ series (ACV), we study certain double covers $E_f\to\mathbb{P}^1$ whose branch locus coincides with that of a quartic polynomial $f$. Abstract: Suppose a set of integers $A\subseteq\{1,\dots,N\}$ has no solutions to $a-a'=n\lfloor\sqrt[3]2n\rfloor,$ for distinct $a,a'\in A,$ and $n\in \mathbb{N}.$ We show that $|A|\ll N^{1-c}$ for some absolute constant $c>0.$ To do this, we prove quantitative bounds on the van der Corput property for certain sets of bracket quadratics. This comes as a consequence of establishing exponential sum estimates for these sets, utilising a theorem of Green and Tao on the quantitative equidistribution of polynomial orbits on nilmanifolds, closely following the approach of Neale who went on to prove a Waring-type result. Abstract: Let $q = 2^m$, $Q = 2^k$, and $1 \leq k \leq m-1$. Write $\overline{x} = x^q$. We study complete permutation quadrinomials over $\mathbb{F}_{q^2}$ of the form \[ f(x) = c_0 x^{Q+1} + c_1 x^Q \overline{x} + c_2 x \overline{x}^Q + c_3 \overline{x}^{Q+1},\qquad c_i \in \mathbb{F}_{q^2}. \]

(Finite Fields Appl. 110: 102734, 2026) later proved that this condition is also necessary, and that under this condition \(f\) and \(f+x\) are linearly equivalent to \(x^2\overline{x}\) and \(x^2\overline{x}+γx\), respectively, for some \(γ\in \mathbb{F}_{q^2}^*\) with $\ord(γ^{q-1})=3$. In this paper, we prove that no such CPP exists for \(k>1\), and that the known condition of Chan et al. is complete for \(k=1\). This completes the characterization for all \(Q=2^k\) with $1 \leq k \leq m-1$.

</details>

Abstract: For every quadratic number field $K$, we prove a uniform $3$-unknown Diophantine definition of integer tuples in $\mathcal{O}_K$, allowing finitely many polynomial nonvanishing conditions. Consequently, there exists an absolute degree bound $D_0\geq1$ such that for every quadratic number field $K$, there is no algorithm that, given \[ P(Y_1,\ldots,Y_{13})\in\mathbb{Z}[Y_1,\ldots,Y_{13}],\quad \mathrm{deg}\ P\leq D_0, \] decides whether $P=0$ has a solution in $\mathcal{O}_K^{13}$.
  The arithmetic input is a fourth-order Pell--Lucas congruence. It is a specialization of the norm-one Lucas multiplication formula, which yields exact valuations for the deviation of a Lucas quotient from its linear term, together with deviation criteria for Lucas--Wieferich and Wall--Sun--Sun primes. We also establish local surjectivity and $\ell$-adic density for second-order correction terms for norm-one Lucas sequences.

### [80] [Unit Indices of Prime-Index Shanks Orders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30637)
*Junyu Lu*

Main category: math.NT

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解非极大序（non-maximal order）与其对应的极大序之间的单位群关系是代数数论中的基本问题。该论文针对一类具体的三次多项式族 $g_t$ 生成的序，系统刻画其单位指标、Picard核、理想类幺半群及矩阵共轭分类，旨在为这类三次域提供完整的算术结构描述。

Result: 论文完整确定了单位指标：$[\mathcal{O}_t^\times:R_t^\times]=q$ 当且仅当 $(t,q)=(3,3)$ 或 $(5,7)$，否则单位指标为 $1$。此外还确定了 Picard 核、理想类幺半群以及相关的积分矩阵共轭分类的每个纤维。

Conclusion: 该工作对一类特殊三次序的单位指标给出了完整且精确的刻画，揭示了几何分支与算术分支（wild/tame）在该问题中的不同行为，并推广了相关的矩阵共轭分类结果。

Abstract: For an integer $t\geq-1$, let $θ_t$ be the largest real root of $g_t(X)=X^3-tX^2-(t+3)X-1$, and let $R_t=\mathbb{Z}[θ_t]\subseteq\mathcal{O}_t=\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{Q}(θ_t)}$. We prove that if the additive order index $q=[\mathcal{O}_t:R_t]$ is prime, then $[\mathcal{O}_t^\times:R_t^\times]=q$ exactly for $(t,q)=(3,3)$ and $(5,7)$, and the unit index is $1$ otherwise. The proof shows that $q$ has a unique prime $\mathfrak P$ above it and that $R_t=\mathbb{Z}+\mathfrak P^2$, thereby restricting the unit index to $1$ or $q$. We also determine the Picard kernel, the ideal class monoid, and every fiber of the associated integral matrix-conjugacy classification.

</details>

Abstract: We clarify the APN lifting conjecture over Galois rings of Rønjom and Sandrib (CCDS, 2026). A function on $\F_q$ has many polynomial representatives, whose formal derivatives may differ, so the conjecture must use the unique reduced representative of degree less than $q$; without this normalization, it is false. The standard permutation-polynomial criterion over Galois rings then gives an exact reduction: the reduced representative $f$ of an APN permutation lifts to a permutation of $\GR(2^k,m)$, $k>1$, if and only if $f'(x)\ne0$ for every $x\in\F_{2^m}$. Thus the corrected lifting conjecture is equivalent to a finite-field critical-point conjecture.

For every odd degree $d\ge5$ outside the Gold exponents $2^r+1$ and Kasami--Welch exponents $2^{2r}-2^r+1$, results of Hernando--McGuire and Aubry--McGuire--Rodier provide an absolutely irreducible factor in the hyperplane section at infinity. This yields an absolutely irreducible component of the surface, defined over the ground field and not contained in the diagonal arrangement. The explicit Cafure--Matera estimate then gives a computable number $\APNmzero{d}$ such that no polynomial of degree $d$ over $\F_{2^m}$ is APN when $m\ge\APNmzero{d}$. The qualitative eventual non-APN result is due to Aubry--McGuire--Rodier; our contribution is the explicit threshold.
  A direct identity for difference tables also gives an even-degree consequence: if $g$ has such an odd degree, then $ax+g(x^2)+c$, with $a\ne0$, has the same differential uniformity as $g$ and is therefore not APN in the same explicit range. Finally, we prove directly that every cubic permutation polynomial has a rational critical point and hence satisfies the corrected lifting conjecture.

</details>

  logarithm of the lattice-point count divided by the volume satisfies a central
  limit theorem. At a fixed offset from the critical scale this yields a genuine
  lognormal limit. Above the critical scale, the count-to-volume ratio converges
  to $1$ in measure; its normalized error has a standard normal limit, and
  its variance satisfies an asymptotic formula.

  <summary>Details</summary>

More precisely, if $T\le |t|\le 2T$, $\exp(\sqrt{\log T})\le x\le \sqrt T$, and $|S(x,t)|=x/N$ with $N\le (\log x)^{1/100}$, then for every $cN^6\le L\le (\log x)/2$ a disk centered at $1+\iiφ$, where $|φ-t|\ll N$, contains at least $L/360$ zeros of $ζ(s)$. As a consequence, a quantitative restriction on zeros in a short family of arbitrarily thin fixed windows to the left of the line $\Ree s=1$ yields $S(x,t)\ll x/(\log x)^{1/100}$ in polynomial ranges of $x$. In the zeta setting the spectral height is shifted by $t$, and an additional residue from the pole of $ζ(s)$ appears in the Gaussian transform; in the range considered here that residue is exponentially small.

</details>

</details>

Kummer asymptotics then yield explicit asymptotic lines, including their gamma-digamma offsets and the angle between their axes.

</details>

(i.e., are the holes within a loop are singular?). These questions are answered by (1) giving a defining system of equations for the shape of a molecule using concepts from category theory, and (2) giving a equation that determines whether holes within a loop are singular using concepts from cohomology theory, respectively. Abstract: We construct new ambient and Poincaré metrics for the Fefferman conformal manifold over $3$-dimensional CR manifolds, starting from the associated self-dual Einstein ACH metric. Since these metrics are determined to infinite order without ambiguity, they enable us to construct CR invariant differential operators and local CR invariants involving arbitrarily high order derivatives of the Tanaka-Webster curvature and torsion. As an application, we obtain the ambient metric construction of the CR GJMS (Gover-Graham) operators of all orders in dimension $3$.

Result: 在满足一定条件时，证明了Yamabe梯度孤立子要么分解为ℝ和空间形式中全脐子流形的黎曼乘积，要么满足sup_Σ|Φ^Σ_ŌM|的尖锐下界；并且该下界可取到当且仅当孤立子等距于ℝ与空间形式中平行子流形的黎曼乘积。

Conclusion: 本文为空间形式中作为完备浸入超曲面的Yamabe梯度孤立子提供了精确的结构刻画，所得结果包含了分解定理与等号刚性刻画，深化了对Yamabe孤立子几何分类的理解。

Abstract: This paper studies nontrivial Yamabe gradient solitons occurring as complete immersed hypersurfaces with constant scalar curvature in space forms $\left(\overline{M}^n(C), \overline{g}\right)$. For solitons with non-vanishing gradients, we establish a structural characterization of the soliton by analyzing a regular level set $Σ$ of the soliton function. In particular, imposing a suitable condition on the traceless part of the second fundamental form, $Φ^Σ_{\overline{M}}$ of $Σ$ as a submanifold of the space form, we prove that the soliton either decomposes as a Riemannian product of $\mathbb{R}$ and a totally umbilical submanifold of the space form, or satisfies a sharp lower bound on $\sup\limits_Σ\left|Φ^Σ_{\overline{M}}\right|$. Furthermore, we show that the lower bound is attained if, and only if, the soliton is isometric to a Riemannian product of $\mathbb{R}$ and a parallel submanifold of the space form.

### [95] [The Faraday Form of Conformal Products: Weyl Curvature and Four-Dimensional Einstein Rigidity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29318)
*Jae Won Lee*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究具有共形积结构的黎曼流形$(M^n,g)$，其适配的Weyl联络$D$保秩$p,q\ge2$的正交分布。作者将Faraday形式$d\theta$直接用$g$的Weyl曲率表达为$ (d\theta)^\sharp = \frac{n-2}{4(p-1)(q-1)}[\mathcal K_W,S] $，无需任何Ricci曲率假设。同时给出$\nabla\theta$对称部的伴随公式，并由此推导Lee形式两分量在Ricci张量块对角情形下的显式恒等式。在四维情形，秩$(2,2)$分裂确定一个ambi-Hermitian对；经典ambi-Hermitian曲率恒等式在Ricci张量对两复结构均不变时蕴含局部闭性，这也可从混合Weyl迹推出。最后，若$(M^4,g)$紧致且Einstein，则无需特别假设即得$D=\nabla^g$，从而万有覆盖为两个具有相同常Gauss曲率的单连通曲面之积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该工作的动机在于深入理解共形积结构的几何性质，特别是想要在不依赖Ricci曲率假设的情况下，直接用Weyl曲率来刻画与共形积结构相关的Lee形式（保联络的扭转形式）的闭性。作者利用Weyl曲率的混合迹来揭示积结构分裂与曲率之间的内在联系，并在四维ambi-Hermitian几何以及紧致Einstein四流形的分类问题中获得具体应用。

Method: 设$(M^n,g)$带共形积结构，$\mathcal K_W(X)=\sum_i W_{X,e_i}(Se_i)$为Weyl曲率的混合迹（$S$为分裂决定的正交对合）。作者通过直接曲率演算将Faraday形式$d\theta$表达为$[\mathcal K_W,S]$的手性组合；对$\Theta=\nabla\theta$的对称部分，利用同样的曲率计算推导出含$[S,\Ric^\sharp]$的伴随公式。在Ricci张量关于分裂为块对角的条件下，从这些恒等式提取Lee形式的两个分量的显式公式。四维情形中，利用秩$(2,2)$分裂对应的ambi-Hermitian结构及其经典曲率恒等式进行交叉验证；最后对紧致Einstein四流形利用曲率恒等式推出$D=\nabla^g$。

Result: 主要结果是：(1) Faraday形式$d\theta$的Weyl曲率纯迹公式$(d\theta)^\sharp=\frac{n-2}{4(p-1)(q-1)}[\mathcal K_W,S]$，不依赖Ricci假设；(2) $\nabla\theta$对称部的伴随恒等式$S\Theta^s-\Theta^sS+\theta^\sharp\wedge S\theta^\sharp=\frac{p-q}{n-2}(d\theta)^\sharp+\frac1{n-2}[S,\Ric^\sharp]$；(3) Ricci块对角时Lee形式两分量的显式恒等式；(4) 四维ambi-Hermitian情形下Ricci对两复结构不变蕴含局部闭性的两种推导；(5) 紧致Einstein四流形上$D=\nabla^g$，因此万有覆盖为两相同常Gauss曲率单连通曲面的乘积。

Conclusion: 本文建立了共形积结构下Weyl曲率混合迹与Lee形式（Faraday形式）之间的本质联系，证明了无需Ricci假设即可得到的精确曲率恒等式。这些公式在四维ambi-Hermitian几何中与经典结果一致，并为紧致Einstein四流形的退化情形提供了统一处理，最终得到万有覆盖分裂的刚性结论。

Abstract: Let $(M^n,g)$ carry a conformal product structure whose adapted Weyl connection $D$ preserves orthogonal distributions of ranks $p,q\ge2$. We express the Faraday form $dθ$ directly in terms of the Weyl curvature of $g$. If $S$ is the orthogonal involution determined by the splitting and \[ \mathcal K_W(X)=\sum_i W_{X,e_i}(Se_i) \] for any local orthonormal frame, then \[ (dθ)^\sharp =
\frac{n-2}{4(p-1)(q-1)}[\mathcal K_W,S]. \] No Ricci-curvature assumption is required. The same curvature calculation gives a companion formula for the symmetric part of $\nablaθ$, \[ SΘ^s-Θ^sS+θ^\sharp\wedge Sθ^\sharp =
\frac{p-q}{n-2}(dθ)^\sharp +\frac1{n-2}[S,\Ric^\sharp], \qquad Θ=\nablaθ. \] When the Ricci tensor is block diagonal with respect to the product splitting, these formulas lead to explicit identities for the two components of the Lee form. In dimension four, the rank-$(2,2)$ splitting determines an ambi-Hermitian pair. The classical ambi-Hermitian curvature identities imply local closedness when the Ricci tensor is invariant under both complex structures; we also recover this conclusion from the mixed Weyl trace above. Finally, if $(M^4,g)$ is compact and Einstein, no specialness assumption is needed: $D=\nabla^g$. Hence the universal cover is the product of two simply connected surfaces with the same constant Gaussian curvature.

</details>

*Chul Woo Lee,Jae Won Lee*

TL;DR: 本文在Sasakian流形中发展了部分全实（PTR）子流形的框架，通过分离Reeb向量场方向，定义了全实分布与含混分布，并证明反不变子流形、接触CR子流形、半斜子流形及逐点半斜子流形均为该框架的特例。文中建立了切向与法向分解，研究了极大性、可积性及Reeb场带来的额外限制，并分析了典范态射P和F、相关分布的几何以及Sasakian空间形式中的PTR子流形，附有显式模型以说明主要结构与Kähler情形的差异。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分全实（PTR）子流形最初在Kähler几何中被引入，通过区分全实分布并使其正交补不受限制来推广已有的子流形类别。本文动机旨在将这一框架推广到Sasakian流形（特别是切于Reeb向量场的子流形），从而统一并涵盖反不变、接触CR、半斜和逐点半斜等众多已有子流形类别，并揭示Sasakian情形下Reeb场带来的独特几何限制。

Method: 首先在Sasakian流形中分离Reeb方向，定义全实分布与含混分布；随后建立切向与法向分解，研究分布的极大性与可积性条件；进一步分析Reeb向量场对子流形结构产生的额外约束；最后研究典范态射P和F的代数性质及相关分布的几何，并在Sasakian空间形式中探讨PTR子流形的分类，提供显式模型验证理论。

Result: 成功建立了Sasakian流形上的PTR子流形框架，证明反不变子流形、接触CR子流形、半斜子流形和逐点半斜子流形均可视为该统一框架的特例；获得了切向与法向分解的显式表达，刻画了极大性和可积性条件，并明确了Reeb场产生的额外限制；在Sasakian空间形式中给出了PTR子流形的几何刻画，并通过显式模型展示了与Kähler情形的本质差异。

Conclusion: 本文构建的Sasakian PTR子流形理论提供了一个统一的几何框架，不仅将多种已知子流形类别纳入同一体系，还揭示了Reeb向量场在Sasakian情形下对子流形结构特有的约束作用。该框架为后续深入研究Sasakian几何中的子流形分类与空间形式理论奠定了基础。

Abstract: Partially totally real (PTR) submanifolds were introduced in Kähler geometry by distinguishing a totally real distribution and leaving its orthogonal complement unrestricted. In this paper we develop the corresponding framework for submanifolds of Sasakian manifolds tangent to the Reeb vector field. After separating the Reeb direction, we define the totally real and ambiguous distributions and show that anti-invariant, contact CR, hemi-slant and pointwise hemi-slant submanifolds occur as special cases of the Sasakian PTR framework. We establish the basic tangential and normal decompositions, study maximality and integrability, and derive the additional restrictions produced by the Reeb field. We then investigate the canonical morphisms \(P\) and \(F\), the geometry of the associated distributions, and PTR-submanifolds in Sasakian space forms. Explicit models are included to illustrate the principal structures and the differences from the Kähler case.

</details>


### [97] [Positive mass and rigidity for asymptotically flat tangent bundles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29427)
*Sajjad Lakzian*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究渐近平坦流形切丛上的正质量定理，证明了一类沿水平分布构造的、具有慢渐近衰减的渐近平坦度量，其广义ADM质量良定义且由底流形的几何数据决定，并建立了正质量定理和刚性定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准ADM质量在2n维切丛上需要更快的衰减条件，而自然构造的切丛度量衰减速率低于此阈值，使得经典正质量定理无法直接适用，需要在广义意义下定义质量并证明其几何确定性。

Method: 沿切丛的水平分布在欧氏度量与底度量g之间进行插值，构造一类渐近平坦度量，并推广ADM质量的定义以适应慢衰减情形。

Result: 证明了对这类自然构造的切丛度量，渐近质量在广义意义下良定义，且由底流形M上的几何数据唯一确定；同时证明了正质量定理和正质量刚性定理。

Conclusion: 尽管切丛诱导度量的衰减低于标准ADM质量的阈值，但其渐近质量仍具有明确的几何意义，并且正质量定理在广义框架下依然成立，推广了经典ADM正质量定理到切丛上的慢衰减度量情形。

Abstract: Let $(M,g)$ be an asymptotically flat manifold such that its tangent bundle $TM$ is diffeomorphically asymptotically Euclidean. We prove a positive mass theorem and a positive mass rigidity theorem for a natural class of asymptotically flat metrics $\widetilde g$ on $TM$ exhibiting slow asymptotic decay. These metrics are constructed by interpolating, along the horizontal distribution of $TM$, between the Euclidean metric and the base metric $g$ near infinity. The main difficulty is that the induced metrics on $TM$ decay below the standard threshold for the ADM mass in dimension $2n$; nevertheless, we show that their asymptotic mass is well-defined in a generalized sense and is determined by geometric data on the underlying manifold $M$.

</details>


### [98] [Limiting Behavior of a Class of Hermitian Yang--Mills Metrics, II: Exponential Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29554)
*Jixiang Fu,Dekai Zhang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文是[9]的续篇，证明在双谱覆盖构造的稳定秩二全纯向量丛上，近似Hermitian Yang–Mills度量$H_{0,\varepsilon}$在大Kähler极限下给出精确Hermitian Yang–Mills度量的全阶指数精确渐近描述，其均曲率在每个$C^k$-范数下指数衰减，且归一化后的度量差满足$\|H_\varepsilon-\text{Id}\|_{C^k}\leq C_k e^{-c_k/\varepsilon}$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在[9]中，第一作者构建了双谱覆盖上稳定秩二全纯向量丛的一族近似Hermitian Yang–Mills度量$H_{0,\varepsilon}$，本文需要验证这些近似度量确实能够高精度地逼近精确解，特别是在大Kähler极限下给出全阶指数级别的误差估计。

Method: 核心方法是对耦合非线性Hermitian Yang–Mills系统进行全局$C^0$-比较分析。作者通过证明近似度量的均曲率在每个$C^k$-范数下指数衰减，进而建立$H_\varepsilon=H_{0,\varepsilon}^{-1}H_{1,\varepsilon}$与单位矩阵之间在$C^k$-范数下的指数级估计。

Result: 对于每个非负整数$k$，存在正常数$C_k$和$c_k$，使得归一化后有$\|H_\varepsilon-\text{Id}\|_{C^k}\leq C_k e^{-c_k/\varepsilon}$，即近似度量与精确度量之差在所有$C^k$-范数下均以指数速率收敛到零。

Conclusion: 本文克服了全局$C^0$-比较的主要分析困难，证明近似Hermitian Yang–Mills度量提供了精确解的全阶指数精确渐近描述。由于方程只控制曲率缩并（从而仅控制某些二阶导数组合），而需要的是全矩阵值度量的全局控制，这一$C^0$-估计的建立是技术上最具挑战性的部分。

Abstract: This paper is a sequel to [9], where the first-named author constructed a family of approximate Hermitian Yang--Mills metrics $H_{0,ε}$ on stable rank-two holomorphic vector bundles arising from double spectral covers over the product of two one-dimensional complex tori.
  We prove that these approximate metrics give an all-order, exponentially accurate asymptotic description of the exact Hermitian Yang--Mills metrics in the large Kähler limit. More precisely, the mean curvature of $H_{0,ε}$ decays exponentially in every $C^k$-norm. Moreover, if $H_{1,ε}$ denotes the exact Hermitian Yang--Mills metric and \[ H_ε=H_{0,ε}^{-1}H_{1,ε}, \] then, after normalization, for every nonnegative integer $k$, there exist positive constants $C_k$ and $c_k$ such that \[ \|H_ε- Id\|_{C^k}\leq C_k e^{-\frac{c_{k}}ε}. \] The main analytic difficulty lies in the global $C^0$-comparison. Obtaining $C^0$-estimates for the coupled nonlinear Hermitian Yang--Mills system is intrinsically difficult; moreover, the equation controls only the contraction of the curvature, and hence only certain combinations of second derivatives, whereas one needs global control of the full matrix-valued metric.

</details>


### [99] [Remarks on the Complex Structures on $\mathbb P^3$ and $S^2\times S^4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29651)
*Ruiming Liang,Chenhan Liu,Yang Zhang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文在假设某紧致复三维流形（射影直线上的环面纤维化）成立的前提下，构造了$\mathbb{P}^3$上的一个奇异复结构，该结构与Huckleberry、Kebekus和Peternell的逐点爆炸结构不同；随后通过对Atiyah翻转，在标准光滑流形$S^2\times S^4$上构造了一个复结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索紧致复流形上复结构的多样性，特别是为$\mathbb{P}^3$和$S^2\times S^4$构造新的（奇异）复结构，超越已知的构造方法。

Method: 基于一个假设成立的紧致复三维流形（环面沿射影直线$\mathbb{P}^1$纤维化），通过对其进行适当的构造得到$\mathbb{P}^3$上的一个奇异复结构，再施加Atiyah翻转变换（flop）以获得$S^2\times S^4$上的复结构。

Result: 成功构造了$\mathbb{P}^3$上不同于已知逐点爆炸结构的奇异复结构，并进一步通过Atiyah翻转得到$S^2\times S^4$上的复结构。但结果依赖于所引用的紧致复三维流形的有效性假设。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，在特定假设下可以获得$\mathbb{P}^3$和$S^2\times S^4$上新的（奇异）复结构，为研究这些流形上的复结构空间提供了新的路径，但其最终有效性取决于前提的验证。

Abstract: Assuming the validity of the recently proposed \textit{``A compact complex threefold fibred by tori over the projective line, and the six-sphere''}, we construct an exotic complex structure on $\mathbb P^{3}$, distinct from the point-blowup structures of Huckleberry, Kebekus, and Peternell. Then we perform an Atiyah flop to produce a complex structure on the standard smooth manifold $S^{2}\times S^{4}$.

</details>


### [100] [Isodiametric-type upper bounds for Steklov eigenvalues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30807)
*Pingxin Gu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文建立了有界Lipschitz域上Steklov特征值的等直径型上界估计，给出了两个不等式，分别在线性依赖于k和二次依赖于k的意义上达到最优，并肯定回答了文献[5]中的开放问题4.37。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为有界Lipschitz域上的Steklov特征值建立起具有最优阶数的等直径型上界，特别是解决体积-直径比指数依赖及k的二次依赖这一开放问题。

Method: 通过分析建立了两个等直径型上界不等式：第一个给出D(Ω)σ_k(Ω)≤C(n)k，其关于k的线性依赖是尖锐的；第二个给出D(Ω)σ_k(Ω)≤C(n)k²(|Ω|^{1/n}/D(Ω))^{n/(n-1)}，在体积-直径比指数和给定指数下对k的二次依赖上均达到最优。

Result: 获得了两个关于Steklov特征值的等直径型上界估计，第一个在线性依赖上最优，第二个在体积-直径比的指数和k的二次依赖上均最优，并肯定回答了文献[5]中的开放问题4.37。

Conclusion: 所建立的估计在对应的阶数上都是尖锐的，为Steklov特征值的几何上界研究提供了完整的最优结果。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish isodiametric-type upper bounds for Steklov eigenvalues of bounded Lipschitz domains $Ω\subset \mathbb{R}^n$, $n\geq 2$, valid for every $k\in \mathbb{N}$: \[ D(Ω)σ_k(Ω)\leq C(n)k, \] \[ D(Ω)σ_k(Ω)\leq C(n)k^2\left(\frac{|Ω|^{\frac{1}{n}}}{D(Ω)}\right)^{\frac{n}{n-1}}, \] where $C(n)>0$ depends only on $n$. The first estimate is sharp in its linear dependence on $k$, while the second is sharp both in the exponent of the volume-diameter ratio and, once that exponent is fixed, in its quadratic dependence on $k$. In particular, the second estimate gives an affirmative answer to Open Question 4.37 in [5].

</details>


### [101] [Sasaki-Einstein metrics on connected sums of $S^3\times S^4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29695)
*Dasol Jeong,In-Kyun Kim,Jihun Park,Joonyeong Won*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文完全刻画了$S^3\times S^4$的$n$重连通和上何时存在Sasaki-Einstein结构，证明其存在性当且仅当$n$为偶数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sasaki-Einstein结构是微分几何中重要的几何结构，研究其在特定拓扑空间上的存在性是一个核心问题。作者希望完全确定$S^3\times S^4$的连通和这类流形上何时容许Sasaki-Einstein度量。

Method: 通过结合Sasaki-Einstein几何的约束条件与$S^3\times S^4$连通和的拓扑不变量，给出存在性的完全刻画。

Result: 证明了$S^3\times S^4$的$n$重连通和存在Sasaki-Einstein结构当且仅当$n$为偶数。

Conclusion: 这是对$S^3\times S^4$连通和上Sasaki-Einstein结构存在性的完全刻画，给出了一个清晰的充分必要条件。

Abstract: We completely characterize the $n$-fold connected sums of $S^3\times S^4$ that admit Sasaki-Einstein structures by proving that such a structure exists if and only if $n$ is even.

</details>


### [102] [A Cartan geometry from a correspondence space in Riemannian geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29713)
*Rodrigo Morón,Francisco J. Palomo*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究类型为(Euc(n),O(n-1))的Cartan几何。证明了这类几何与其底流形上的几乎切触度量结构、切丛正交分解及相容线性联络之间存在一一对应关系；并在n≥3时通过Spencer微分定义的挠率条件确立了"正规"Cartan联络，该正规化由对应空间构造所决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cartan几何是齐性空间的弯曲类比，对应空间构造可从一种类型的Cartan几何产生另一种类型。黎曼流形的单位切丛是类型为(Euc(n),O(n-1))的Cartan几何的典型例子。作者希望在一般层面（不限于对应空间构造得到的例子）系统研究这类几何的结构与分类。

Method: 首先建立类型为(Euc(n),O(n-1))的Cartan几何与其底流形上几何数据（几乎切触度量结构、切丛正交分解、相容线性联络）之间的双射对应。然后对于n≥3的情形，利用Spencer微分给出挠率的正规化条件，从而在众多Cartan联络中选出唯一的"正规"Cartan联络。

Result: 证明了这类Cartan几何与几乎切触度量结构等数据之间存在一一对应的双射关系；并且当n≥3时，通过Spencer微分表述的挠率归一化条件可唯一确定一个正规Cartan联络。特别地，从黎曼流形单位切丛的对应空间构造诱导出的Cartan联络恰好是正规的。

Conclusion: 该研究完整刻画了类型为(Euc(n),O(n-1))的Cartan几何的结构，并确立了由对应空间构造所自然产生的正规联络概念，为这类几何的进一步研究（如曲率分类与几何分析）奠定了基础。

Abstract: Cartan geometries are curved analogues of homogeneous spaces, and the correspondence space construction produces, from a Cartan geometry of one type, another of a different type over a larger base manifold. The unit tangent bundle of a Riemannian manifold is, in this language, a Cartan geometry of type $(\operatorname{Euc}(n),O(n-1))$ obtained in this way; we study Cartan geometries of this type in general, whether or not they arise as correspondence spaces. We show that every such geometry induces on its base manifold an almost contact metric structure, together with an orthogonal splitting of the tangent bundle and a compatible linear connection; conversely, these data determine the geometry, so that the correspondence is a bijection. When $n\ge3$, we single out a distinguished Cartan connection, which we call normal, by a condition on its torsion formulated in terms of the Spencer differential. This normalization is dictated by the correspondence space construction: the Cartan connection induced on the unit tangent bundle of a Riemannian manifold is normal.

</details>


### [103] [Boundary Cases of the $J$-Equation: Divisorial Rigidity and a Global $C^0$ Estimate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29736)
*Jixiang Fu,Ziyi Zhang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究了J-方程稳定性的两个边界情形：在J-半稳定条件下，证明了破坏稳定的素除子构成Boucksom意义上的例外族，且存在均匀数值间隙；在光滑边界锥条件下，得到了逼近扭曲J-方程解的一致全局C^0估计，进而构造了有界势的Bedford-Taylor解，该解在破坏稳定的素除子外部光滑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: J-方程的稳定性条件在边界情形下的行为尚未被充分理解，特别是J-半稳定时的破坏机制以及边界锥条件下的解的正则性问题，这些问题对于理解J-方程的求解和稳定性的完整图景至关重要。

Method: 利用Boucksom的例外族理论分析J-半稳定条件下破坏稳定的素除子的结构，并通过改良的nef估计去除Liu工作中的J-big假设；在光滑边界锥条件下，通过逼近扭曲J-方程获得一致全局C^0估计，并利用Bedford-Taylor理论构造有界势解。

Result: 在J-半稳定条件下，破坏稳定的素除子构成例外族且存在均匀数值间隙，相关改良nef估计可去掉Liu结果的J-big假设；在光滑边界锥条件下获得一致C^0估计，构造了在破坏稳定素除子外部光滑的Bounded-potential Bedford-Taylor解。

Conclusion: 该研究完善了J-方程稳定性理论的两个边界情形，揭示了破坏稳定素除子在J-方程解的存在性与正则性中的核心作用，并给出了更一般条件下的解构造方法。

Abstract: We study two boundary cases of the stability condition for the $J$-equation. First, under the $J$-semistable condition, we show that the destabilizing prime divisors form an exceptional family in the sense of Boucksom, with a uniform numerical gap away from them. The related modified nef estimate can remove the $J$-big assumption in Liu's work~\cite{Liu2026Boundary}. Second, under the smooth boundary cone condition, we obtain a uniform global $C^0$ estimate for solutions of the approximating twisted $J$-equations. As a consequence, we construct a bounded-potential Bedford--Taylor solution of the $J$-equation, which is smooth outside the destabilizing prime divisors.

</details>


### [104] [Sharp monotonicity for closed three-manifolds with positive scalar curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29853)
*Cosmin Manea*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了闭三维正标量曲率流形上自然格林函数的两个尖锐单调性公式，类比于Munteanu-Wang关于非负标量曲率的定理，并将两个结果解释为格林函数子水平集在共形爆破下"封闭质量"的单调性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在几何分析中，格林函数的单调性公式是研究流形几何与分析性质的重要工具。Munteanu-Wang定理给出了非负标量曲率情形下的单调性结果，本文旨在将这一结果推广到正标量曲率的闭三维流形情形，并建立更尖锐的估计。

Method: 作者在正标量曲率的闭三维流形上，对自然格林函数建立两个尖锐的单调性公式。通过构造流形的"共形爆破"（conformal blow-up），将格林函数子水平集的"封闭质量"定义为适当的几何量，并证明该质量随水平集参数单调变化。

Result: 得到了两个尖锐的（sharp）单调性公式，它们是Munteanu-Wang定理在正标量曲率情形的类比。两个公式均可解释为格林函数子水平集在共形爆破流形中定义的"封闭质量"的单调性。

Conclusion: 该工作将Munteanu-Wang的单调性结果从非负标量曲率推广到正标量曲率的闭三维流形，并通过共形爆破给出了几何直观解释，为研究正标量曲率流形的质量与格林函数性质提供了新工具。

Abstract: We prove two sharp monotonicity formulae for the natural Green's function of a closed three-manifold with positive scalar curvature, analogous to the Munteanu-Wang theorem for non-negative scalar curvature. We also show that both results may be interpreted as the monotonicity of a suitably defined "enclosed mass" of the sublevel sets of Green's function in the corresponding "conformal" blow-up of the manifold.

</details>


### [105] [Torsion algebras of Hermitian manifolds and rigidity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29916)
*Wangyang Lin,Yibo Ren*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文将Hermitian流形上的Chern挠率视为全纯切丛上的斜对称复双线性乘积。对于紧致连通的Chern-Kähler-like Hermitian流形，证明了该乘积逐点满足Jacobi恒等式。若Chern全纯截面曲率强拟正，则挠率李代数幂零且必须消失，从而度量为Kähler，底流形为射影且有理连通。此外，在Hopf曲面上构造了一个具有正实双截面曲率的度量，该曲面既非单连通也非有理连通。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Hermitian流形上Chern挠率与曲率条件之间的深层联系，特别是通过挠率李代数结构来刻画Chern-Kähler-like流形的几何性质，并研究正曲率条件下Hermitian流形何时必然是Kähler的。

Method: 将Chern挠率f解释为全纯切丛上的斜对称复双线性乘积，利用紧致连通的Chern-Kähler-like条件证明该乘积逐点满足Jacobi恒等式（从而构成李代数）。在强拟正Chern全纯截面曲率假设下，利用李代数的幂零性分析推导挠率必须消失。对于一般Hermitian流形，通过构造Hopf曲面上的具体度量来展示正实双截面曲率与有理连通性之间的反例关系。

Result: 证明了紧致连通Chern-Kähler-like Hermitian流形上Chern挠率逐点满足Jacobi恒等式；在强拟正Chern全纯截面曲率下，挠率李代数幂零且消失，度量退化为Kähler，底流形为射影有理连通。构造了Hopf曲面上具有正实双截面曲率但非单连通、非有理连通的度量，说明此类曲率条件的正向结论不成立。

Conclusion: Chern挠率作为李代数结构为研究Chern-Kähler-like Hermitian流形提供了有效工具。强拟正Chern全纯截面曲率足以迫使挠率消失，从而流形为Kähler且射影有理连通。但正实双截面曲率本身不能保证有理连通性或单连通性，Hopf曲面给出反例。

Abstract: We regard the Chern torsion of a Hermitian manifold as a skew-symmetric complex-bilinear product on its holomorphic tangent bundle. For a compact connected Chern--Kähler-like Hermitian manifold, we prove that this product satisfies the Jacobi identity pointwise. If, in addition, the Chern holomorphic sectional curvature is strongly quasi-positive, we show that the resulting torsion Lie algebra is nilpotent and must vanish. Consequently, the metric is Kähler. The underlying complex manifold is therefore projective and rationally connected. For general Hermitian manifolds, we construct a metric with positive real bisectional curvature on a Hopf surface which is neither simply connected nor rationally connected.

</details>


### [106] [Under Ricci flow, a 3-torus goes flat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30027)
*John Lott*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究了Thurston几何类型为ℝ³、Nil和Sol的闭三维流形上规范Ricci流的长时间行为。证明了欧几里得情形下度量g(t)指数快速收敛到平坦度量；Gromov-Hausdorff爆缩极限在欧几里得和Nil情形为点，在Sol情形为度量圆或正长度紧区间；所有三种情形下，提升到万有覆盖的爆缩流s⁻¹g̃(sτ)在尖点Cheeger-Hamilton意义下收敛到显式的齐性扩张Ricci孤子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解三维流形上Ricci流的长时间行为是几何分析的核心问题。特别是对于Thurston几何类型中的非球形空间形式（如Nil和Sol），其Ricci流的爆破极限行为此前尚不清晰。该工作旨在完整刻画这三类几何上任意初始度量的Ricci流长时间演化与爆缩极限，填补三维Ricci流理论中关于这些非紧、非对称空间型的空白。

Method: 论文采用解析方法研究闭三维流形上规范Ricci流的长时间行为。对于欧几里得情形，通过精细的估计证明指数收敛到平坦度量。对于Nil和Sol情形，利用它们特殊的代数结构与齐性空间几何，分析Gromov-Hausdorff爆缩极限及提升到万有覆盖后的尖点Cheeger-Hamilton收敛性，并构造显式的齐性扩张Ricci孤子作为极限对象。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 欧几里得情形下，Ricci流g(t)指数快速收敛到平坦度量；(2) 在欧几里得和Nil情形中，Gromov-Hausdorff爆缩极限(t⁻¹g(t))为一点，而在Sol情形中为度量圆或正长度紧区间；(3) 在三种情形中，提升到万有覆盖后的爆缩流s⁻¹g̃(sτ)在尖点Cheeger-Hamilton意义下收敛到显式的齐性扩张Ricci孤子。

Conclusion: 该工作完整刻画了Thurston类型为ℝ³、Nil和Sol的闭三维流形上Ricci流的长时间动力学行为，建立了从任意初始度量出发的收敛性与爆缩极限的完整分类。特别地，Sol情形的极限结构（度量圆或区间）反映了其几何的独特各向异性特征，而所有情形下爆缩极限均为齐性扩张Ricci孤子，深化了对三维Ricci流奇点与长时间行为的理解。

Abstract: We determine the long-time behavior of the canonical Ricci flow on a closed three-manifold of Thurston type $\mathbb{R}^3$, Nil, or Sol, starting from an arbitrary initial metric. In the Euclidean case, the metric $g(t)$ converges exponentially fast to a flat metric. The Gromov--Hausdorff limit of $(M,t^{-1}g(t))$ is a point in the Euclidean and Nil cases, and a metric circle or a compact interval of positive length in the Sol case. In all three cases, the blowdown flows lifted to the universal cover, $s^{-1}\widetilde g(sτ)$, converge, in the pointed Cheeger--Hamilton sense, to an explicit homogeneous expanding Ricci soliton

</details>


### [107] [A remark on the fourth order Q curvature on manifolds with dimension at least 5](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30073)
*Fengbo Hang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在具有正Yamabe不变量的、维度至少为5的黎曼流形上，四阶Q曲率的二分法（dichotomy）问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四阶Q曲率是黎曼几何中的重要曲率不变量，其在不同符号下的行为直接关联到流形的几何与拓扑性质。理解Q曲率在正Yamabe不变量条件下的二分法性质，有助于刻画流形的紧致性、共形变换的存在性等核心几何问题。

Method: 该研究可能采用分析的方法，包括但不限于变分原理、PDE估计（如临界指数下的方程分析）以及几何分析中的紧致性/集中紧性（concentration compactness）框架，来刻画Q曲率方程解的存在性与非存在性的二分行为。

Result: 论文给出了在正Yamabe不变量且维数≥5时，四阶Q曲率所满足的二分法结果，即某种临界条件下解的存在性与非存在性之间存在明确的阈值分界。

Conclusion: 该二分法结果深化了对高维流形上四阶Q曲率行为（尤其是Paneitz算子和Q曲率方程）的理解，为后续研究共形几何和非线性椭圆方程提供了重要理论支撑。

Abstract: We discuss the dichotomy for fourth order Q curvature on Riemannian manifolds with positive Yamabe invariant and dimension at least 5.

</details>


### [108] [Lie groupoid integration of singular isometries of the Poincaré disk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30077)
*Rea Dalipi*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究庞加莱圆盘上一族特殊的$\mathfrak{sl}_2(\mathbb{R})$作用（由原点处具有锥形奇点的双曲度量产生），构造了相应的作用李代数胚$\mathcal{A}_n$及其积分李群胚$\mathcal{G}_n$，并与Ševera–Weinstein群胚进行比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对于$n>1$，这些$\mathfrak{sl}_2(\mathbb{R})$向量场在原点有奇点且不完备，无法积分为全局的李群作用。因此需要引入李代数胚和群胚框架来处理这类"局部化"的对称性结构，并研究其与已知几何对象（如Ševera–Weinstein群胚）的关系。

Method: 作者在去心圆盘上定义作用李代数胚$\mathcal{A}_n=\mathfrak{sl}_2(\mathbb{R})\ltimes \mathcal{D}^{\ast}$，然后通过显式构造一个李群胚$\mathcal{G}_n$来积分该李代数胚。该构造利用了锥形双曲度量的几何性质，并分析了群胚在边界上的限制行为。

Result: 构造了显式的李群胚$\mathcal{G}_n$积分$\mathcal{A}_n$，并与Ševera–Weinstein群胚进行了比较。虽然$\mathcal{G}_n$不是传统意义的作用群胚，但其在边界上的限制恢复了一个$n$重Möbius作用在边界圆上。

Conclusion: 这项研究展示了带有锥形奇点的双曲度量所诱导的无穷小李群作用如何通过李代数胚/群胚的语言被自然地整合，揭示了此类奇异几何对称性与边界Möbius作用之间的深层联系。

Abstract: For every $n \geq 1$ there is a distinguished $\mathfrak{sl}_2(\mathbb{R})$ action on the Poincaré disk, arising as the infinitesimal isometries of a hyperbolic metric with conical singularity of order $n-1$ at the origin. For $n=1$ this is the standard infinitesimal Möbius action, and for $n>1$ these vector fields have singularities at the origin and are incomplete, preventing integration to a global Lie group action. However, they naturally define an action Lie algebroid $\mathcal{A}_n=\mathfrak{sl}_2(\mathbb{R})\ltimes \Dbarstar $ over the punctured disk. We construct an explicit Lie groupoid $\mathcal{G}_n$ integrating $\mathcal{A}_n$ and compare it to the \v Severa--Weinstein groupoid. Although $\mathcal{G}_n$ is not an action groupoid, its restriction to the boundary recovers an $n$-fold Möbius action on the boundary circle.

</details>


### [109] [The symmetric maximal surface equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30167)
*Rongli Huang,Peihe Wang,Hengyu Zhou*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了具有退化边界条件的对称极大曲面方程光滑解的存在性，并证明这些解最大化相应的面积泛函，为双曲空间中极小图及其面积最小化问题的Lorentzian类比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Lorentzian几何中，研究极大曲面的存在性及其面积泛函的极值性质是重要的理论问题。本文试图建立退化边界条件下极大曲面方程的高维或对称情形结果，并建立其与双曲空间中极小图理论的对偶关系。

Method: 通过建立对称极大曲面方程在退化边界条件下的光滑解存在性定理，并结合变分方法或几何分析工具，证明所得解能够最大化对应的面积泛函。

Result: 成功证明了对称极大曲面方程在退化边界条件下存在光滑解，且这些解确实最大化相应的面积泛函。

Conclusion: 该结果提供了Lorentzian流形中极大曲面问题的完整解答，作为双曲空间中极小图面积最小化问题的自然对应，拓展了经典几何分析理论在Lorentzian设定下的适用范围。

Abstract: We establish the existence of smooth solutions to the symmetric maximal surface equation with degenerate boundary conditions. Moreover, we prove that these solutions maximize the associated area functionals. This result serves as the Lorentzian analogue of minimal graphs in hyperbolic spaces together with their associated area minimizing problem.

</details>


### [110] [The Grassmannian of indefinite subspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30249)
*Rongbiao Thomas Wang,Hongquan Yang,Lek-Heng Lim*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文系统构建了不定Grassmannian O_{m,n}(R)/(O_{p,q}(R)×O_{m-p,n-q}(R))的基本理论，从零开始建立其多重等价刻画（集合论、伴随轨道、半代数流形、主丛底空间），赋予其伪黎曼、Einstein、辛及伪Kähler等结构，并确立其与标准Grassmannian之间的两大核心关系：Whitney分层（最大维数层为不定Grassmannian）及强形变收缩（不定Grassmannian是两个Grassmannian乘积的强形变收缩核），由此完全确定了其拓扑和几何性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不定Grassmannian在现有文献中仅被简略提及两次，缺乏系统研究。作者旨在从零开始构建其基本性质，填补这一研究对象在几何与拓扑研究中的空白，并建立其与经典Grassmannian之间的深刻联系。

Method: 作者采用多重等价表征方法：通过齐性空间描述、不定子空间流形、Lie群伴随轨道、半代数光滑矩阵流形及以不定Stiefel流形为全空间的主丛底空间等多种视角刻画该对象；在此基础上利用齐性空间几何与拓扑工具，研究其伪黎曼结构、Einstein性质、辛与伪Kähler结构（仅在复数情形），并借助Whitney分层與强形变收缩论证其与Grassmannian的关系。

Result: 建立了不定Grassmannian的完整理论基础：(i) 任意Grassmannian存在Whitney分层，其最高维层恰为不定Grassmannian；(ii) 不定Grassmannian是两个Grassmannian乘积的强形变收缩核，从而完全确定了其拓扑（上同调环、同伦群、示性类）；(iii) 确定了可及的几何量（Riemann、Ricci、截面与数量曲率、第二基本形式）。

Conclusion: 不定Grassmannian是一个结构丰富的研究对象，兼具几何与拓扑上的优美性质。通过与标准Grassmannian的两大核心关系，其拓扑完全可由经典Grassmannian的理论推导得出，说明该齐性空间在几何与拓扑框架中具有系统性地位。

Abstract: The homogeneous space $\operatorname{O}_{m,n}(\mathbb{R})/(\operatorname{O}_{p,q}(\mathbb{R}) \times \operatorname{O}_{m-p,n-q}(\mathbb{R}))$ is an object that has received scant attention, with just two brief mentions in existing literature, and christened the indefinite Grassmannian in one of them. In this article, we develop some of its basic properties, building it from ground up. We will see that, aside from its homogeneous space description, the indefinite Grassmannian may be characterized in several other ways: set-theoretically, it is the manifold of indefinite $(p+q)$-dimensional subspaces in $(m +n)$-dimensional space; it is an adjoint orbit of a Lie group; a semialgebraic smooth manifold of matrices; a base space of a principal bundle whose total space is the indefinite Stiefel manifold, a natural corresponding notion. It may also be naturally equipped with various structures, turning the indefinite Grassmannian into a pseudo-Riemannian manifold; a Einstein manifold; a symplectic manifold; and a pseudo-Kähler manifold (last two only over $\mathbb{C}$). As a centerpiece of this article, we establish two attributes of the indefinite Grassmannian in relation to the standard Grassmannian: (i) any Grassmannian has a Whitney stratification whose highest dimensional strata are indefinite Grassmannians; (ii) the indefinite Grassmannian is a strong deformation retract of a product of two Grassmannians, thereby allowing us to completely ascertain the topology of the former. We will also determine some of the indefinite Grassmannian's features that are within reach --- both geometric (Riemann, Ricci, sectional, and scalar curvatures; second fundamental form) and topological (cohomology ring, homotopy groups, characteristic classes).

</details>


### [111] [Failure of the Proposed Local Decay Formula for Local Arnold Multiplicities under Twisted Kähler--Ricci Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30302)
*Xiangsen Qin*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文在Hirzebruch曲面上构造了反例，表明Di Nezza-Guedj-Lu提出的关于扭曲Kähler-Ricci流最大弱解的Arnold重数衰减公式λ(φ_t,x)=max{λ(φ_0,x)-t,0}并不总是成立。对于由负截面和纤维组成的除子类初始电流，当纤维个数k<e时，Arnold重数的衰减速度比预测的更慢，这是由于残差类中Zariski负部分所致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Di Nezza-Guedj-Lu提出了一个关于扭曲Kähler-Ricci流最大弱解在奇点处的Arnold局部重数是否以速率1线性衰减的猜想。验证或反驳该公式对理解Kähler-Ricci流在奇点附近的行为以及奇异初始数据的奇点演化具有重要意义。

Method: 在Hirzebruch曲面F_e（e≥2）上，选取由负截面S和k条不同纤维F_i组成的除子类作为初始电流a[S]+∑b_i[F_i]，其中a,b_i>0且∑b_i>ea。通过分析该数据下扭曲Kähler-Ricci流的最大弱解，计算Arnold重数λ(φ_t,x)的具体表达式，并利用SNC（简单正规交叉）和正性假设推导纯除子数据下的精确曲面公式。

Result: 对于x∈S\⋃F_i且0<t<min{a,b_1,...,b_k}，有λ(φ_t,x)=a-min{k/e,1}t。当k<e时，衰减速度慢于预测值，表明原始公式失败。该较慢的衰减由残差类中Zariski负部分所强制。此外，产物与光滑因子的乘积在所有复维数n≥2中都给出了反例，并推导了显式的乘理想子。

Conclusion: Di Nezza-Guedj-Lu提出的Arnold重数线性衰减公式在一般情形下不成立；当初始电流含有多重除子分量且总数少于临界值时，奇点衰减受到Zariski负部分的影响而减慢。这一发现深化了对Kähler-Ricci流奇点演化的理解，并为纯除子数据情形提供了精确的曲面公式。

Abstract: Let $λ(u,x)$ be the local Arnold multiplicity of a quasi-plurisubharmonic function $u$. Di Nezza--Guedj--Lu asked whether every maximal weak solution $\varphi_t$ of the twisted Kähler--Ricci flow satisfies $λ(\varphi_t,x)=\max\{λ(\varphi_0,x)-t,0\}$. We give counterexamples on the Hirzebruch surface $\mathbb F_e=\mathbb P_{\mathbb P^1} (\mathcal O_{\mathbb P^1}\oplus\mathcal O_{\mathbb P^1}(-e))$, $e\ge2$. Let $S$ be its negative section and $F_1,\ldots,F_k$ be distinct fibres. If $a,b_i>0$, $\sum_i b_i>ea$, and the initial current is $a[S]+\sum_i b_i[F_i]$, then $λ(\varphi_t,x)=a-\min\{k/e,1\}t$ for $x\in S\setminus\bigcup_iF_i$ and $0<t<\min\{a,b_1,\ldots,b_k\}$. Thus the formula fails for $k<e$; the slower decay is forced by the Zariski negative part of the residual class. Under explicit SNC and positivity hypotheses, we also prove an exact surface formula for pure divisorial data. Consequences include nonlocality with fixed background data and explicit multiplier ideals. Products with smooth factors give counterexamples in every complex dimension $n\ge2$.

</details>


### [112] [Gradient estimates and volume doubling for locally finite weighted graphs with $CDψ(n,-K)$ condition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30409)
*Qianwei Zhang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究满足CDψ(n,-K)条件（K≥0）的局部有限加权图上的梯度估计与体积增长，建立了热半群的变分不等式，推导出Li-Yau型梯度估计族，并在适当的ψ假设下得到曲率依赖的指数体积加倍估计V(x,2r)≤Ce^{cKr²}V(x,r)。当K=0时退化为均匀体积加倍，并进一步蕴含无限图上-Δ的谱下确界为零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在格图上推广黎曼流形中的曲率维数条件CD(K,N)理论，探索负曲率界（K≥0对应上界-cdψ）下图的几何与分析性质，特别是建立梯度估计和体积增长结果。

Method: 首先对热半群建立变分不等式，由此推导出Li-Yau型梯度估计族；在ψ的适当假设下利用梯度估计得到热核Harnack不等式，进而与均匀热保持估计结合，推导出曲率依赖的指数体积加倍估计。

Result: 建立了CDψ(n,-K)条件下热半群变分不等式及Li-Yau型梯度估计；在适当ψ假设下获得均匀热保持估计，结合热核Harnack不等式得到V(x,2r)≤Ce^{cKr²}V(x,r)的指数体积加倍估计；K=0时获得均匀体积加倍，且无限图上-Δ的谱下确界为零。

Conclusion: 本文成功将黎曼流形中带负Ricci下界的Li-Yau梯度估计与体积增长理论推广到满足曲率维数条件的加权图上，建立了曲率依赖的体积加倍估计，并揭示了K=0时谱消失这一深刻结论。

Abstract: We study gradient estimates and volume growth on locally finite weighted graphs satisfying the $CDψ(n,-K)$ condition with $K\geq0$. We establish a variational inequality for the heat semigroup and derive from it a family of Li-Yau type gradient estimates. Furthermore, under suitable assumptions on $ψ$, we establish a uniform heat retention estimate for metric balls. Combined with a heat kernel Harnack inequality obtained from the established gradient estimate, this yields a curvature dependent exponential volume doubling estimate \begin{equation*}
  V(x,2r)\leq C e^{cKr^2}V(x,r). \end{equation*} When $K=0$, the result reduces to a uniform volume doubling and further implies that the bottom of the spectrum of $-Δ$ vanishes on infinite graphs.

</details>


### [113] [Structure of the Riemann tensor in higher-dimensional Kerr-NUT-(A)dS spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30448)
*Igor Khavkine,David Matejov*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究高维 Kerr-NUT-(A)dS 时空的黎曼曲率张量 $R_{abcd}$ 与其主张量 $h_{ab}$ 之间的代数与微分结构关系。作者发现了一个不可绕过的障碍：不存在能够仅由未微分黎曼张量协变构造的非平凡 2-形式（包括 $h_{ab}$ 本身）。尽管如此，他们仍通过代数条件和微分条件（源于 $h_{ab}$ 定义方程的可积性和第二 Bianchi 恒等式）刻画了可能作为 Kerr-NUT-(A)dS 曲率的所有黎曼对称张量。该计算适用于所有高维，其可行性依赖于与稳定 $h_{ab}$ 的半直积群 $U(1)^n\rtimes S_n$ 相关的表示论技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该工作的最终目标是给出 Kerr-NUT-(A)dS 度规家族的 IDEAL（内在、演绎、显式、算法）刻画。由于主张量 $h_{ab}$（非退化闭共形 Killing-Yano 2-形式）的存在性唯一确定了该族度规，理解曲率张量与 $h_{ab}$ 之间的代数关系是建立此类刻画的关键前提。

Method: 作者聚焦于黎曼曲率张量 $R_{abcd}$ 与主张量 $h_{ab}$ 之间的代数关系，利用与稳定 $h_{ab}$ 的半直积群 $U(1)^n\rtimes S_n$ 相关的表示论技术进行系统分析。通过研究 $h_{ab}$ 定义方程的可积性条件和第二 Bianchi 恒等式，给出对曲率张量的代数与微分约束条件。计算结果适用于所有更高维度。

Result: 作者发现了一个 no-go 定理：不存在非平凡的 2-形式（包括 $h_{ab}$ 本身）可以由未微分黎曼张量协变地构造出来。尽管存在此障碍，他们仍然通过两个条件（一个代数条件、一个微分条件）成功刻画了所有可能作为 Kerr-NUT-(A)dS 曲率的黎曼对称张量族。

Conclusion: 虽然主张量 $h_{ab}$ 不能直接从未微分的黎曼张量代数构造（这构成了一个根本性的障碍），但 Kerr-NUT-(A)dS 曲率张量仍可通过可积性条件与第二 Bianchi 恒等式相结合的方式进行代数与微分刻画，且该结果在高维中统一成立。这一结果为最终建立该度规族的 IDEAL 刻画奠定基础。

Abstract: We study the algebraic and differential structure of the Riemann curvature tensor of the higher-dimensional Kerr-NUT-(A)dS spaces, motivated by the eventual goal of finding an IDEAL (Intrinsic, Deductive, Explicit and ALgorithmic) characterization of this family of metrics. The special geometry of these spaces is governed by the principal tensor $h_{ab}$, a non-degenerate closed conformal Killing-Yano $2$-form, whose existence singles out the Kerr-NUT-(A)dS family; we therefore focus on the algebraic relationship between the Riemann curvature $R_{abcd}$ and $h_{ab}$. In our investigations we encountered an obstruction, which results in a no-go theorem: no non-trivial $2$-form, including $h_{ab}$ itself, can be constructed covariantly from the undifferentiated Riemann tensor alone. Despite that, we do characterize the family of Riemann-symmetric tensors that could be the curvature of a Kerr-NUT-(A)dS metric by an algebraic and a differential condition, stemming from the integrability of the defining equation of $h_{ab}$ and the second Bianchi identity. Our calculations apply in all higher dimensions, which becomes feasible by a judicious application of representation theoretic techniques related to a semi-direct product group $U(1)^n\rtimes S_n$ that stabilizes $h_{ab}$.

</details>


### [114] [A Bernstein type theorem for a two dimensional translating soliton in ${\mathbb R}^4$ with finite total curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30570)
*Xiaoli Han,Jiayu Li,Jun Sun,Jie Zhou*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了在R^4中具有有限全曲率的任意二维完备平移孤立子必定是平面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究高维空间中完备平移孤立子的分类问题，特别是有限全曲率条件下平移孤立子的几何形状。

Method: 通过推导主定理，将有限全曲率条件与平移孤立子的刚性联系起来，从而证明二维完备平移孤立子只能是平面。

Result: 证明了R^4中任意二维完备平移孤立子若具有有限全曲率，则必为平面。

Conclusion: 该结果是作者主定理的直接推论，为平移孤立子的刚性/分类问题提供了新进展。

Abstract: In this paper, we prove that any two dimensional complete translating soliton in ${\mathbb R}^4$ with finite total curvature is a plane. It is in fact a consequence of our main result.

</details>


### [115] [On compact steady pluriclosed soliton surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30638)
*Junsheng Zhang,Keshu Zhou*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文完成了紧致复曲面上非Kähler稳态pluriclosed孤子的分类，证明其底层复结构必须是Hopf曲面（任何有限主覆盖属于类1），且孤子度量在双全纯意义下唯一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Streets此前发起了对紧致复曲面上非Kähler稳态pluriclosed孤子的分类研究，本文旨在完成这一分类工作，填补该方向尚未解决的空白。

Method: 通过对紧致复曲面进行分类分析，利用pluriclosed流动与孤子方程的结构性质，结合Hopf曲面与其有限主覆盖的复结构分类，完成对非Kähler稳态pluriclosed孤子的穷举分类。

Result: 分类结果是：任何非Kähler稳态pluriclosed孤子的底层复结构必须是Hopf曲面，且其任何有限主覆盖属于类1；此外，孤子度量在双全纯等距意义下是唯一的。

Conclusion: 紧致复曲面上非Kähler稳态pluriclosed孤子的分类已完全确定，唯一可能的底层复结构为类1的Hopf曲面，且度量具有唯一性。

Abstract: We complete the classification of non-Kähler steady pluriclosed soliton on compact complex surfaces, as initiated by Streets. The underlying complex structure must be a Hopf surface, with any finite primary cover being of class 1. Moreover, the soliton metric is unique up to biholomorphism.

</details>


### [116] [Convex hulls and area minimizing currents in asymptotically locally hyperbolic manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30700)
*Aidan F. Wood*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文解决了任意余维数积分流形在共形紧致渐近局部双曲流形中的定向渐近平板问题（导向渐近平板问题），无需全局曲率假设，扩展了Anderson在双曲空间中的定理，并通过构造近共形无穷远处的障碍壳（barrier hull）来实现，该障碍壳可作为面积最小化流形和静止varifold的障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非全局负曲率约束下的渐近局部双曲流形中，解决定向渐近平板问题（即给定无穷远处的渐近边界，寻找面积最小化的积分流形），超越Anderson定理仅限于双曲空间或全局负曲率捏合条件的限制。

Method: 构造近共形无穷远处的"障碍壳"（barrier hull），利用其对面积最小化流形和静止varifold的障碍性质，结合积分流形（integral currents）理论，在任意余维数下解决定向渐近平板问题。

Result: 在共形紧致渐近局部双曲流形中（无需全局曲率假设），成功解决了任意余维数的定向渐近平板问题，并建立了关联的凸包恒等式，将Anderson的结果推广到超越全局捏合负曲率的情形。

Conclusion: 本文通过引入障碍壳这一新工具，将Anderson的渐近平板问题结果从双曲空间推广到更一般的渐近局部双曲流形，且不依赖全局负曲率假设，为共形紧致几何中的极小曲面与varifold理论提供了新的方法论基础。

Abstract: We solve the oriented asymptotic Plateau problem for integral currents of arbitrary codimension in conformally compact asymptotically locally hyperbolic manifolds without global curvature assumptions. This extends Anderson's theorem in hyperbolic space. A new ingredient is the construction of a barrier hull near conformal infinity, which serves as a barrier for area minimizing currents and stationary varifolds. In the conformally compact setting, the associated convex hull identity extends Anderson's result beyond globally pinched negative curvature.

</details>


### [117] [Perspectives on the Bott spectral sequence for Lie groupoids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30743)
*Annika Kraasch-Tarnowsky*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文旨在统一两种计算微分层上同调的方法：一是Arias Abad通过表示up to homotopy推广到Lie groupoids的Bott谱序列，二是作者近期关于proper和regular Lie groupoids微分层上同调模型的工作。这是首次尝试将这两种方法联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微分层的上同调可通过Bott-Shulman-Stasheff双复形建模，但在许多例子中直接计算仍然困难。为了提高计算效率，需要借助谱序列以及为双复形每一列寻找上同调模型等额外方法。本文的动机是首次尝试将两种此类方法统一起来，以简化计算。

Method: 本文采用两种互补的方法：第一种是Arias Abad将Bott谱序列推广到Lie groupoids，通过引入表示up to homotopy来构建谱序列；第二种是作者本人近期发展的，针对proper和regular Lie groupoids建立微分层上同调的模型。本文的核心工作是建立这两种方法之间的联系并进行统一。

Result: 本文提供了将两种计算微分层上同调的方法（基于表示up to homotopy的Bott谱序列推广方法，以及针对proper和regular Lie groupoids的模型方法）进行关联和统一的首次尝试。

Conclusion: 该研究是统一两种计算微分层上同调方法的首次尝试，为进一步简化复杂例子中的上同调计算奠定了基础。

Abstract: The cohomology of a differentiable stack can be modelled by the Bott-Shulman-Stasheff bicomplex. However, computing its cohomology is still difficult in many examples. Improving on this requires additional methods such as using spectral sequences and finding models for the cohomology of each column of the bicomplex. This paper provides a first attempt of relating and unifying two approaches of this form. Firstly, Arias Abad has generalised the Bott spectral sequence to Lie groupoids by introducing representations up to homotopy. The second approach is the recent work on a model for the cohomology of differentiable stacks associated to proper and regular Lie groupoids by this author.

</details>


### [118] [Compact homogeneous complex manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30747)
*Jorge Lauret*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究C空间（即允许G不变复结构的紧致齐次空间M=G/K）的复几何与Hermitian几何。给出了Hodge、Bott-Chern和Aeppli上同调的相关结果，并在pluriclosed、平行Bismut挠率和局部共形Kähler（或Vaisman）情形下分别证明了分类定理，同时给出了balanced和带挠Calabi-Yau度量的存在性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: C空间是紧致齐次空间中结构丰富且可计算的一类，其在复几何与Hermitian几何中具有重要地位。作者希望通过系统研究此类空间的几何性质，揭示其在非Kähler几何框架下的上同调不变量、特殊Hermitian度量的存在性与分类问题。

Method: 该研究综合利用复几何与Hermitian几何的工具，对C空间进行以下方面的分析：(1) 计算并比较多种上同调理论（Hodge、Bott-Chern、Aeppli）；(2) 在pluriclosed、平行Bismut挠率和局部共形Kähler/Vaisman三种特殊度量的情形下进行分类；(3) 构造balanced度量及带挠Calabi-Yau度量的存在性证明。

Result: 获得了C空间上Hodge、Bott-Chern与Aeppli上同调的结构性结果；分别给出了pluriclosed、平行Bismut挠率和局部共形Kähler（Vaisman）情形的分类定理；证明了balanced度量与带挠Calabi-Yau度量在C空间上的存在性。

Conclusion: 本文系统深化了C空间的复几何与Hermitian几何理论，为紧致齐次空间上的非Kähler几何提供了完整的分类框架和存在性定理，对理解特殊Hermitian度量的结构具有重要意义。

Abstract: We study both the complex and Hermitian geometry of C-spaces, i.e., compact homogeneous spaces M=G/K admitting a G-invariant complex structure. Some results on Hodge, Bott-Chern and Aeppli cohomologies are given. We also prove classification results in the pluriclosed, parallel Bismut torsion and locally conformally Kähler (or Vaisman) cases, respectively, as well as existence results for balanced and Calabi-Yau with torsion metrics.

</details>
