### [145] [Isometries on algebras of locally measurable operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28710)
*Jinghao Huang,Karimbergen Kudaybergenov,Bing Yan*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文研究von Neumann代数$\mathcal{M}$的局部可测算子代数$LS(\mathcal{M})$上的等距映射。证明了任何双射线性等距都具有规范形式$\Phi(x)=wJ(x)$（其中$w$为酉元，$J$为Jordan $^*$-同构），推广了Banach-Stone定理和Kadison定理。同时对多种von Neumann代数（包括所有$\mathrm{II}_\infty$型、$\mathrm{III}$型代数、所有因子及原子中心为空的代数）证明了$F$-范数与概率测度和维度函数对$(\mu, D)$之间的一一对应关系，但对具有原子中心的代数该对应关系不成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Banach-Stone定理和Kadison定理刻画了连续函数代数和C*-代数上的等距结构，但这些问题尚未在局部可测算子代数（即von Neumann代数仿射到非有界算子的一般化框架）中得到系统研究。作者旨在建立局部可测算子代数框架下的等距表示定理，并明确$F$-范数与维度函数、概率测度之间的内在联系。

Method: 通过引入基于维度函数与概率测度所定义的$F$-范数，系统研究$LS(\mathcal{M})$上的双射线性等距。作者分别从等距映射的规范性表示与$F$-范数的参数化两个方向展开分析，利用Jordan $^*$-同构理论及von Neumann代数结构分类（$\mathrm{II}_\infty$型、$\mathrm{III}$型、因子等）来证明主要结果，并通过构造反例说明原子中心情形下对应关系的失效。

Result: (1) 所有$LS(\mathcal{M})$上的双射线性等距均可规范表示为$\Phi(x)=wJ(x)$，其中$w$是酉元，$J$是Jordan $^*$-同构；(2) 在$\mathrm{II}_\infty$型、$\mathrm{III}$型代数、所有因子以及原子中心为空的代数等广泛条件下，$F$-范数与$(\mu, D)$之间一一对应；(3) 对具有原子中心的代数，这一对应关系不成立，表明该情形需要更精细的结构分析。

Conclusion: 本文将Banach-Stone定理和Kadison定理推广到局部可测算子代数范畴，建立了等距映射的规范表示定理，并在大部分von Neumann代数类型下给出了$F$-范数的完整参数化。同时指出原子中心的情形是例外，这为进一步研究开辟了方向。

Abstract: Let $LS(\mathcal{M})$ be the algebra of locally measurable operators affiliated with a von Neumann algebra $\mathcal{M}$, equipped with an $F$-norm defined via a dimension function and a probability measure. We prove that every bijective linear isometry between $LS(\mathcal{M})$ admits a canonical representation of the form $Φ(x)=wJ(x)$, where $w$ is a unitary element and $J$ is a Jordan $^*$-isomorphism, which extends classical results such as the Banach--Stone theorem and Kadison's theorem. Under several structural assumptions on the underlying von Neumann algebras (including all type $\mathrm{II}_\infty$ and type $\mathrm{III}$ algebras, and all factors, and algebras with atomless centers), we prove the one-to-one correspondence between the $F$-norm and the pair $(μ, D)$ of a probability measure and a dimension function, which fails for algebras with atomic centers.

</details>


### [146] [Non-MF groups and non-finite full group C*-algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28772)
*Caleb Eckhardt*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 该论文研究具有性质(T)的群Γ及其关联的上升HNN扩张G，证明该构造产生的群W不是MF群，且C*-代数C*(G)不是有限C*-代数。论文还声明其核心思想与证明由ChatGPT 5.6 Sol生成，作者仅进行了整理和润色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在探索性质(T)群与上升HNN扩张相关的群的算子代数性质，具体回答这类构造产生的C*-代数是否具有MF性质或有限性质，从而加深对非可分解群及其C*-代数结构的理解。

Method: 论文利用性质(T)群Γ的特定性质（存在单的非满自同态），构造上升HNN扩张G，再通过半直积结构定义群W。证明策略是通过群的结构性质来推理其约化C*-代数是否具备MF性质或有限性，具体论证细节未在摘要中展开。

Result: 证明了W不是MF群，且C*(G)不是有限C*-代数。这些结果为性质(T)群及其HNN扩张在算子代数框架下的分类提供了新的否定性结果。

Conclusion: 该研究明确了具有性质(T)的群通过上升HNN扩张得到的关联C*-代数不满足MF和有限性条件，为相关领域的进一步研究提供了新方向。同时，论文坦诚指出其证明过程借助了AI工具生成，体现了一种新颖的学术协作模式。

Abstract: Let $Γ$ be a property (T) group admitting an injective, non-surjective endomorphism and let $G$ be the associated ascending HNN-extension. Let $W=\left(\bigoplus_{G/Γ} \mathbb{Z}/2\mathbb{Z}\right) \rtimes G.$ We show that $W$ is not an MF group and that $C^*(G)$ is not a finite C*-algebra. The ideas and proofs were generated by ChatGPT 5.6 Sol, we have only refined their arguments in a hopefully more palatable form.

</details>


### [147] [Property (SP) and Inclusions of C*-algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30057)
*Alec Gow,Roberto Hernández Palomares*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文引入C*-代数包含的Property (SP)概念（推广Gabe和Neagu的实秩零包含），系统研究其拓扑描述、构建下的久存性，并证明不可约C*-离散包含保持简单可分C*-代数的Property (SP)，最后否定地回答了关于Furstenburg边界包含的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gabe和Neagu提出的实秩零包含是一个重要概念，但其适用范围有限。作者希望通过引入更一般的Property (SP)包含来推广并系统化研究，同时回答Gabe和Neagu遗留的关于Furstenburg边界包含是否具有实秩零（进而是否具有Property (SP)）的开放问题。

Method: 首先定义SP-包含（超越实秩零包含的推广），在交换情形下给出拓扑描述；其次验证其在多种构造下的久存性；再次将在经典对称群框架下绕道保持Property (SP)的结果推广到量子对称（不可约C*-离散包含）；最后通过构造一个可数、离散、非顺从群G，证明C_r^*(G)⊆C(∂_F G)⋊_r G不是SP-包含，从而否定了Gabe和Neagu的问题。

Result: 建立了SP-包含的交换拓扑刻画及多种构造下的久存性；证明Property (SP)在不可约C*-离散包含下被保持（这是从经典对称到量子对称的推广）；构造了具体的非顺从群G，使得C_r^*(G)⊆C(∂_F G)⋊_r G不是SP-包含，否定回答了Gabe和Neagu的问题。

Conclusion: Property (SP)是实秩零包含的合理一般化，具有丰富的结构和良好的稳定性；量子对称情形下的久存性成立；但Furstenburg边界对应的包含并不总是具有Property (SP)，这揭示了该领域更深层的复杂性。

Abstract: As a generalization of Gabe and Neagu's inclusions of real rank zero, we introduce a notion of Property (SP) for inclusions of C*-algebras. We begin the systematic study of SP-inclusions, establishing a topological description in the commutative setting and permanence under many constructions. We also extend known results about the permanence of Property (SP) for C*-algebras under classical symmetries to the setting of quantum symmetries; we show that Property (SP) for (simple, separable) C*-algebras is preserved under irreducible C*-discrete inclusions. Finally, we resolve (in the negative) a question raised by Gabe and Neagu about inclusions coming from Furstenburg boundaries. Namely, we show the existence of a countable, discrete, non-amenable group $G$ such that $C_r^*(G) \subseteq C(\partial_F G) \rtimes_r G$ is not an SP-inclusion (and therefore not an inclusion of real rank zero).

</details>


### [148] [Simplicity and pure infiniteness for $\text{C}^*$-algebras associated with stabilizers of boundary actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30127)
*Felipe Flores,Joseph Gondek*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文利用Rieffel诱导方法，将协变(Λ, X)-表示从稳定子子群Λ诱导到整个群Γ上，从而构造新的C*-代数。当X是Γ-边界时，得到新的简单、无迹群C*-代数类；当X是极值边界时，这些C*-代数还是纯无限的，并证明相关的C*-代数在Robert意义下是"selfless"的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在通过群在紧空间上的作用所诱导的C*-代数类，丰富对群C*-代数结构的理解，特别是回答Kalantar和Scarparo关于Thompson群相关C*-代数的问题。

Method: 采用Rieffel诱导的方法，将协变(Λ, X)-表示（其中Λ是X的稳定子子群）诱导到全群Γ，利用Λ上特定字符构造表示及其生成的C*-代数。通过考察边界理论（Γ-边界、极值边界）来刻画所构造C*-代数的结构性质。

Result: 1) 当X是Γ-边界时，获得新的简单、无迹群C*-代数类；2) 当Γ-边界X是极值边界时，所得群C*-代数是纯无限的，部分回答了Kalantar和Scarparo的问题；3) 与Thompson群相关的C*-代数被证明在Robert意义下是selfless的。

Conclusion: Rieffel诱导为构造和分类新的简单群C*-代数提供了有效途径，且在极值边界的条件下可进一步保证纯无限性，推进了群C*-代数结构理论以及Thompson群相关问题的研究。

Abstract: Given a discrete group $Γ$ acting on a compact space $X$, and a stabilizer subgroup $Λ\leq Γ$ of $X$, we use the Rieffel induction of covariant $(Λ, X)$-representations to study classes of representations induced by certain characters on $Λ$ and the $\text{C}^*$-algebras they generate. When $X$ is a $Γ$-boundary, we obtain new classes of simple, traceless group $\text{C}^*$-algebras. When the $Γ$-boundary $X$ is an extreme boundary, we show that the associated group $\text{C}^*$-algebras are also purely infinite, answering part of a question posed by Kalantar and Scarparo on $\text{C}^*$-algebras associated with Thompson's groups. The latter $\text{C}^*$-algebras are shown to be selfless in the sense of Robert.

</details>


### [149] [Tingley's Problem for Haagerup Noncommutative $L_p$-Spaces, $1<p\ne 2<\infty$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30131)
*Jinghao Huang,Yunpeng Zhu*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文解决了Haagerup非交换L_p空间上的Tingley问题（1<p≠2<∞），肯定回答了Mori提出的公开问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Tingley问题关注于Banach空间单位球面之间的等距延拓问题，是泛函分析中长期未解的经典难题。Mori在其研究中将其推广到非交换L_p空间的框架下并提出公开问题（Problem 6.3），本文旨在解决该问题。

Method: 本文采用特定的分析技术（摘要未详细说明），在Haagerup非交换L_p空间的框架下求解Tingley问题，覆盖1<p≠2<∞的参数范围，即排除了p=1和p=2这两个特殊情况。

Result: 成功解决了Haagerup非交换L_p空间（1<p≠2<∞）上的Tingley问题，给出了肯定性的回答，从而解决了Mori提出的问题6.3。

Conclusion: Tingley问题在Haagerup非交换L_p空间上（除p=2外）得到肯定解答，确认了Mori猜想在该框架下成立，这是非交换调和分析与Banach空间几何理论交叉领域的重要进展。

Abstract: We solve Tingley's problem for Haagerup noncommutative $L_p$-spaces, where $1<p\ne 2<\infty$, which gives an affirmative answer to Mori's problem \cite[Problem~6.3]{Mori}.

</details>


### [150] [Jensen' s trace inequality with equality condition and noncommutative Lamperti's theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30172)
*Kai Fang,Xin He,Jinghao Huang*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文在一般半有限冯·诺依曼代数框架下完整建立了Jensen迹不等式并刻画了其等号成立条件，回答了Kosaki和Harada-Kosaki提出的两个开放问题；作为应用，推导了非交换Lamperti型不等式及其等号条件，并以此刻画了一类F-范数非交换Orlicz空间上的线性等距（不必是满射），提供了非交换的Lamperti线性等距定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在半有限冯·诺依曼代数中，Jensen迹不等式的完整形式和等号条件尚未被解决，这是Kosaki（2013）和Harada-Kosaki（2008）提出的开放问题。此外，非交换Orlicz空间上的线性等距刻画是非交换分析中的核心课题，需要借助更一般的工具（如Lamperti型不等式）来解决。

Method: 作者首先在带半有限忠实正规迹τ的半有限冯·诺依曼代数上建立完整的Jensen迹不等式并刻画等号条件；随后利用该结果推导非交换Lamperti型不等式及其等号条件；最后运用这些不等式对一个关于F-范数非交换Orlicz空间上线性（不必满射）等距的刻画问题给出完整解答。

Result: 获得了Jensen迹不等式的完整形式及其等号刻画，从而回答了Kosaki和Harada-Kosaki提出的两个问题；据此导出了非交换Lamperti型不等式及等号条件，并完成了F-范数非交换Orlicz空间上线性等距的完整刻画。

Conclusion: 本文的工作填补了半有限冯·诺依曼代数中Jensen迹不等式一般化的空白，并将其成功应用于非交换Orlicz空间等距理论，建立了非交换版本的Lamperti线性等距定理。

Abstract: Let $\mathcal M$ be a semifinite von Neumann algebra equipped with a semifinite faithful normal trace $τ$. We establish Jensen's trace inequality in full generality and characterize its equality case, which answers two questions raised in [Kosaki2013] and [HaradaKosaki2008]. As an application, we derive noncommutative Lamperti-type inequalities and their equality conditions. Employing this result, we characterize linear isometries (not necessarily surjective) on a class of $F$-normed noncommutative Orlicz spaces, which provides a noncommutative Lamperti's theorem for linear isometries.

</details>


### [151] [Pointwise convergence of noncommutative ergodic averages along the primes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30339)
*Guixiang Hong,Liang Wang*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文研究von Neumann代数上沿素数序列的遍历平均。在正常忠实半有限迹和保迹自同构的设定下，作者证明了强极大不等式成立，并获得了L_p空间上的双边几乎一致收敛，同时解答了Chen、Hong、Wang等人之前遗留的一个开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典情况下，Bourgain等人利用点态极大函数和例外集工具证明了沿素数的遍历平均收敛性。但在非交换（von Neumann代数）设定中，这些点态工具不再可用，因此需要开发新的技术路径来建立类似的收敛结果，并回答Chen等人的开放问题。

Method: 作者采用了圆法（circle method）与非交换采样原理相结合的技术路线。对于收敛性证明，不同于Bourgain的交换论证（依赖点态极大函数和例外集），作者转而证明遍历平均的尾部在L_2(N;ℓ_∞)中趋于零，并证明与极限的差属于L_2(N;c_0)，由此推导出双边几乎一致收敛。

Result: 对于所有1<p<∞，证明了(L_p(N))上沿素数遍历平均(A_N)的强极大不等式，并得到A_N(x)在L_p(N)中对每个x双边几乎一致收敛。

Conclusion: 该结果将Bourgain关于素数遍历平均的经典定理推广到了非交换设定，填补了非交换遍历理论中沿稀疏序列收敛性的空白，并正面回答了Chen、Hong、Wang在2024年论文中遗留的问题。

Abstract: Let $\mathcal N$ be a von Neumann algebra equipped with a normal faithful semifinite trace, and let $γ$ be a trace-preserving automorphism of $\mathcal N$. We consider the ergodic averages along the prime numbers \[
  A_N(x)
  :=
  \frac1{|P_N|}
  \sum_{q\in P_N}γ^q(x),
  \qquad
  P_N:=\{q\leq N:q\ \text{is prime}\}. \] For every $1<p<\infty$, we prove a strong maximal inequality for $(A_N)_{N\geq2}$ on $L_p(\mathcal N)$ and that $A_N(x)$ converges bilaterally almost uniformly for every $x\in L_p(\mathcal N)$.
  The proof exploits the circle method and a noncommutative sampling principle. For the convergence result, Bourgain's commutative argument uses pointwise maximal functions and exceptional sets. These tools are not available in the noncommutative setting. Instead, we show that the tails of the ergodic averages tend to zero in $L_2(\mathcal N;\ell_\infty)$ and that the difference from the limit belongs to $L_2(\mathcal N;c_0)$. This gives the desired b.a.u. convergence, and provides a positive answer to one question left open in \cite{ChenHongWang+arXiv2024}.

</details>


### [152] [Quantum non-local games: Quantum relations, projection lattices and rule operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30507)
*Alexandros Chatzinikolaou*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的算子代数框架来比较多种量子非局部博弈模型（包括rank-one量子博弈、量子XOR博弈、投影格博弈、概率量子超图博弈和Frobenius代数规则算子博弈）。作者为每个规则引入"获胜变换空间"（winning transformation space）作为统一比较工具，并提出了具有有限维输入输出von Neumann代数及任意（可能无限维）裁判von Neumann代数的R-投影测试量子博弈，证明其获胜变换空间恰好是具有自然双模结构的算子空间（即矩形量子关系）。论文比较了该形式化与投影格博弈、超图量子博弈和图形规则算子定义，将规则算子通过Daws技术转化为有限维von Neumann代数张量积中的投影，最后与Goldberg及Bochniak-Kasprzak-Sołtan的同步性条件进行比较，并用经典博弈、rank-one、量子XOR、着色、量子图同态及同构博弈加以说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子非局部博弈在量子输入或输出的情形下已被多种不同数学语言形式化，包括rank-one博弈、量子XOR博弈、支撑映射、概率量子超图以及Frobenius代数规则算子等。这些模型之间缺乏统一的比较框架，难以厘清它们各自的表达能力、等价关系以及相互间的转换。作者旨在建立一个统一的算子代数视角，以系统性地比较这些看似不同但内在关联的量子博弈模型。

Method: 作者提出"获胜变换空间"这一核心概念——即能被确定性接受的变换的算子空间，作为统一比较工具。具体方法包括：(1) 引入具有有限维输入/输出von Neumann代数及任意维裁判von Neumann代数R的R-投影测试量子博弈，证明其获胜变换空间恰为具有自然双模结构的算子空间（矩形量子关系）；(2) 与投影格博弈、超图量子博弈及图形规则算子定义逐一比较；(3) 利用Daws技术将规则算子转化为有限维von Neumann代数张量积中的投影；(4) 比较并发性（concurrency）与Goldberg和Bochniak-Kasprzak-Sołtan的同步性条件。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 投影格博弈恰好刻画自反获胜双模；(2) 超图量子博弈可被值保持地实现为投影测试博弈，且在完美变换下与投影格博弈描述相同的自反部分；(3) 规则算子可映射为有限维von Neumann代数张量积中的投影，同时保持博弈值和完美性；(4) 统一框架可成功涵盖经典、rank-one、量子XOR、着色、量子图同态和同构博弈等多种实例。

Conclusion: 作者成功建立了量子非局部博弈的统一算子双模框架，证明多个先前看似独立的模型实际上在获胜变换空间的观点下可以相互关联甚至等价。该框架不仅澄清了不同博弈模型之间的关系，还提供了与同步性条件比较的手段，为量子博弈理论的进一步研究提供了统一的数学基础。

Abstract: Quantum non-local games with quantum inputs or outputs have been formulated in several different languages, including rank-one and quantum XOR games, support maps between projection lattices, probabilistic quantum hypergraphs, and Frobenius-algebraic rule operators on finite quantum sets. We give a unified operator-algebraic comparison of these models by assigning to each rule its winning transformation space: the operator space of transformations accepted with certainty. We introduce \(\mathcal R\)-projection-test quantum games with finite-dimensional input and output von Neumann algebras and an arbitrary, possibly infinite-dimensional, referee von Neumann algebra \(\mathcal R\). Their winning transformation spaces are precisely operator spaces with a natural bimodule structure, equivalently rectangular quantum relations. We compare this formalism with projection-lattice games, hypergraph quantum games, and the graphical rule-operator definition. Projection-lattice games capture exactly the reflexive winning bimodules. Hypergraph quantum games admit value-preserving projection-test realisations, and, after passing to perfect transformations, describe the same reflexive part as projection-lattice games. Using Daws' technique, we also translate rule operators to projections in tensor products of finite-dimensional von Neumann algebras. This identification places graphical rules in the same operator-bimodule framework and preserves both values and perfectness. Finally, we compare concurrency with the synchronicity conditions of Goldberg and of Bochniak--Kasprzak--Sołtan. The framework is illustrated by classical, rank-one, quantum XOR, colouring, and quantum graph homomorphism and isomorphism games.

</details>


### [153] [The pure infiniteness transfer problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30906)
*Arindam Sutradhar*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文研究纯无限性传递问题：稠密子代数作为环是纯无限的，是否迫使其C*-完备化也是纯无限的。作者在A有实秩零且A₀在全纯函数演算下封闭的假设下解决了该问题，证明此时两者等价，并给出一个障碍结果和一个具体实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Pino、Goodearl、Perera和Molina提出的问题8.4：一个C*-代数A的稠密子代数A₀若作为环是纯无限的，是否能推出A作为C*-代数也是纯无限的？该问题即使在一元且简单的情形下仍是开放的，本研究旨在在额外假设下解决此问题。

Method: 作者引入"纯无限性传递问题"的概念，在A具有实秩零（real rank zero）且A₀在全纯函数演算（holomorphic functional calculus）下封闭的假设下，建立环论纯无限简单与C*-代数纯无限简单之间的等价性。同时给出一个障碍定理（存在非零有限投影的一元C*-代数不含稠密的hfc-封闭纯无限简单一元子环），并以Cuntz代数上规范作用的子代数为例验证假设的成立性。

Result: 在A具有实秩零且A₀在全纯函数演算下封闭的假设下，A₀作为环是纯无限简单的当且仅当A作为C*-代数是纯无限简单的；一元C*-代数若有非零有限投影，则不含稠密的hfc-封闭纯无限简单一元子环；Cuntz代数O_n的光滑子代数O_n^∞是适当稠密的hfc-封闭子代数，且作为环是纯无限简单的。

Conclusion: 作者在实秩零和全纯函数演算封闭这两个自然假设下解决了纯无限性传递问题，同时通过障碍定理刻画了问题失败的情形，并通过O_n的具体例子表明该假设在实际中是成立的。

Abstract: Problem 8.4 of Pino, Goodearl, Perera and Molina in [arxiv.org/abs/0806.4156] asks whether a dense subalgebra $A_{0}$ of a $C^*$-algebra $A$ that is purely infinite as a ring forces $A$ to be purely infinite as a $C^*$-algebra. We call this the \emph{pure infiniteness transfer problem}, the transfer being from the ring to its $C^*$-completion. The problem is open, even when $A_{0}$ is unital and simple. We settle it under two hypotheses: $A$ has real rank zero, and $A_{0}$ is closed under holomorphic functional calculus. Under these hypotheses $A_{0}$ is purely infinite simple as a ring if and only if $A$ is purely infinite simple as a $C^*$-algebra. We also prove an obstruction: a unital $C^*$-algebra with a nonzero finite projection has no dense hfc-closed purely infinite simple unital subring. Finally, the hypotheses hold for proper subalgebras, for the gauge action of $\mathbb{T}$ on a Cuntz algebra $\mathcal{O}_n$, the smooth subalgebra $\mathcal{O}_n^\infty$ is a proper dense hfc-closed subalgebra that is purely infinite simple as a ring.

</details>


### [154] [Obstructions to the full Hao-Ng isomorphism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31050)
*Adam Dor-On,Ian Thompson*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文证明Hao-Ng同构在全交叉积的情况下不成立，并针对C*-对应X上的广义规范作用G↷X，确定了三个结构障碍，它们阻碍了全交叉积与Cuntz-Pimsner C*-代数之间的典范交换关系O_X⋊G ≅ O_{X⋊G}成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hao-Ng同构为Cuntz-Pimsner代数与交叉积之间的典范交换提供了理论依据，但作者发现该同构在全交叉积意义下并非普遍成立。识别阻碍该同构成立的结构条件，对于理解C*-对应、规范作用与交叉积之间的深层关系具有重要意义。

Method: 作者通过系统分析，将阻碍Hao-Ng同构成立的因素归结为三个结构性障碍：(1) 超刚性（hyperrigidity）的缺失；(2) 系数C*-代数A不满足WEP（弱期望性质）；(3) 群G在A上的作用的全交叉积与约化交叉积不一致。

Result: 成功隔离出三个独立的障碍条件，它们分别从超刚性、WEP以及全/约化交叉积的一致性三个方面，阻止了Hao-Ng同构在全交叉积框架下的成立。

Conclusion: Hao-Ng同构并非无条件成立，其成立需要额外假设，例如超刚性条件、系数代数的WEP，以及全交叉积与约化交叉积的一致性等结构性条件。

Abstract: We show that the Hao-Ng isomorphism for full crossed products fails to hold. More precisely, for a non-degenerate $C^*$-correspondence $X$ over a $C^*$-algebra $\mathcal{A}$ and a generalized gauge action $G \curvearrowright X$ by a locally compact group $G$, we isolate three structural obstructions for the canonical commutation $\mathcal{O}_X\rtimes G \cong \mathcal{O}_{X\rtimes G}$ between full crossed product and Cuntz--Pimsner $C^*$-algebra. The first concerns the lack of hyperrigidity, the second is the lack of WEP of the coefficient $C^*$-algebra $\mathcal{A}$, and the third concerns the lack of coincidence of full and reduced crossed products by the action of $G$ on $\mathcal{A}$.

</details>


<div id='math.FA'></div>

# math.FA [[Back]](#toc)

### [155] [A Proof of the Matrix Spencer Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28816)
*Emrullah Akbas,Suvrit Sra*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于矩阵小球估计的新方法来解决矩阵不一致性问题，通过行列式权重缩放高斯测度的配分函数，并利用矩阵加权Poincaré不等式获得无维度常数，最终解决了Matrix Spencer猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矩阵Spencer猜想是经典Spencer定理的矩阵推广，此前尚未被解决。该问题在计算机科学和数学领域具有重要应用意义，需要一个统一的框架来证明矩阵着色问题的期望界。

Method: 作者提出了一种新方法：(1) 使用从log-barrier获得的行列式权重来缩放矩阵小球概率，形成一个高斯测度的倾斜配分函数；(2) 利用矩阵加权Poincaré不等式将此配分函数与矩阵的压缩（pinched）或对角部分进行比较，从而获得无维度常数；(3) 该方法得到高斯级数的遗传性（hereditary）小球估计。

Result: 该方法解决了Matrix Spencer猜想：对于满足范数界||A_i||≤1的对称n×n矩阵A_1,...,A_n，可以高效地找到着色x∈{±1}^n使得||Σ_{i=1}^n x_iA_i||=O(√n)。此外还得到高斯级数的遗传性小球估计，具有独立的研究价值。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一个通用的矩阵小球估计框架，通过log-barrier权重与Poincaré不等式的结合，成功解决了长期悬而未决的Matrix Spencer猜想，展示了该方法在矩阵集中不等式领域的强大潜力。

Abstract: We develop a novel approach to matrix discrepancy based on matrix small-ball estimates. Specifically, we use a determinantal weight (obtained from the log-barrier) to scale the small-ball probability into a partition function of a tilt of the Gaussian measure. We then employ matrix-weighted Poincaré inequalities to compare this partition function to that of a pinched or diagonal part of the matrix, obtaining \emph{dimension-free} constants. Our technique yields a hereditary small-ball estimate for Gaussian series that should be of independent interest. As the main application, we resolve the Matrix Spencer conjecture: for symmetric $n\times n$ matrices $A_1,\dots,A_n$ with $\|A_i\|\le1$, one can efficiently find a coloring $x\in\{\pm1\}^n$ with $\|\sum_{i=1}^n x_iA_i\|=O(\sqrt n)$.

</details>


### [156] [Transfinite Schreier families and cardinal invariants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28900)
*Kevin Beanland,Christian Rosendal*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文研究传递Schreier层次结构对可数极限序数基本序列选择方式的依赖性。作者引入区间端点函数，证明：当且仅当基本序列可选取使得这些端点函数在最终支配序下构成无界族时，有界数等于ω₁（此时层次结构满足Shiliaev的尾覆盖性质）。同时证明：当且仅当基本序列可选取使得每个紧致的有限子集族（来自{2,3,…}）包含于某个Schreier族中时，支配数等于ω₁，后者结合了Fremlin关于有理数紧子集的共尾性定理与紧族的吸收构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究传递Schreier层次结构（描述序数α的Schreier族）如何依赖于基本序列的选择，并揭示该依赖性与基数不变量（有界数、支配数）之间的深层联系，从而将描述集合论、组合集论中的经典不变量与超限递归结构的构造性选择关联起来。

Method: 通过为每个Schreier族关联区间端点函数，利用最终支配序下的无界族刻画有界数等于ω₁的条件（等价于尾覆盖性质或无限紧区间族交条件）；利用Fremlin的共尾性定理结合紧族的吸收构造，刻画支配数等于ω₁的条件。

Result: 给出了有界数和支配数等于ω₁的精确刻画：前者等价于端点函数形成最终支配序下的无界族（即层次结构满足尾覆盖性质），后者等价于基本序列可使每个紧致有限子集族被某个Schreier族吸收包含。

Conclusion: Schreier层次结构的基数不变量性质本质上由基本序列的选择决定，其刻画分别与无界性和紧族吸收性相联系，确立了超限组合结构与经典基数不变量之间的精确等价关系。

Abstract: We study the dependence of the transfinite Schreier hierarchy on the choice of fundamental sequences for the countable limit ordinals. With each Schreier family we associate an interval endpoint function. We prove that the bounding number is equal to $ω_1$ exactly when the fundamental sequences may be chosen so that these endpoint functions form an unbounded family in the eventual domination order. Equivalently, the hierarchy then satisfies the tail-covering property isolated by Shiliaev, or every infinite compact interval family has infinite intersection with some member of the hierarchy. We also prove that the dominating number is equal to $ω_1$ exactly when the fundamental sequences may be chosen so that every compact family of finite subsets of $\{2,3,\ldots\}$ is contained in one Schreier family. The latter equivalence combines Fremlin's cofinality theorem for compact subsets of the rationals with an absorption construction for compact families.

</details>


### [157] [Mordukhovich Derivatives and Covering Constant for Set-Valued Metric Projection in General Banach Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28961)
*Jinlu Li*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文给出了从l1空间到其单位闭球的集值度量投影算子的显式表示，并利用该表示研究了其Mordukhovich导数的性质，进而计算了其覆盖常数。作为特例，在2维Banach空间中求得了该投影算子的显式解，并详细计算了其Mordukhovich导数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集值度量投影算子在近似理论、最优化和变分分析中具有重要地位，但其显式表示和Mordukhovich导数的计算在l1空间中尚未得到充分研究。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 首先推导从l1到其单位闭球的集值度量投影算子的显式表示；基于该表示分析Mordukhovich导数的性质并计算覆盖常数；最后在2维Banach空间中给出投影算子的显式解，并详细计算其Mordukhovich导数。

Result: 获得了l1空间中集值度量投影算子的显式表示及其Mordukhovich导数的完整性质，计算出了覆盖常数；在2维Banach空间中得到了投影算子解的显式形式及详细的Mordukhovich导数计算结果。

Conclusion: 本文通过显式表示方法成功刻画了l1空间中集值度量投影算子及其Mordukhovich导数，为相关优化问题和变分分析研究提供了理论基础和具体计算工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we find the explicit representation of the set-valued metric projection operator from l1 to the unit closed ball in l1. By using this representation, we investigate the properties of the Mordukhovich derivatives of the set-valued metric projection operator, which is applied to calculate its covering constant. As a special case, we consider the 2-d Banach space. We find the explicit solutions of the set-valued metric projection operator from the considered 2-d Banach space to its unit closed ball. By these solutions, we will calculate its Mordukhovich derivatives in details.

</details>


### [158] [A note on a sparse sampling conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29217)
*Oleg Asipchuk,Sinai Robins*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文推翻了第二作者早先关于凸中心对称体在傅里叶变换在Z^d上一致时必相差刚体运动的猜想，在维度2和3中也构造了反例；同时证明了一个更强的正面结果：对任意满秩格L及满足无模L同余点对的连通凸体有限并集P和Q，若其示性函数的傅里叶变换在格L*上一致，则Q=P+ℓ（ℓ∈L）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期猜想认为两个正测度的凸中心对称体若其示性函数的傅里叶变换在Z^d格上一致，且二者均非多重铺砌体，则它们必相差一个刚体运动。第一作者已在d≥4时构造反例表明Z^d格可能过于稀疏，但d=2,3的情况仍悬而未决。本文旨在解决这一剩余维数的问题，并同时寻求一个更稳健的正面结果。

Method: 作者首先在维度2和3中构造反例，证明原猜想在这些维度下不成立。随后，他们放弃中心对称性和非多重铺砌假设，转而证明一个更一般化的正面定理：在满秩格L下，若P和Q均为连通凸体有限并集且各自内部任意两个不同点模L不同余（即P-P与Q-Q不与L\{0}相交），则傅里叶变换在L*上一致可推出Q恰好是P的某个格平移。

Result: 在维度2和3中构造了原猜想的反例，表明该猜想在所有维度（d≥2）下均为假；同时证明了更强的正面定理：在连通性、无模格同余对及满秩格条件下，傅里叶变换在格L*上的一致足以推出两集合相差一个格平移。特别地，若两集合均关于原点中心对称，则可直接推出P=Q。

Conclusion: 原猜想在维度2和3下同样不成立，因此它在所有维度上都是假的；但通过弱化条件（移除中心对称与非多重铺砌假设）并加强集合的一般性（允许连通凸体有限并集），可以在满秩格框架下获得一个肯定的平移结论，这为格傅里叶唯一性问题提供了一个更清晰的正面刻画。

Abstract: A conjecture made by the second author was that two convex, centrally symmetric bodies of positive measure which are not multi-tilers must agree up to a rigid motion whenever the Fourier transforms of their indicator functions agree on \(\Z^d\). Counterexamples were constructed by the first author in dimensions \(d\geq4\), showing that the lattice \(\Z^d\) can be too sparse. Here we show that the initial conjecture is also false in the remaining dimensions \(2\) and \(3\).
  However, we prove a related positive result, with a stronger conclusion and without either central symmetry or the non-multitiling assumption. Let \(\mathcal L\subset\R^d\) be a full-rank lattice, and let \(\mathcal P,Q\subset\R^d\) be connected finite unions of convex bodies such that no two distinct points of either set are congruent modulo \(\mathcal L\). If the Fourier transforms of their indicator functions agree on the dual lattice \(\mathcal L^*\), then \(Q=\mathcal P+\ell\) for some \(\ell\in \mathcal L\). In particular, if both sets are symmetric about the origin, then \(\mathcal P=Q\).

</details>


### [159] [Numerical range and invariant subspaces of weighted composition operators on the Hardy space of Dirichlet series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29294)
*Sudeshna Lahiri,Sarita Ojha,Subhadip Halder,Riddhick Birbonshi*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文研究了Dirichlet级数Hardy空间上加权复合算子的数值域，给出了数值域包含零点和圆盘/椭圆盘的条件，并利用不变子空间确定了可约加权复合算子的数值域，同时讨论了Davis-Wielandt壳的相关结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Dirichlet级数Hardy空间上加权复合算子的数值域，这是算子理论中一个重要的研究方向，此前在该空间上的数值域研究尚不充分，作者希望填补这一空白并回答随之产生的自然问题。

Method: 利用加权复合算子的结构性质，通过算子不变子空间的分析来确定可约算子的数值域；同时借助Davis-Wielandt壳这一工具扩展对复合算子谱性质的刻画。

Result: 给出了加权复合算子数值域包含零点和某些圆形/椭圆圆盘的条件；确定了可约加权复合算子的数值域；获得了关于Davis-Wielandt壳的若干结果。

Conclusion: 本文系统性地刻画了Dirichlet级数Hardy空间上加权复合算子的数值域及相关性质，为后续研究提供了基础，并提出了几个值得进一步探索的自然问题。

Abstract: In this paper, we study various results on numerical range of weighted composition operators on Hardy space of Dirichlet series. Certain conditions are provided under which the numerical range contains zero and some circular or elliptic disks. With the help of invariant subspaces of the operator, we also determine the numerical range of reductive weighted composition operators on this space. Additionally, we discuss some results on the Davis-Wielandt shell of composition operators with some natural questions that arise from our findings.

</details>


### [160] [On C*-Transitivity and generalized numerical transitivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29436)
*Stefan Ivkovic*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文受数值超循环概念的启发，在Banach代数上的算子中引入了两个新的线性动力学概念：广义数值传递性和C*-传递性，证明了它们彼此不同且与现有的动力学概念（如超循环性、超超循环性、弱超循环性等）有所区别，并给出了相关算子实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于数值超循环（numerical hypercyclicity）的概念，作者希望在线性动力学中引入新的传递性概念，以更深入地理解算子动力学的结构，并探究这些新概念与现有动力学概念之间的关系与差异。

Method: 在Banach代数上的算子框架下定义广义数值传递性和C*-传递性；同时将C*-传递性推广到Hilbert-Schmidt算子空间上的算子。通过构造具体算子例子来证明这些新概念之间的区别，以及与超循环性、超超循环性、弱超循环性、弱超超循环性和数值超循环性等标准概念的不同。

Result: 证明了广义数值传递性和C*-传递性彼此不同，且与标准的动力学概念不同。构造了数值超循环但既非C*-传递性也非广义数值传递性的算子；在可分Hilbert空间上的紧算子C*-代数上构造了非超超循环、非弱超循环的C*-传递性和广义数值传递性算子；在标准Hilbert C*-模上也构造了相应算子；并证明了存在弱超超循环但不是C*-传递性的算子。

Conclusion: 本文引入的广义数值传递性和C*-传递性是相互不同且独立于现有线性动力学概念的新动力学性质，丰富了算子动力学的理论框架，并为进一步研究数值型动力学行为提供了新的视角和工具。

Abstract: Motivated by the concept of numerical hypercyclicity, in this paper, we introduce two new notions in linear dynamics: generalized numerical transitivity and C*-transitivity. Both notions are formulated in the setting of operators on a Banach algebra, however, the concept of C*-transitivity is also applicable for operators on the space of Hilbert-Schmidt operators. We prove that these new notions are mutually different, and we also show that they differ from the standard notions in dynamics like hypercyclicity, supercyclicity, weak hypercyclicity and weak supercyclicity, numerical hypercyclicity. In particular, we provide examples of numerically hypercyclic operators that are neither C*-transitive nor generalized numerically transitive. Also, we construct non-supercyclic and non-weakly hypercyclic C*-transitive and generalized numerically transitive operators on the C*-algebra of compact operators on a separable Hilbert space, as well as C*-transitive and generalized numerically operators on the standard Hilbert C*-module. Finally, we argue that there exist weakly supercyclic operators that are not C*-transitive.

</details>


### [161] [Optimality in a Multilinear Extension of Kwapień's Theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29443)
*Luis Miguel Castaño Marín,Daniel Núñez-Alarcón,Geivison Ribeiro,Diana Serrano-Rodríguez*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文证明了Bayart、Pellegrino和Rueda关于连续m-线性算子绝对(r_{m,p};1)-求和性的最优指数在1≤p<2范围内的最优性，填补了此前仅知2≤p≤∞时的空白。证明采用有限维方法，基于F_2^d上的卷积和Walsh特征系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bayart等人证明了每个从(ℓ_1)^m到ℓ_p的连续m-线性算子都是绝对(r_{m,p};1)-求和的，但该指数的最优性仅在2≤p≤∞范围内得到验证。在剩余范围1≤p<2中，最优性尚未证明，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用有限维论证方法，利用F_2^d上的卷积结构和Walsh特征系统构造出所需的线性算子，以此展示Bayart等人给出的指数在1≤p<2范围内不能再改进。同一构造还可推广为局部障碍定理，适用于一致包含ℓ_p^n的赋范空间。

Result: 成功证明绝对求和指数的最优性在1≤p<2范围内成立；作为应用，恢复了L_1[0,1]-值映射的精确指数2/m，并证明cotype性质单独并不能确定最优绝对(r;1)-重合指数。

Conclusion: 本文通过有限维构造完整解决了Bayart等人数论中关于绝对求和指数最优性的遗留问题，并进一步揭示了该构造在局部障碍定理和重合指数判定中的广泛应用价值。

Abstract: Bayart, Pellegrino and Rueda proved that every continuous $m$-linear operator from $(\ell_1)^m$ into $\ell_p$ is absolutely $(r_{m,p};1)$-summing, for explicit exponents $r_{m,p}$. The optimality of these exponents was known for $2\le p\le\infty$. We prove optimality in the remaining range $1\le p<2$. Our argument is finite-dimensional and is based on convolution on $\mathbb F_2^d$ and the Walsh character system. The same construction yields a local obstruction theorem for range spaces containing $\ell_p^n$ uniformly. As applications, we recover the sharp exponent $2/m$ for $L_1[0,1]$-valued mappings and show that cotype alone does not determine the optimal absolute $(r;1)$-coincidence exponent.

</details>


### [162] [Spectral Rigidity of Commutators: Dynamics, Resonance, and Nilpotency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29574)
*Hranislav Stanković*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文研究了由A诱导的内导子Δ_A(T)=AT-TA作用于T时，在二阶关系Δ_A²(T)+αΔ_A(T)+βT=0（α,β∈ℝ）下的幂零性问题。结论取决于二次方程z²+αz+β的根的位置：当根具有同号非零实部时，T与Δ_A(T)均为幂零；当根为纯虚数且非零时，一般无幂零性结论；当一根为零另一根非零时，Δ_A(T)幂零但T不一定；双零根情形退化为Kleinecke–Shirokov定理。对于异号实根，结果由算术阈值p+q>n（其中z₁/z₂=-p/q为最简分数）决定。对任意阶关系Σc_k Δ_A^k(T)=0，当关联多项式根位于以原点为边界的开半平面时，所有迭代换位子Δ_A^j(T)均为幂零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索内导子Δ_A的幂零性传播规律，将经典的Kleinecke–Shirokov定理推广到更一般的二阶及高阶多项式关系中，并精确刻画在不同特征根下幂零性结论成立与否的边界条件。

Method: 通过分析特征多项式z²+αz+β的根在复平面中的位置来分类讨论幂零性问题。对异号实根情形，引入最简分数表示z₁/z₂=-p/q，利用p+q与矩阵阶数n的大小关系作为判定条件；对p+q≤n的情形，构造显式的循环（cyclic）反例使得T与Δ_A(T)均为可逆矩阵。高阶情形则利用开半平面条件进行推广。

Result: （1）根具有同号非零实部时，T与Δ_A(T)均幂零；（2）根为纯虚数非零时无一般幂零性结论；（3）一零一非零根时Δ_A(T)幂零但T未必幂零；（4）双零根对应Kleinecke–Shirokov定理；（5）异号实根时，p+q>n则所有解幂零，p+q≤n则存在显式构造使双方可逆；（6）任意阶关系下，若特征根位于以原点为边界的开半平面，则所有迭代换位子均幂零。

Conclusion: 本文完整刻画了内导子满足二阶及高阶多项式关系时幂零性的充要条件，建立了几何（根的位置）与算术（p+q与n的比较）紧密关联的分类标准，并推广了经典Kleinecke–Shirokov定理。

Abstract: Let $A, T \in M_n(\mathbb{C})$ and let $Δ_A(T) = AT - TA$ denote the inner derivation induced by $A$. We determine when $T$ and $Δ_A(T)$ are nilpotent under the second-order relation
  $$
  Δ_A^2(T) + α\, Δ_A(T) + β\, T = 0, \qquad α, β\in \mathbb{R},
  $$
  according to the location of the roots of $z^2 + αz + β$. If the roots have nonzero real parts of the same sign, then both $T$ and $Δ_A(T)$ are nilpotent. If the roots are purely imaginary and nonzero, no nilpotency conclusion holds in general, whereas if one root is zero and the other is nonzero, $Δ_A(T)$ is nilpotent but $T$ need not be. The double zero root gives the Kleinecke--Shirokov theorem. For real roots of opposite signs, the answer is governed by an arithmetic threshold: writing $z_1/z_2=-p/q$ in lowest terms, every solution is nilpotent when $p+q>n$, while for $p+q\le n$ an explicit cyclic construction yields solutions for which both $T$ and $Δ_A(T)$ are invertible. Finally, for a relation
  $$
  \sum_{k=0}^{m} c_k\,Δ_A^k(T)=0
  $$
  of arbitrary order, every iterated commutator $Δ_A^j(T)$ is nilpotent whenever the roots of the associated polynomial lie in an open half-plane whose boundary passes through the origin.

</details>


### [163] [The Beurling density of the spectrum of self-similar measure generated by Hadamard triple](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29660)
*Zong-Sheng Liu,Xiao-Yu Yan*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文研究自相似测度μ的谱的Beurling维数与Beurling密度之间的自由灵活性。作者证明：对任意t∈(0, log q/log b)和s∈[0,∞]，存在μ的谱Λ使得其Beurling维数恰为t且t-Beurling密度恰为s。该结果推广了Lu的先前工作，回答了Dai、Fu和He提出的公开问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Hadamard三元组(b,D,L)条件下，已知自相似测度μ的谱的Beurling维数具有中间结构性质。已有研究（如Lu的工作）表明Beurling维数与Beurling密度可分别调节，但尚不清楚二者能否同时自由选取。Dai、Fu和He在文献中提出了相关猜想（Conjecture 5.3），本文旨在证明Beurling维数与Beurling密度可同时实现完全灵活性。

Method: 作者构造了一族参数化谱Λ_{t,s}，其中t控制Beurling维数、s控制t-Beurling密度。利用Hadamard三元组结构的谱自相似性，通过精细的谱构造技术同时调节维数与密度参数，并利用基数论证证明满足条件的谱集合具有连续统基数。

Result: 对任意t∈(0, log q/log b)和s∈[0,∞]，均存在μ的谱Λ使得dim_Be(Λ)=t且D_t^+(Λ)=s，即Beurling维数与Beurling密度可同时任意指定。进一步证明了满足该条件的谱集合的基数为连续统。

Conclusion: 本文结果推广了Lu的先前结果，正面回答了Dai、Fu和He提出的公开猜想，揭示了奇异连续谱测度的谱在维数与密度维度上的精细结构性质，表明二者可以完全独立地自由调节。

Abstract: Let $D\subset\Bbb Z, q, b\in \Bbb Z$ with $q<b$, and let $μ:=μ_{b,D}$ be the associated self-similar measure. It is well known that if there exists $L\subset \Bbb Z$ such that $(b,D,L)$ be a Hadamard triple, then the Beurling dimension of the spectrum of $μ$ exhibits an intermediate structural property. In this paper, we establish a stronger result that both Beurling dimension and Beurling density of the spectra of $μ$ can achieve full flexibility simultaneously. More precisely, for any $t\in(0, \frac{\log q}{\log b})$ and $s\in [0,\infty]$, there exists a spectrum $Λ:=Λ_{t,s}$ of $μ$ such that $$\dim_{Be}(Λ)=t,\quad D_t^+ (Λ)=s.$$ Here, $\dim_{Be}$ and $D_t^+$ denote the Beurling dimension and the $t$-Beurling density, respectively. We further prove that the set of such spectrum whose Beurling dimension and Beurling density are equal to any fixed $t$ and $s$ has the cardinality of the continuum. \par This work generalizes a previous result of Lu \cite{Lu}, answers an open question raised by Dai, Fu and He \cite[Conjecture 5.3]{DaiFuHe}, and sheds new light on the fine structural properties of spectra for singularly continuous spectral measures.

</details>


### [164] [Singular inner eigenfunctions of composition operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29722)
*V. A. Anjali,P. Muthukumar,P. Shankar*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文刻画了由离散测度产生的复合算子 C_φ 的所有奇异内特征函数，其中 φ 是单位圆盘的共形自同构。通过建立 Beurling 不变子空间与模型不变子空间之间的联系，分类了所有使得对应 Beurling 子空间在非椭圆自同构诱导的复合算子下不变的内函数，并给出了该联系的一些应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复合算子的不变子空间和内特征函数结构是算子理论与函数空间理论中的核心问题。此前对复合算子的特征函数研究不够完整，特别是关于奇异内函数的情形。本文旨在通过 Beurling 与模型不变子空间之间的桥梁，系统分类奇异内特征函数，填补这一研究空白。

Method: 本文首先建立 Beurling 不变子空间与模型不变子空间之间的对应关系，然后利用这一对应将复合算子的不变性问题转化为模型子空间的求解问题。具体地，通过求解复合算子的特征函数方程，对非椭圆自同构（包括双曲型和抛物型）诱导的复合算子进行分类，刻画所有使得 Beurling 子空间不变的奇异内函数。

Result: 完全刻画了由离散测度产生的所有奇异内特征函数；在非椭圆自同构的情形下，分类了所有使对应 Beurling 子空间在复合算子下保持不变的奇异内函数，并通过求解特征函数方程给出了显式分类。

Conclusion: Beurling 与模型不变子空间之间的联系为研究复合算子的不变子空间提供了有效工具，据此完成了非椭圆自同构情形下奇异内特征函数的完整分类，该联系还可推广应用于其他相关算子理论问题。

Abstract: This paper characterizes all the singular inner eigenfunctions of the composition operators $C_φ$ that arise from discrete measures, when $φ$ is an automorphism of unit disk. By establishing a connection between Beurling and model invariant subspaces, we classify all the inner functions so that the corresponding Beurling subspace is invariant under the composition operators induced by non-elliptic automorphisms. This classification involves solving the eigenfunction equation for the composition operator. Further, we present some applications of the above-mentioned connection.

</details>


### [165] [Inertia-Sensitive Kreiss Bounds for $J$-Selfadjoint Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29823)
*Thanh Nguyen-Cung,Binh T. Nguyen*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文研究自伴随于不定度量（即J-自伴随）矩阵的幂增长（Power常数）与Kreiss常数之间的关系。经典Kreiss定理给出Power(A) ≤ en·Kreiss(A)，其中n为矩阵维数且线性依赖是必不可少的。本文证明，对于满足A*J = JA（J为惯性为(n-q,q)的基本对称矩阵）的矩阵，可将n替换为有效维度min{d(A), 2q+1, 2(n-q)+1}，得到改进的Kreiss型不等式。证明不需假设可对角化或谱为实数，而是将每个循环轨道与有限秩自伴随Hankel算子联系起来。文中还展示了惯性指数较小时界是渐近最优的，并推广到任意非奇异Hermitian度量的加权范数情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的Krein定理给出所有矩阵上的一致性Kreiss不等式，但上界依赖于矩阵维数n。对于一般矩阵，这种线性依赖不可消除。然而，对于具有特殊结构（如J-自伴随）的矩阵，人们期望利用其额外对称性获得更精细的估计，用反映问题本质的有效维度取代环境维度n。

Method: 该方法的关键创新在于：将每个循环轨道（cyclic orbit）与一个有限秩自伴随Hankel算子相关联，然后将该算子的秩和惯性转化为系数估计。利用J-自伴随条件A*J = JA和Hankel算子的结构性质，替换经典Kreiss定理证明中对满秩的依赖，得到以有效维度min{d(A), 2q+1, 2(n-q)+1}为系数的界。此外，还利用缩放nilpotent移位构造示例证明界的渐近最优性。

Result: 主要结果为：对于满足A*J = JA的矩阵A（J为惯性为(n-q,q)的基本对称矩阵），有Power(A) ≤ e·min{d(A), 2q+1, 2(n-q)+1}·Kreiss(A)，其中d(A)为最小多项式的次数。该界不依赖于可对角化假设或谱的实性。通过基于缩放nilpotent移位的例子，证明了当较小惯性指数相对矩阵规模可忽略（且d(A)=n）时，对较小惯性指数的线性依赖是渐近最优的。文章还获得缩放圆盘衰减估计及到任意非奇异Hermitian度量的加权范数推广。

Conclusion: 本文建立了J-自伴随矩阵的Kreiss定理精细版本，用由最小多项式和度量惯性决定的有效维度取代环境维度n，扩展了经典理论的适用范围。该结果对研究不定度量空间上的动力系统暂态放大现象具有理论意义，其渐近最优性说明这类矩阵族上的界本质上由较小惯性指数控制。

Abstract: Let $A \in \mathbb{C}^{n \times n}$ have spectrum in the closed unit disk. Its maximal power growth $\operatorname{Power}(A) := \sup_{k \ge 0} \Vert{}A^k\Vert{}$ measures transient amplification, whereas the Kreiss constant $\operatorname{Kreiss}(A) := \sup_{\vert{}z\vert{}>1} (\vert{}z\vert{}-1) \Vert{}(zI-A)^{-1}\Vert{}$ measures the corresponding resolvent growth outside the disk. The classical finite-dimensional Kreiss theorem gives $\operatorname{Power}(A) \le en \operatorname{Kreiss}(A)$, and the linear dependence on $n$ is unavoidable for general matrices. We show that, for matrices selfadjoint with respect to an indefinite metric, the ambient dimension $n$ can be replaced by an effective dimension determined by the minimal polynomial and the inertia of the metric. Specifically, if $A^*J = JA$, where $J$ is a fundamental symmetry with inertia $(n-q,q)$, then $\operatorname{Power}(A) \le e \min\{d(A), 2q+1, 2(n-q)+1\} \operatorname{Kreiss}(A)$, where $d(A)$ is the degree of the minimal polynomial. Our proof requires no assumption on diagonalizability or reality on the spectrum. Instead, we associate each cyclic orbit with a finite-rank selfadjoint Hankel operator and transfer its rank and inertia to a coefficient estimate. Examples based on scaled nilpotent shifts show that the linear dependence on the smaller inertia index is asymptotically sharp, even when this index is negligible relative to the matrix size and $d(A)=n$. We also obtain scaled-disk decay estimates and weighted-norm extensions to arbitrary nonsingular Hermitian metrics.

</details>


### [166] [Graphs of operators as points in the Grassmann manifold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30120)
*Esteban Andruchow,Lazaro Recht,Alejandro Varela*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文研究希尔伯特空间中闭稠定算子图像在Grassmann流形中的几何性质，证明有界算子图像构成以零算子图像为中心的开放单位球，并与B(H)微分同胚；无界闭算子图像位于边界。同时研究两图之间的极小测地线存在性及特征、紧算子与受限Grassmannian的关系，以及公共补的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将闭稠定算子与其图建立联系，把算子理论问题转化为Grassmann流形上的几何问题，从而利用无穷维流形的几何工具（如测地线、距离结构）来研究算子对的度量性质、公共补的存在性等基础问题。

Method: 利用Grassmann流形P(H×H)中正交投影的几何结构，将有界算子的图映射为投影算子P_T，证明该映射给出到开单位球的微分同胚。对无界算子，利用闭图像定理和边界分析研究其在流形中的位置。对于测地线问题，采用五空间分解（five-space decomposition）构造自伴算子对之间的显式测地线，并通过低维例子验证测地线是否保持在图集Γ内。

Result: 有界算子图像集合Γ^b是P(H×H)中以P_0为中心的开放单位球，与B(H)微分同胚；无界闭算子图像位于边界。自伴算子对的测地线总存在，且可通过五空间分解显式构造；但低维例子表明连接两图的测地线不必完全由图像构成。对紧算子与受限Grassmannian的关系及公共补问题给出了若干正性结果。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了算子理论与无穷维几何之间的桥梁，揭示了有界算子与无界算子在流形结构上的本质差异，并为算子对的测地线理论和公共补问题提供了新的几何视角和工具。

Abstract: We study the set $Γ$ of graphs of closed, densely defined operators in a Hilbert space $H$, regarded as a subset of the Grassmann manifold $P(H\times H)$ of orthogonal projections in $H\times H$. We show that the subset $Γ^b$ of graphs of bounded operators is the open unit ball of $P(H\times H)$ centered at the graph of the zero operator $P_0$ (which projects onto $H\times\{0\}$). This ball is diffeomorphic to $B(H)$ via the map $T\mapsto P_T$ ($=$ the projection onto the graph ${Gr(T)}$ of $T$). We show that graphs of unbounded closed operators lie at the boundary of $Γ$. We also study the existence and characteristics of minimal geodesics of $P(H\times H)$ joining two graphs $Gr(A)$, $Gr(B)$. If $A,B$ are selfadjoint, such a geodesic always exists, and we construct explicitly a distinguished exponent using the five-space decomposition associated to the pair of subspaces $Gr(A)$, $Gr(B)$. An explicit low-dimensional example shows that the geodesic joining two graphs need not remain inside $Γ$, i.e., does not consist entirely of graphs. We also relate graphs of compact operators to the restricted Grassmannian, and study the problem of common complements for pairs $Gr(S)$, $Gr(T)$, giving positive results when one operator is bounded or under lower boundedness conditions.

</details>


### [167] [The Pego Theorem for the Hilbert--Schmidt Class](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29976)
*Yaogan Mensah*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文在一般局部紧阿贝尔相空间的量子调和分析框架下，建立了Pego紧性定理的算子理论版本，证明了一个有界Hilbert-Schmidt算子集合是预紧的当且仅当它在相空间平移下一致等度连续且其Fourier-Weyl变换在對偶相空间上一致等度连续，并给出了量子物理中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典的Pego紧性定理推广到算子理论框架，借助量子调和分析在一般局部紧阿贝尔相空间上建立算子集合的紧性判别准则，为量子物理中的分析问题提供数学基础。

Method: 利用量子调和分析工具，在一般局部紧阿贝尔相空间上定义相空间平移作用与Fourier-Weyl变换，通过建立两个方向的等价性来证明算子集合预紧性的充要条件，并应用该结果到量子物理语境。

Result: 证明了有界Hilbert-Schmidt算子集在相空间平移下的一致等度连续性以及其Fourier-Weyl变换在對偶相空间上的一致等度连续性共同构成预紧性的充要条件。

Conclusion: 该结果推广了经典Pego紧性定理到算子理论范畴，为量子物理中算子集合的紧性与收敛性分析提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: This paper establishes an operator-theoretic version of Pego's compactness theorem within the framework of quantum harmonic analysis on general locally compact abelian phase spaces. We show that a bounded set of Hilbert-Schmidt operators is precompact if and only if it is uniformly equicontinuous under phase-space shifts and its Fourier-Weyl transform is uniformly equicontinuous on the dual phase space. We provide applications to quantum physics.

</details>


### [168] [Three-term asymptotics for discrete Hardy--Rellich constants in high dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30888)
*Carlos Lizama*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文确定了离散Hardy–Rellich不等式最优常数C_ℓ(N)在高维情形下的前三阶渐近展开：C_ℓ(N)=2^ℓ N^ℓ+γ_ℓ N^{ℓ-1}+δ_ℓ N^{ℓ-2}+O_ℓ(N^{ℓ-3})，给出了显式系数γ_ℓ和δ_ℓ，解决了随维数增长而出现的简并性问题，并特别得到C_1(N)=2N-4-20/(3N)+O(N^{-2})。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前已有独立工作确定了离散Hardy–Rellich常数C_ℓ(N)在维数N→∞时的主导阶行为2^ℓ N^ℓ，但更高阶的修正项（反映临界逆幂势的谱阈性质）尚属未知。确定下一阶甚至下下阶系数对于理解该问题的精细谱结构至关重要，且中心困难——共轭后leading operator在原点2N个最近邻上的简并随维数增长——是此前方法无法克服的障碍。

Method: 通过符号置换对称性(signed-permutation symmetry)解析简并簇，将问题约化为定义在四个晶格轨道(lattice orbits)上的有效算子；结合加权环面估计(weighted torus estimates)与Feshbach–Schur约化技术，将有限维展开嵌入全算子框架中；利用残差界(residual bound)与Temple不等式控制高阶项，最终得到带显式系数的渐近展开。

Result: 证明了C_ℓ(N)=2^ℓ N^ℓ+γ_ℓ N^{ℓ-1}+δ_ℓ N^{ℓ-2}+O_ℓ(N^{ℓ-3})，其中γ_ℓ和δ_ℓ具有显式表达式。特别地，当ℓ=1时得到C_1(N)=2N-4-20/(3N)+O(N^{-2})，展示了前两个修正项的精确结构。

Conclusion: 该结果完整刻画了离散Hardy–Rellich常数在高维极限下的前二阶修正，揭示了临界逆幂势算子的谱阈与最优常数之间的深层联系，为更高阶展开或相关离散不等式的研究提供了方法论基础。

Abstract: Sharp Hardy--Rellich constants are spectral thresholds for operators with critical inverse-power potentials. Let $C_\ell(N)$ denote the optimal constant in the $\ell$th-order discrete Hardy--Rellich inequality on $\Z^N$. Recent independent work established the leading high-dimensional behavior $C_\ell(N)\sim2^\ell N^\ell$ for every fixed $\ell$. We determine the next two orders and prove \[
  C_\ell(N)=2^\ell N^\ell+γ_\ell N^{\ell-1}
  +δ_\ell N^{\ell-2}+O_\ell(N^{\ell-3}), \] with explicit coefficients $γ_\ell$ and $δ_\ell$. The central difficulty is a degeneracy that grows with the dimension: after conjugation, the leading operator is scalar on the $2N$ nearest neighbours of the origin. We resolve this cluster using signed-permutation symmetry and an effective operator on four lattice orbits. Weighted torus estimates and Feshbach--Schur reduction justify the finite-dimensional expansion inside the full operator, while a residual bound and Temple's inequality give the stated remainder. In particular, $C_1(N)=2N-4-20/(3N)+O(N^{-2})$.

</details>


### [169] [The Daugavet property in symmetric operator spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30491)
*Jinghao Huang,Yerlan Nessipbayev,Fedor Sukochev,Ran Xu*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文在温和假设下，刻画了具有Daugavet性质的、隶属于半有限无原子von Neumann代数的实或复对称算子空间，并证明其等价于L₁或原代数本身（等价范数或成比例范数）。结果对复数对称函数空间也是新的，扩展了此前关于实数情形的相关定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前已有工作研究了实数对称函数空间的Daugavet性质（如AKM12、KMMW13、AKM15），但该问题尚未在一般算子空间框架下（尤其是复数情形）得到完整刻画。作者旨在将Daugavet性质的分类从函数空间推广到算子空间，并覆盖复数情形。

Method: 作者在温和假设下，通过分析半有限无原子von Neumann代数上的对称算子空间，利用Daugavet性质的几何刻画，结合算子空间结构与范数性质，推导出满足该性质的空间必须同构（同等价范数或成比例范数）于L₁(ℳ,τ)或ℳ本身。

Result: 证明了具有Daugavet性质的实或复对称算子空间（隶属于半有限无原子von Neumann代数）本质上只能是L₁(ℳ,τ)或ℳ（至多相差等价范数或成比例范数）。该结果对复数对称函数空间亦为新的，并推广了此前关于实数对称函数空间的结论。

Conclusion: 本文给出了半有限无原子von Neumann代数框架下对称算子空间的Daugavet性质的完全刻画，填补了复数情形的空白，并将已有实数函数空间的结果推广至更一般的算子空间设定。

Abstract: Under mild assumptions, the main results of this paper characterize a (real or complex) symmetric operator space affiliated with a semi-finite atomless von Neumann algebra $(\mathcal M, τ)$ possessing the Daugavet property as $L_1(\mathcal M,τ)$ or $\mathcal M$ (up to equivalent norms, or even proportional norms). Our results are new even for complex symmetric function spaces, which extend results of \cite{AKM12, KMMW13, AKM15} concerning the Daugavet property for real symmetric function spaces.

</details>


### [170] [Strong law of large numbers for generalized operator means II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30554)
*Zoltán Léka,Miklós Pálfia*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文继续研究大数强定律（SLLN）向算子均值的推广，修正了先前论文中关于Thompson度量空间中一致指数收缩流对应均值的大数强定律的陈述与证明中的不准确之处，并将算子均值大数强定律所需的矩条件从限制性条件放宽为更一般的L log⁺(L)条件，通过证明在该条件下单调算子的隐式随机逼近几乎必然收敛来实现这一改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前的论文提出了广义算子均值的大数强定律，但包含两方面的不足：一是关于Thompson度量空间中一致指数收缩流对应均值的大数强定律陈述和证明存在不准确之处；二是算子均值版本的大数强定律所需的矩条件过于严格。因此需要修正前者的错误，并放宽后者所需的矩条件以增强该定理的适用范围和理论完备性。

Method: 本文首先对前作中涉及一致指数收缩流在Thompson度量空间中的大数强定律的陈述与证明进行了澄清与修正。随后，通过证明单调算子在L log⁺(L)矩条件下的隐式随机逼近（implicit stochastic approximations）的几乎必然收敛性，将大数强定律的矩条件从原先的限制性条件显著放宽到L log⁺(L)，从而改进算子均值版本的强律。

Result: 成功修正了前作中关于Thompson度量空间一致指数收缩流均值大数强定律的陈述与证明错误，并显著改进了算子均值的大数强定律变体，将所需的矩条件放宽至L log⁺(L)，同时保证了单调算子隐式随机逼近的几乎必然收敛性。

Conclusion: 本文不仅修复了先前工作中的技术缺陷，还通过引入L log⁺(L)条件下单调算子隐式随机逼近几乎必然收敛的结果，大幅拓广了大数强定律在算子均值领域的适用范围，为该方向的后续研究奠定了更坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: In this paper we continue the investigation of generalizations of the strong law of large numbers to operator means which has been initiated in the earlier paper "Strong law of large numbers for generalized operator means", Adv. Math. 457 (2024), 109933 of the authors. In particular, we clarify an inaccuracy in the statement and proof of the strong law for means associated with uniformly exponentially contracting flows in Thompson metric spaces. We then significantly improve the variant of this strong law given in the mentioned paper for operator means by relaxing the restrictive required moment condition to $L\log^+(L)$ by proving almost sure convergence of implicit stochastic approximations for monotone operators under this condition.

</details>


### [171] [Morita Induction and Geometric Ideals in $\ell^p$ Roe-Type Algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30380)
*Yeong Chyuan Chung,Xinhui Du*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文研究了Paravicini意义下Morita等价的Banach代数之间的理想对应关系，并应用于ℓ^p一致Roe代数与ℓ^p一致代数之间的几何理想格刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在算子代数理论中，Morita等价是研究两个代数结构之间对应关系的核心工具，但此前对于带有有界逼近恒等元的Banach代数在Paravicini意义下Morita等价时，其闭双边理想之间如何对应尚不清楚，特别是在ℓ^p一致Roe代数的几何应用背景下。

Method: 作者首先描述了Morita等价Banach代数之间闭双边理想的诱导对应关系，刻画了对应理想何时是Morita等价的，并证明对应商代数是Morita等价的；其次证明Morita诱导保持由相容稠密代数核定义的几何理想，并给出识别对应理想的局部化子模判据；最后将这些结果应用于ℓ^p一致Roe代数B_u^p(X)与ℓ^p一致代数UB^p(X)之间的Morita等价，并证明UB^p(X)具有由投影构成的有界逼近恒等元。

Result: 成功建立了Morita等价Banach代数之间闭双边理想的完全对应理论，证明了对应商代数的Morita等价性以及几何理想的保性；在具体应用中，通过证明UB^p(X)具有投影构成的有界逼近恒等元这一技术性结论，实现了两个代数几何理想格的相互对应，从而将UB^p(X)的几何理想与有界粗结构的理想一一对应起来。

Conclusion: 本文系统发展了带近似恒等元的Banach代数在Paravicini Morita等价下的理想对应理论，并通过对ℓ^p一致Roe代数与ℓ^p一致代数的应用，建立了几何理想与粗几何结构之间深层联系，为ℓ^p空间的粗几何分析提供了新的代数工具。

Abstract: Given two Banach algebras with bounded approximate identities that are Morita equivalent in the sense of Paravicini, we describe the induced correspondence between their closed two-sided ideals, characterize when corresponding ideals are Morita equivalent, and show that corresponding quotients are always Morita equivalent. We also prove that Morita induction preserves geometric ideals defined by compatible dense algebraic cores and give a localized submodule criterion for identifying corresponding ideals.
  We apply these results to the Morita equivalence between the $\ell^p$ uniform Roe algebra $B_u^p(X)$ and the $\ell^p$ uniform algebra $UB^p(X)$ of a bounded geometry metric space. As a technical ingredient, we show that $UB^p(X)$ has a bounded approximate identity of projections. Morita induction then identifies the geometric ideal lattices of the two algebras, and hence the geometric ideals of $UB^p(X)$ with the ideals of the bounded coarse structure of $X$.

</details>


### [172] [Maximally Spread Out Measures and Implications for Phase Transitions in Approximation Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30549)
*Hannes Matt,Erwin Riegler,Felix Voigtlaender*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文证明在（拟）Banach空间的全有界子集上存在"最大分散"的Borel概率测度，该测度满足特定的小球条件，从而使得幂指数Hausdorff维数与下幂指数Minkowski维数一致，并推广了先前关于临界测度构造的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作表明临界测度（最大分散测度）对于无损压缩和量化神经网络的逼近具有相变意义，但先前仅在Besov和Sobolev空间的单位球（作为L²子集）情形下构造了这类测度。本文旨在提供一个完全一般的构造方法，适用于更广泛的（拟）Banach空间子集，包括支配混合光滑度的函数空间。

Method: 作者在以下两个温和条件下构造最大分散测度：(i) 覆盖数N(C,ε)满足下幂指数Minkowski维数s_*>0的增长条件；(ii) 一个技术性拓扑条件，该条件在C⊂X为闭、有界且凸时自动满足。构造出的Borel概率测度μ满足μ*(X\C)=0，且对所有0<s<s_*满足小球条件μ*(B(x,r))≤exp(-c(s)·(1/r)^s)。

Result: 建立了最大分散测度的一般存在性定理，由此得出幂指数Hausdorff维数与下幂指数Minkowski维数相等的结论，并将此前仅适用于特定Besov/Sobolev空间的临界测度构造推广到完全一般的情形，包括支配混合光滑度的函数空间。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个统一的框架来构造临界测度，其条件十分宽松（有界凸闭集即满足），从而将相变理论推广到更广阔的函数空间类别，为量化神经网络逼近和信息论压缩中的相变分析奠定了更坚实的基础。

Abstract: $\newcommand{\X}{\mathbb{X}}\newcommand{\SC}{\mathcal{C}}$ We establish the existence of a "maximally spread out" Borel probability measure on a totally bounded subset $\SC$ of a (quasi)-Banach space $\X$ under two mild conditions: (i) a growth condition on the covering numbers $N(\SC, ε)$ of $\SC$, and (ii) a technical topological condition that is in particular satisfied whenever $\SC\subset\X$ is closed, bounded, and convex.
  More formally, condition (i) requires that the so-called lower power-exponential Minkowski dimension of $\SC$, i.e., \[s_\ast:=\liminf_{ε\downarrow 0}\frac{\log\log N(\SC,ε)}{\log(1/ε)}\] satisfies $s_\ast>0$. Under these conditions, we construct a Borel probability measure $μ$ on $\X$ that is critical for $\SC$, or maximally spread out, meaning that the associated outer measure $μ^\ast$ satisfies $μ^\ast(\X\setminus\SC)= 0$ and furthermore satisfies for every $0<s<s_\ast$ the small-ball condition \[μ^\ast(B(x,r))\le\exp\bigl(-c(s)\cdot(1/r)^s\bigr)\quad\text{ for all }x\in\X\text{ and }0<r<r_0(s).\]
  The existence of such a critical measure in particular implies that the so-called power-exponential Hausdorff dimension of $\SC$ introduced in [J.~Topol.~Anal.~4(2):203--235, 2012] coincides with the lower power-exponential Minkowski dimension.
  Previous work [Found.~Comput.~Math.~23(1):329--392, 2023] shows that such a critical measure gives rise to a phase transition regarding lossy compression and approximation by quantized neural networks of elements of $\SC$, provided that the $\liminf$ in the definition of $s_\ast$ exists as an actual limit. There, critical measures were constructed for unit balls of certain Besov and Sobolev spaces considered as subsets of $L^2$. In contrast, our construction is completely general. In particular, our results apply to function spaces of dominating mixed smoothness.

</details>


### [173] [Vector-Valued Wavelet Bases as Hilbert $\mathbb{M}_m(\mathbb{R})$-Module Bases: A Construction from Scalar Wavelets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30589)
*Hicham Tarif,Nadir Maaroufi*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Vector-valued multiscale representations are essential when signals or fields take values in $\mathbb{R}^m$ and component interactions carry meaningful information. Most multiwavelet and super-wavelet constructions are formulated in scalar Hilbert-space settings and typically produce channelwise scalar coefficients followed by recombination. We develop an intrinsic framework for vector-valued wavelets on $L^2(\mathbb{R}^d,\mathbb{R}^m)$ by endowing this space with a natural $\mathbb{M}_m(\mathbb{R})$-valued inner product, thereby turning it into a Hilbert $\mathbb{M}_m(\mathbb{R})$-module. This module viewpoint yields matrix-valued coefficients that encode cross-component interactions and provides canonical reconstruction through a Parseval-type identity. Within this setting, we introduce a constructive lifting procedure that builds separable multivariate vector-valued wavelet bases in $L^2(\mathbb{R}^d,\mathbb{R}^m)$ from scalar wavelet bases while preserving compact support, vanishing moments, and regularity.

</details>


### [174] [Infinite Matrix Operators and $E$-Frames in Hilbert Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30592)
*Hassan Hedayati Rad,Tayebe Lal Shateri*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文研究了E-框架的概念，证明如果矩阵E在序列空间\bigoplus_{n=1}^\infty\mathcal{H}上满射作用，那么每个普通框架都是E-框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: E-框架是框架理论中一个新引入的概念，通过将无限可逆复矩阵作用于Hilbert空间中元素序列而得到。需要确定序列Ψ和矩阵E满足何种条件时，EΨ才能成为框架，这是本文要回答的自然问题。

Method: 通过分析矩阵E在序列空间\bigoplus_{n=1}^{\infty}\mathcal{H}上的作用方式，证明当E是满射时，任意普通框架经过E变换后仍然是框架，即成为E-框架。

Result: 证明了若矩阵E在序列空间上满射，则所有普通框架都是E-框架。

Conclusion: E-框架的满射条件保证了普通框架在该变换下的封闭性，为E-框架理论的进一步研究提供了基础性结果。

Abstract: The concept of an $E$-frame, recently introduced in frame theory, is obtained by applying an infinite invertible complex matrix to a sequence of elements of a Hilbert space $\mathcal{H}$. Here, $E$ is considered as a matrix mapping on the sequence space $\bigoplus_{n=1}^{\infty}\mathcal{H}$. A natural question is to determine the conditions on a sequence $Ψ=\{ψ_k\}_{k=1}^{\infty}$ and a matrix $E$ under which $EΨ$ becomes a frame. In this paper, we show that if $E$ acts surjectively on $\bigoplus_{n=1}^{\infty}\mathcal{H}$, then every ordinary frame is an $E$-frame.

</details>


### [175] [Sharp discrete Hardy constants in dimensions three and four and strict upper bounds from dimension nine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30831)
*Carlos Lizama*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文研究$\mathbb Z^N$上最近邻Hardy不等式的最优常数$C(N)$，证明连续系数$A_N=(N-2)^2/4$在所有维度都是上界，并精确确定$C(3)=1/4$、$C(4)=1$，同时在高维（$N\ge11$）给出严格低于$A_N$的显式上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散Hardy不等式是分析、偏微分方程和谱理论中的基本工具。此前关于格点$\mathbb Z^N$上Hardy常数与连续情形系数$A_N$的关系尚无完整认识，特别是在低维精确值和高维最优性方面存在空白。本文旨在确定各维度下离散Hardy不等式的最优常数，并揭示离散与连续情形的本质差异。

Method: 本文采用多种技术手段：低维情形（$N=3,4$）通过平移径向超解（shifted radial supersolutions）、角凸性（angular convexity）和离散基态表示（discrete ground-state representation）获得精确等式；$N=9$通过Gaussian Rayleigh-Ritz构造结合精确Jacobi theta函数估计处理；更高维度通过有限维轨道压缩（finite-dimensional orbit compressions）获得上界。此外还证明了平移幂机制从$N=5$起无法在连续系数下工作。

Result: 证明了$C(N)\le A_N$对所有$N\ge3$成立；精确求出了$C(3)=1/4$和$C(4)=1$；对$N=9,10$证明了严格不等式$C(N)<A_N$；给出了对所有$N\ge3$成立的显式上界$C(N)\le 3N-\sqrt{N^2+8N-8}<2N$，该上界从$N=11$起低于$A_N$；在维度3和4导出了正空间余量（positive spatial remainders），并在高维情形得到离散Schrödinger算子的谱推论。

Conclusion: 离散格点上的Hardy不等式最优常数在低维（3、4维）与连续系数重合，但从更高维度起严格偏离。文中建立的显式上界框架和多种技术工具为理解离散-连续Hardy常数差异提供了系统方法。

Abstract: For $N\ge3$, let $C(N)$ be the optimal constant in the nearest-neighbour Hardy inequality on $\mathbb Z^N$ with $u(0)=0$, and set $A_N=(N-2)^2/4$. We prove that the continuum coefficient remains an upper bound, $C(N)\le A_N$, in every dimension, and determine the exact values $C(3)=A_3=1/4$ and $C(4)=A_4=1$. In higher dimensions we show $C(N)<A_N$ for $N=9,10$ and obtain the explicit bound \[
  C(N)\le 3N-\sqrt{N^2+8N-8}<2N\qquad(N\ge3), \] which lies below $A_N$ from dimension eleven onward. The low-dimensional equalities follow from shifted radial supersolutions, angular convexity, and a discrete ground-state representation. We also show that this shifted-power mechanism cannot work at the continuum coefficient from dimension five onward. Dimension nine is treated by a Gaussian Rayleigh--Ritz construction combined with exact Jacobi theta-function estimates, while the higher-dimensional bounds are obtained through finite-dimensional orbit compressions. Finally, we derive positive spatial remainders in dimensions three and four and a spectral consequence for the associated discrete Schrödinger operators in the high-dimensional regime.

</details>


### [176] [B-Frames, B-Riesz basis, and Their tensor products](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30967)
*Abdelkrim Bourouihiya,Chaimae Mezzat*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文定义了双b-框架和b-Riesz基，给出了b-Riesz基的多种刻画，证明了两个Hilbert空间中序列的张量积构成b-框架（或b-Riesz基）当且仅当两个分量都是b-框架（或b-Riesz基），并建立了b-框架与g-框架之间的对应关系及构造方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: b-框架作为g-框架的推广，具有更简单的定义和潜在更低的处理成本，但现有的文献中缺少对双b-框架和b-Riesz基的精确定义，且b-框架与g-框架之间的关系尚未系统建立。

Method: 首先严格定义双b-框架和b-Riesz基，随后给出b-Riesz基的多个等价刻画条件；利用张量积分析两个Hilbert空间中序列的b-框架性质；最后建立b-框架与g-框架之间的对应关系，并提出由b-框架诱导构造框架的流程。

Result: 证明了张量积序列构成b-框架（或b-Riesz基）当且仅当每个分量分别是b-框架（或b-Riesz基）；给出了b-Riesz基的多种刻画；建立了b-框架与g-框架的对应关系。

Conclusion: 本文填补了b-框架理论中双b-框架和b-Riesz基定义的空白，揭示了b-框架与g-框架的内在联系，并为信号处理等领域提供了由b-框架构造框架的有效途径。

Abstract: Like g-frames, b-frames were introduced to generalize the concept of frames, allowing for broader applications in signal processing and other fields. The advantage of b-frames resides in their simpler definition, which may lead to reduced processing times. In this paper, we define dual b-frames and b-Riesz bases which were not precisely defined in previous literature and provide several characterizations of b-Riesz bases. We prove that the tensor product of two sequences, each lying in a Hilbert space, constitutes a b-frame (or a b-Riesz basis) if and only if both components of the product are b-frames (or b-Riesz bases). Finally, we establish a correspondence between b-frames and g-frames and propose a process for constructing frames induced by b-frames.

</details>


### [177] [Bang vectors in the real polarization problem: The exact dimensional range](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31101)
*Damián Pinasco*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文确定了归一化最长带符号和（称为Bang向量）总是满足实数极化不等式的精确维度范围：该规则仅当n≤14时普遍成立。对于每个n≥15，构造了n个单位向量，其所有正项和（在全局符号意义下）是唯一的最长带符号和，但其归一化方向的极化积小于n^{-n/2}。反例由单一显式族给出，且对相同构型还展示了满足极化界的非极大带符号和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Bang向量（归一化最长带符号和）是否总能满足实数极化不等式，这是一个关于向量构型与极化理论的基础性问题。确定该性质成立的精确维度边界，并理解临界维度之后反例的结构。

Method: 首先确定Bang向量满足实数极化不等式的精确维度范围（n≤14）。然后对每个n≥15构造一个显式的n个单位向量族，使所有正项求和为唯一最长带符号和（至全局符号），同时证明其归一化方向的极化积小于n^{-n/2}。同一构型下还展示了非极大带符号和满足极化界。

Result: 确定了维度阈值：n≤14时Bang向量普遍满足实数极化不等式，n≥15时存在反例。反例由一个单一的显式向量族给出，且在同一构型中存在满足极化界的非极大带符号和。

Conclusion: 实数极化不等式对Bang向量的普遍有效性在n=14处发生临界转变：n≤14时成立，n≥15时被单一显式反例族所破坏。这一结果精确定位了Bang向量极化性质的维度边界。

Abstract: We determine the exact dimensional range in which a normalized longest signed sum, which we call a Bang vector, always satisfies the real polarization inequality: this prescription is universally valid if and only if \(n\leq 14\). For every \(n\geq 15\) we construct \(n\) unit vectors whose all-positive sum is, up to global sign, the unique longest signed sum, while its normalized direction has polarization product smaller than \(n^{-n/2}\). The counterexamples are given by a single explicit family, and for the same configurations we exhibit nonmaximal signed sums whose normalized directions do satisfy the polarization bound.

</details>


<div id='math.SP'></div>

# math.SP [[Back]](#toc)

### [178] [Complex Analysis in completeness, spectral and scattering problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28801)
*Alexei Poltoratski*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 本文是由一系列讲义组成的课程资料，面向初级研究者，系统介绍复分析在调和分析、傅里叶分析、微分算子谱理论与散射理论等领域的应用，涵盖从经典完备性问题（Bernstein、Beurling-Malliavin、Wiener、Kolmogorov、Krein）到Gap问题与Type问题的解、Schrödinger算子的逆谱问题、典范Hamilton系统以及非线性傅里叶变换的点态收敛等前沿主题，每讲附有习题与参考文献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在为初级研究人员提供一条系统进入复分析在调和分析、傅里叶分析、微分算子谱理论与散射理论等领域应用的入门路径，填补相关领域教学材料的空白。

Method: 采用系列讲义的形式，从经典完备性问题出发，逐步推进到前沿课题，包括Gap与Type问题、逆谱问题、典范Hamilton系统及非线性傅里叶变换的点态收敛等，每讲配有习题和参考文献以辅助学习。

Result: 课程内容覆盖了从经典到前沿的一系列重要问题及其解决方案，形成了一套完整的教学体系。

Conclusion: 该讲义系列为初级研究者提供了系统学习复分析应用领域的关键理论与方法的完整课程框架。

Abstract: These lectures are aimed at junior researchers seeking to enter the field of applications of complex analysis to harmonic analysis, Fourier analysis, spectral and scattering theory for differential operators and related fields. Starting with classical completeness problems posed by Bernstein, Beurling and Malliavin, Wiener, Kolmogorov and Krein, the course progresses to the solutions of the Gap and Type Problems, inverse spectral problems for Schrödinger operators and canonical Hamiltonian systems, and pointwise convergence of the non-linear Fourier transform. Each lecture is supplemented with exercises and references.

</details>


### [179] [Non-Elliptic Quadratic $\mathcal{PT}$-Symmetric Operators and Similarity to Self-Adjoint Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28821)
*Stepan Malkov*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 本文证明了满足动力学平均条件的、谱为实数的非自伴非椭圆二次PT对称算子具有相似性结果：该算子弱相似于一个自伴算子当且仅当其相关二次型的基矩阵可对角化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非自伴算子（特别是PT对称算子）与自伴算子之间的相似性关系，这是谱理论中一个核心问题。对于非椭圆二次算子，其相似性刻画此前尚未完全清楚，本文旨在给出一个精确的充要条件。

Method: 采用动力学平均条件作为技术假设，通过分析二次型算子的基矩阵（fundamental matrix）的可对角化性，建立算子弱相似于自伴算子的充要条件。

Result: 证明了在动力学平均条件下，非自伴非椭圆二次PT对称算子弱相似于自伴算子，当且仅当其基矩阵可对角化。

Conclusion: 这一结果为非椭圆PT对称二次算子的相似性分类提供了完整的刻画，将算子层面的弱相似性与代数层面基矩阵的可对角化性联系起来。

Abstract: We prove a similarity result for non-self-adjoint non-elliptic quadratic $\mathcal{PT}$-symmetric operators with real spectrum satisfying a dynamical averaging condition. In particular, we show that such an operator is weakly similar to a self-adjoint operator precisely when the fundamental matrix of the associated quadratic form is diagonalizable.

</details>


### [180] [Scaling inequalities and limits for clamped plate eigenvalues on geodesic disks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29498)
*Scott Harman*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究了球面与双曲空间中双调和算子的固定（clamped）振动与屈曲特征值在测地圆盘上的尺度不等式，这些不等式类比于欧氏空间中平板特征值的标准尺度不变性。研究将曲率空间中的尺度结果从拉普拉斯算子推广到四阶特征值问题，并确定了圆盘膨胀充满整个空间时尺度化特征值的极限行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将欧氏空间中薄板振动与屈曲特征值的尺度不变性推广到非欧几何（球面与双曲空间），并理解弯曲几何如何影响四阶椭圆特征值问题的标度行为及其极限。

Method: 对球面与双曲空间中双调和算子的固定振动与屈曲特征值建立尺度不等式；通过分析圆盘半径趋于无穷（充满全空间）时的渐近行为，结合几何与不同尺度选择确定特征值趋于零、正/负无穷或与双曲谱底部相关的有限值；利用共形反演将球面屈曲极限中的负发散与外部屈曲问题联系起来。

Result: 建立了球面与双曲空间中双调和特征值的尺度不等式；揭示了极限行为依赖于几何与尺度选择：特征值可能趋于零、正/负无穷或双曲谱底部相关的有限值；发现球面屈曲极限中的负发散可通过共形反演与外部屈曲问题相关联。

Conclusion: 本文成功将曲率空间中拉普拉斯算子的尺度结果延伸至四阶双调和特征值问题，为弯曲几何中高阶椭圆算子的谱分析提供了新见解，并建立了不同几何中屈曲问题之间的共形联系。

Abstract: For the bilaplacian in spherical and hyperbolic spaces, clamped vibrating and buckling eigenvalues on geodesic disks are shown to satisfy scaling inequalities analogous to the standard scale invariance of Euclidean plate eigenvalues. These results extend curved-space scaling results from the Laplacian to fourth-order eigenvalue problems. In addition, the limiting behavior of the scaled eigenvalues is determined as the disks expand to fill the ambient space. Depending on the geometry and the choice of scaling, the eigenvalues tend to zero, positive or negative infinity, or finite values associated with the bottom of the hyperbolic spectrum. The negative divergence in one spherical buckling limit is related by conformal inversion to an exterior buckling problem.

</details>


### [181] [Uniform Non-Localization for Laplace Eigenfunctions on the Equilateral Triangle: Dirichlet, Neumann, and Robin Boundary Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30071)
*Binh T. Nguyen*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究了等边三角形上完备Laplace特征空间在Dirichlet、Neumann和Robin边界条件下的均匀非局域化问题，重点分析了Robin微扰对Neumann问题的稳定性，并给出了Dirichlet/Neumann情形下频率无关的正质量捕获比例的严格证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Laplace特征函数在观测集合上的质量分布（非局域化）是谱几何和量子混沌领域的基础问题。在等边三角形这一具有高度算术对称性的区域上，特征空间维数（算术重数）可任意增大，使得均匀非局域化结论是否仍成立成为一个关键且非平凡的问题。特别地，Robin边界条件作为Neumann问题的自然微扰，其稳定性需要严格论证。

Method: 本文的方法包括：（1）将三角形展开为平坦环面，结合二维Fourier聚类论证与Jarník型格点引理，给出Dirichlet/Neumann情形下正测度观测集捕获正比例L²质量的严格证明；（2）利用McCartin关于Robin问题的精确参数化获得模态空间估计的谱指标均匀性；（3）运用Rudnick-Wigman小参数简单性定理防止去对称化谱类合并；（4）借助多元Turán–Nazarov不等式，在Robin模态类有界重合复杂度的条件下建立特征空间级观测的均匀性。

Result: 证明了对于等边三角形上的Dirichlet和Neumann特征空间，任意正测度可测观测集在所有特征空间中均匀捕获正比例的L²质量，与算术重数无关。进一步证明该Neumann估计在0≤σ≤σ₀的Robin参数范围内均匀保持。对于任意固定σ>0，证实有界重合复杂度足以保证统一特征空间级观测。由此将无条件小Robin定理与一般固定Robin参数下的全局谱复杂度问题明确区分。

Conclusion: 等边三角形上Laplace特征空间的均匀非局域化在Dirichlet和Neumann情形下无条件成立，且在小Robin微扰范围内保持稳定；一般固定Robin参数下的全局结论则归结为一个谱复杂度假设，论文为此给出了充分条件（有界重合复杂度）并明确了该问题的层次结构。

Abstract: We study uniform non-localization for complete Laplace eigenspaces on the equilateral triangle under Dirichlet, Neumann, and Robin boundary conditions, with particular emphasis on stability under Robin perturbations of the Neumann problem. As a stationary reference estimate, we give a self-contained proof that every measurable observation set $V\subset T$ of positive measure captures a frequency-independent positive proportion of the $L^2$ mass of every vector in every Dirichlet or Neumann eigenspace, even in the presence of arbitrarily large arithmetic multiplicity. The proof unfolds the triangle to a flat torus and combines a two-dimensional Fourier-cluster argument with a Jarník-type lattice lemma. Our principal perturbative result shows that the Neumann estimate persists uniformly for $0\leqσ\leqσ_0$ under Robin boundary conditions. McCartin's exact Robin parametrization yields modal-space estimates uniform in the spectral indices, while the small-parameter simplicity theorem of Rudnick and Wigman prevents distinct desymmetrized spectral classes from merging. Finally, for an arbitrary fixed $σ>0$, we show that bounded coincidence complexity of Robin modal classes is sufficient for uniform eigenspace-level observation via a multivariate Turán--Nazarov inequality. Thus the unconditional small-Robin theorem is separated from the remaining global spectral-complexity question at a general fixed Robin parameter.

</details>


### [182] [Band edges of periodic Schrödinger operators are generically isolated and nondegenerate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30189)
*Zhongkai Tao,Mengxuan Yang*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 本文证明了对周期薛定谔算子，在一般（generic）势能条件下，每个谱隙端点仅由单个Bloch带在有限多个准动量处取得，且在该处Hessian非退化，从而证明了谱边猜想（Spectral Edge Conjecture）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 周期薛定谔算子的谱结构是数学物理中的核心问题，谱隙端点的Bloch带性质和准动量个数决定了能带的几何行为。谱边猜想此前尚未得到证明，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 通过分析周期薛定谔算子H_V=-Δ+V在\mathbb R^d（d≥2）上具有有界实值势能的情形，利用generic势能分析（Baire范畴/代数几何方法）对谱隙端点处的Bloch带性质进行刻画，证明其仅由单个Bloch带在有限多个准动量处取得，并在该处Hessian非退化。

Result: 证明了对于一般势能（generic），每个谱隙端点仅由一个Bloch带在有限多个准动量处取得，且在这些取得点处Hessian矩阵非退化。这一结果直接确立了谱边猜想的成立。

Conclusion: 本文成功证明了周期薛定谔算子的谱边猜想，给出了谱隙端点处Bloch带的精细几何性质。该结论对量子物理中能带结构的研究具有重要理论意义。

Abstract: For periodic Schrödinger operators $H_V=-Δ+V$ with bounded real-valued potentials on $\mathbb R^d$ with $d\ge2$, we show that for generic potentials, each endpoint of every spectral gap is attained by a single Bloch band at only finitely many quasimomenta, and has a nondegenerate Hessian at every attaining point. This proves the Spectral Edge Conjecture for periodic Schrödinger operators.

</details>


### [183] [Spectra of Non-Self-Adjoint Almost Mathieu Matrices and the Scottish Flag Operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30191)
*Simon Becker,Izak Oltman*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类非自伴的几乎Mathieu矩阵（即乘以复相位e^{iφ}的离散Laplacian加上周期势cos(2πj/N+θ)），推导出Chambers公式并分离出仅依赖于N和φ的中心多项式Q_{N,φ}，证明其零点位于两条垂直线上e^{iφ/2}ℝ∪e^{i(φ/2+π/2)}ℝ。对偶数N，定理进一步推广到θ∈2πℤ/N的情形，并显式计算了极限特征值测度。对于φ∈[-π,π]，特征值分布逼近椭圆积分密度，质量分别为1-|φ|/π和|φ|/π，最大半径分别为2cos(φ/2)和2sin(φ/2)。特别地，在φ=π/2时，中心多项式分解为正四次因子，从而严格证明了Trefethen与Chapman引入的"苏格兰旗矩阵"的谱位于盐十字的两条对角线上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非自伴几乎Mathieu矩阵的谱性质，特别是其特征多项式的结构以及特征值在复平面上的分布规律。该问题源于非Hermitian量子力学和准周期系统，且与数值计算中"伪谱"现象密切相关。通过建立Chambers公式并将问题简化为一个不依赖于相位的中心多项式，可以揭示谱的几何结构，同时严格证明此前仅凭数值观察得到的"苏格兰旗矩阵"谱的已知现象。

Method: 本文采用解析方法：首先推导修改后的Chambers公式，从特征多项式中分离出仅依赖于N和φ的中心多项式Q_{N,φ}，该多项式不受相位θ或边界条件变化的影响。然后利用代数与复分析工具（包括多项式因式分解和椭圆积分）研究Q_{N,φ}的零点分布，证明其位于两条垂直线上。对偶数N，通过显式计算获得极限特征值测度，并利用椭圆积分给出渐近密度。

Result: (1) 对任意N，中心多项式Q_{N,φ}的零点全部落在两条垂直线e^{iφ/2}ℝ与e^{i(φ/2+π/2)}ℝ上。(2) 当N为偶数且θ∈2πℤ/N时，矩阵A_N(φ,θ)的谱同样位于这两条线上，并得到了极限特征值测度的显式表达式。(3) 当φ∈[-π,π]时，特征值分布逼近两类椭圆积分密度，各自携带质量1-|φ|/π和|φ|/π，对应的最大半径分别为2|cos(φ/2)|和2|sin(φ/2)|。(4) 在φ=π/2时，Q_{N,φ}分解为正四次因子之积，从而证明了Trefethen-Chapman苏格兰旗矩阵的谱严格位于盐十字的两条对角线上。

Conclusion: 本文完整刻画了一类非自伴几乎Mathieu矩阵的谱结构，建立了谱位于两条垂直线上的严格定理，并给出了极限特征值测度的显式公式。特别是φ=π/2情形的因式分解结果，为"苏格兰旗矩阵"的数值观察提供了严格的数学证明。这项工作为理解非自伴周期算子的谱几何提供了新的解析工具。

Abstract: For $N\geq 3$ and a potential phase $\vartheta\in\mathbb{R}$, we study the non-self-adjoint almost Mathieu matrix obtained by multiplying the discrete Laplacian by a complex phase with angle $\varphi\in\mathbb{R}$, $A_N(\varphi,\vartheta)=e^{i\varphi}(S+S^{-1})/2+\operatorname{diag}(\cos(2πj/N+\vartheta))_{j\in\mathbb{Z}/N\mathbb{Z}}$, where $S e_j=e_{j+1}$ is the periodic shift on $\mathbb{C}^N$. We derive a Chambers formula and isolate the part $Q_{N,\varphi}$ of the characteristic polynomial that depends only on $N$ and $\varphi$, but not on $\vartheta$ or on a change of boundary conditions for the shift operator. We then show, for every $N$, that the zeros of $Q_{N,\varphi}$ lie on the two perpendicular lines $e^{i\varphi/2}\mathbb{R}\cup e^{i(\varphi/2+π/2)}\mathbb{R}$. For even $N$, the same property holds for the matrices $A_N(\varphi,\vartheta)$ with $\vartheta\in 2π\mathbb{Z}/N$, and we compute their limiting eigenvalue measure explicitly. For $\varphi\in[-π,π]$, the eigenvalue distribution approximates elliptic-integral densities with masses $1-|\varphi|/π$ and $|\varphi|/π$, and maximal radii $2|\cos(\varphi/2)|$ and $2|\sin(\varphi/2)|$, respectively. At $\varphi=π/2$, the central polynomial $Q_{N,\varphi}$ factors into positive quartic factors. This proves that the Scottish flag matrix, after Trefethen and Chapman, has its spectrum on the two diagonal lines of the saltire.

</details>


### [184] [On the discrete spectrum of Dirac operators with Lorentz-scalar $δ$-shell interactions supported on unbounded curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30651)
*Markus Holzmann,Vladimir Lotoreichik,Marco Vogel*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究了平面上带正质量的Dirac算子，其势为支撑在局部变形折线上的Lorentz标量δ-壳吸引相互作用（强度τ∈(-∞,0)\{-2}）。证明了该算子本质谱中有间隙，间隙内离散特征值数目有限；在Σ围成的某个区域为凸的假设下，当|τ|充分小或充分大时，离散谱非空。该离散谱由几何诱导产生，因为直线上相同相互作用的离散谱为空。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Dirac算子在半平面或折线类几何边界上受δ-壳势作用时离散谱的存在性与结构，特别是几何扰动（局部变形的折线）如何诱导出本质谱间隙中的束缚态。

Method: 通过取适当的传输条件定义奇异相互作用算子，利用自伴性分析与本质谱间隙的显式计算，结合几何假设（Σ围成的域之一为凸），针对相互作用强度τ绝对值充分小或充分大的情形进行分析，证明离散谱非空并给出有限性结论。

Result: 证明了间隙中离散特征值数量有限；在Σ所围区域有凸性假设下，当|τ|充分小或充分大时，离散谱非空。该结论对任意开角的折线局部变形均成立，且离散谱由几何（非直线）诱导。

Conclusion: 几何曲率/变形是产生该Dirac算子离散谱的关键因素；与直线支撑情形（离散谱为空）对比，明确了局部变形折线几何对束缚态产生的决定性作用。

Abstract: We consider the massive Dirac operator (with positive mass) in the plane with an attractive Lorentz-scalar $δ$-shell interaction of strength $τ\in(-\infty,0)\setminus\{-2\}$ supported on a $C^\infty$-smooth curve $Σ\subset\mathbb{R}^2$ being a local deformation of the broken line. This singular interaction is defined by imposing a suitable transmission condition on the curve $Σ$ in the operator domain. Such a Dirac operator is self-adjoint and has a gap in the essential spectrum, whose size is explicit and depends on the mass and the interaction strength. We show that the number of discrete eigenvalues in the gap is finite. Under the assumption that one of the domains bounded by $Σ$ is convex, we prove that the corresponding Dirac operator has a non-empty discrete spectrum, provided that $τ$ is either sufficiently small or sufficiently large in absolute value. The result holds for any perturbation of a broken line of any opening angle as described above, and this discrete spectrum is induced by the geometry, since for the same type of a singular interaction supported on the straight line the discrete spectrum is empty.

</details>


### [185] [Eigenvalue Asymptotics in High-Contrast Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30721)
*Huaian Diao,Long Li,Mourad Sini,Qilong Zhai*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究有界声学腔中包含缩小的高对比度夹杂物时的特征频率渐近行为，该配置源于对比增强超声成像（微泡作为声学对比剂）。文章识别了维度依赖的材料缩放，使夹杂物诱导的共振保持在固定的一阶频率区间。主要结果给出了任意预设有界频率窗口内谱的完整渐近描述，揭示两种不同的谱机制：背景Dirichlet特征频率以扰动特征值簇形式存在，而奇异材料对比产生额外Minnaert特征值分支。极限Minnaert频率在三维中具有基于电容的显式表达式，二维中具有基于面积的显式表达式。二维数值实验验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究由对比增强超声成像应用驱动，其中微泡用作声学对比剂。理解微泡在腔体中缩小时的共振行为对于优化超声成像对比度和灵敏度至关重要，因此需要严格的数学分析来刻画高对比度小夹杂物引起的频谱结构。

Method: 采用渐近分析的方法，识别依赖于空间维度的材料缩放条件，使夹杂物诱导的共振保持在固定的频率（一阶）范围。通过谱分析建立该配置下完整的渐近展开，区分背景Dirichlet特征频率的扰动效应与Minnaert共振分支的生成，并分别在三维和二维中推导Minnaert频率的显式表达式（分别为基于电容和基于面积的形式），辅以二维数值实验验证。

Result: 在任意预设的有界频率窗口内，得到了谱的完整渐近描述。结果表明：背景Dirichlet特征频率保留为受扰动的特征值簇；奇异材料对比度产生额外的Minnaert特征值分支。极限Minnaert频率在两种维度下均有显式表达式，三维为电容表达式，二维为面积表达式。二维数值实验证实了背景Dirichlet特征频率的扰动和额外Minnaert分支的出现。

Conclusion: 本文为声学腔中缩小高对比度夹杂物的特征频谱建立了严格的渐近理论框架，揭示了两类谱机制的共存（背景特征值扰动簇与Minnaert分支），并给出了维度相关的显式极限频率公式，为超声成像中微泡对比剂的频谱预测提供了数学基础。

Abstract: We investigate the eigenfrequency asymptotics of a bounded acoustic cavity containing a shrinking high-contrast inclusion. This configuration is motivated by contrast-enhanced ultrasound imaging, in which microbubbles are employed as acoustic contrast agents.
  We identify dimension-dependent material scalings that keep the inclusion-induced resonance in a fixed order-one frequency regime as the inclusion shrinks. Our main result gives a complete asymptotic description of the spectrum in any prescribed bounded frequency window. Two distinct spectral mechanisms arise: the background Dirichlet eigenfrequencies persist as perturbed eigenvalue clusters, while the singular material contrast creates an additional Minnaert eigenvalue branch. The limiting Minnaert frequency is explicit in both dimensions, with a capacitance-based expression in three dimensions and an area-based expression in two dimensions.
  Two-dimensional numerical experiments confirm both the perturbation of the background Dirichlet eigenfrequencies and the emergence of the additional Minnaert branch.

</details>


### [186] [On almost commuting matrices with respect to the normalized Hilbert--Schmidt norm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31000)
*Mohit Bansil,Ilya Kachkovskiy*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究了Rosenthal–Halmos问题，证明对任意自伴矩阵X和Y（满足范数限制），存在交换的自伴矩阵X'和Y'，使得在归一化Hilbert–Schmidt范数下的逼近误差不超过5‖[X,Y]‖^{1/3}，且该指数1/3不可改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rosenthal–Halmos问题探讨的是几乎交换的矩阵能否被精确交换的矩阵所逼近，以及逼近误差与交换子范数之间的最佳数量关系。这一问题的解决对理解矩阵近似交换结构具有重要理论意义。

Method: 论文针对归一化Hilbert–Schmidt范数下的Rosenthal–Halmos问题，构造了满足约束条件（包括保持X与X'交换）的交换自伴矩阵X'和Y'，并给出了误差界5‖[X,Y]‖^{1/3}。同时通过反例论证了在给定约束下指数1/3为最优。

Result: 证明了存在交换自伴矩阵对使得逼近误差有界为5‖[X,Y]‖_{2,d}^{1/3}，并且证明了在附加约束[X,X']=0的条件下，指数1/3无法被改进，即达到最优。

Conclusion: 本文给出了Rosenthal–Halmos问题在归一化Hilbert–Schmidt范数下的一个显式常数（5）和最优指数（1/3）的结果，是该问题研究的实质性进展。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider the Rosenthal -- Halmos problem of almost commuting matrices with respect to the normalized Hilbert -- Schmidt norm $\|\cdot\|_{2,d}=d^{-1/2}\|\cdot\|_2$. We show that if $X$ and $Y$ are self-adjoint matrices with $\|X\|\le 1$, $\|Y\|\le 1$, then there exist commuting self-adjoint matrices $X',Y'$ such that $\|X-X'\|_{2,d}+\|Y-Y'\|_{2,d}\le 5\|[X,Y]\|_{2,d}^{1/3}$, and $[X,X']=0$. Within these constraints, the exponent $1/3$ cannot be improved.

</details>


<div id='math.AP'></div>

### [187] [A nonlinear Li-Yau inequality and its consequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28881)
*Agnid Banerjee,Nicola Garofalo,Hamidreza Mahmoudian*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明了闭黎曼流形（非负Ricci曲率）上归一化p-Laplace方程的sharp非线性Li-Yau不等式，通过解耦二阶正则化与一阶eikonal项的逼近流族，在极大值原理下获得最优常数，并推广到Ricci下界为-κ的闭流形及完备非紧流形，进而得到sharp Harnack不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Li-Yau不等式是偏微分方程与几何分析中的核心工具，用于刻画热方程正解的对数梯度估计并推导Harnack不等式。经典线性情形已被充分研究，但对归一化p-Laplace方程（非线性的退化/奇异抛物型方程）建立最优的Li-Yau型不等式，特别是在临界集上的粘性解释下，仍是一个挑战。本文旨在给出该非线性方程sharp常数版本的Li-Yau不等式，并处理临界集的正则性问题。

Method: 本文方法的核心是构造一族带二阶正则化的均匀抛物逼近流，其中二阶正则化项与一阶eikonal项被解耦。对该逼近流族，极大值原理以sharp常数一致地适用（不依赖正则化参数），且无需对解的临界集作任何假设。关键工具是一个精确的Bochner型恒等式，该恒等式在经典的α-松弛Li-Yau泛函下保持形式不变性，从而使得估计可以从非负Ricci曲率情形推广到Ricci下界为-κ的情形。

Result: 得到了归一化p-Laplace方程（1<p<∞）在非负Ricci曲率闭流形上的sharp非线性Li-Yau不等式，常数最优——由平坦Rn中的显式自相似解达到，并通过大环极限（R^n/(LZ)^n，L→∞）将sharp性转移到紧致情形。进一步得到：(i) Ricci≥-κ闭流形上的对应不等式；(ii) 完备非紧流形（Ricci≥0）上逼近流的sharp不等式（仅在距离函数截断假设和Li-Yau量的多项式增长条件下）；(iii) 相应的sharp全局Harnack不等式。

Conclusion: 本文建立了归一化p-Laplace方程在最优常数意义上的Li-Yau不等式，克服了临界集上的正则性困难，并通过Bochner型恒等式的解耦技巧统一处理了闭流形与非紧流形。所得结果不仅在几何上是最优的（常数不可改进），而且在推广到弱Ricci下界和完备非紧情形时保持了sharp性，为非线性抛物型方程与几何分析的交叉领域提供了新的工具。

Abstract: We prove a sharp nonlinear version of the celebrated Li-Yau inequality for positive solutions of the normalized parabolic $p$-Laplacian equation $u_t = Δu + (p-2)|\nabla u|^{-2}\nabla^2u(\nabla u,\nabla u)$, $1<p<\infty$, on a closed Riemannian manifold with nonnegative Ricci curvature, the equation being interpreted in the viscosity sense on the critical set of the solution. The constant is best possible, even within the class of closed manifolds: it is attained identically by an explicit self-similar solution in flat $\Rn$, and its sharpness transfers to the compact setting through a large-torus limit along the flat tori $\mathbb R^n/(L\mathbb Z)^n$, $L \to \infty$. The proof rests on an exact Bochner-type identity for a family of uniformly parabolic approximating flows in which the second-order regularization and the first-order eikonal term are decoupled: for this family the maximum principle applies with the sharp constant, uniformly in the regularization parameter, and with no assumption on the critical set of the solution. The identity is moreover form-invariant under the classical $α$-relaxation of the Li-Yau functional; as a consequence we also obtain the corresponding inequality on closed manifolds with $\operatorname{Ric} \ge -κ$ (again with no assumption beyond the Ricci lower bound), and, on complete noncompact manifolds with $\operatorname{Ric} \ge 0$, the sharp inequality for the approximating flows -- again with no assumption on the critical set -- under a cutoff hypothesis on the distance function (automatic in $\Rn$, and, for $p \ge 2$, under nonnegative sectional curvature) and a qualitative polynomial growth condition on the Li-Yau quantity, satisfied, with exponent zero, by the extremal profile. A sharp global Harnack inequality follows.

</details>


### [188] [Analysis validation of a new continuum model for the evolution of grain boundaries in polycrystalline materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28954)
*Peicheng Zhu,Xiaoxue Qin,Yang Xiang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文对Zhang等人2017年提出的晶界演化连续方程进行了严格的数学分析，证明了初边值问题弱解的整体存在性、唯一性和正则性，建立了稳态解的存在唯一性，并证明了弱解随时间收敛到稳态解，最后通过数值模拟验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晶界的演化涉及位错（离位）等线缺陷的微观机制以及多种热力学驱动力的综合效应，Zhang等人2017年提出的连续方程描述这一过程，但该模型的数学理论性质（解的存在性、唯一性、正则性及长时间行为）尚缺乏严格的数学证明，需要加以建立。

Method: 采用能量方法、希尔伯特型奇异积分估计、希尔伯特变换的傅里叶变换以及带权重的未知函数时间导数估计等关键工具，对初边值问题进行严格的数学分析；同时辅以数值模拟验证。

Result: 证明了弱解的整体时间存在性、唯一性和正则性；建立了稳态解的存在性和唯一性；证明了演化问题的弱解在适当意义下收敛到稳态解；数值模拟结果与分析结果一致。

Conclusion: 该晶界演化模型的数学理论（解的存在唯一性、正则性与长时间收敛性）得到了完整建立，数值模拟验证了理论分析的可靠性。证明中的主要困难来源于奇异非局部项、最高阶导数系数关于未知数梯度不光滑，以及自由能非一致下有界等特性。

Abstract: To describe the evolution of grain boundaries based on the underlying microscopic mechanisms of line defects (disconnections) and the integrated effects of a diverse range of thermodynamic driving forces, Zhang, et al. in 2017 formulated a continuum equation. We prove the global-in-time existence, uniqueness, and regularity of the weak solution to an initial-boundary value problem for this model. The existence, uniqueness of the stationary solution are also established.
