Finally, we investigate the large-time behavior of the weak solution of the evolution problem, and show that the solution converges to the stationary solution in a suitable sense. Numerical simulations are carried out to validate the analysis results. The main difficulties in the proof of main theorems are due to a non-local term with singularity, a non-smooth coefficient of the highest derivative associated with the gradient of the unknown, and the special form of free energy which is not uniformly bounded from below. The key ingredients in the proof are the energy method, an estimate for a singular integral of the Hilbert type, Fourier transform of Hilbert transformations, and an estimate with a weight, for time-derivative of the unknown.

</details>


### [189] [Schauder estimates for the linearized very fast diffusion equations in bounded domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28983)
*Tianling Jin,Xushan Tu,Jingang Xiong,Zhen Zheng*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文在有界光滑区域上，针对带Dirichlet边界条件的线性化极快扩散方程建立了Schauder估计，为推导极快扩散方程解的最优边界正则性和长时间动力学提供了关键基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极快扩散方程的解的边界正则性和长时间动力学分析需要关键的解析工具——线性化方程的Schauder估计。该估计是后续推导最优边界行为和长时间行为的重要前提。

Method: 在有界光滑区域上，考虑带Dirichlet边界条件的线性化极快扩散方程，建立相应的Schauder估计（即在Hölder空间中建立解的高阶导数估计）。

Result: 成功建立了线性化极快扩散方程在有界光滑区域上、Dirichlet边界条件下的Schauder估计。

Conclusion: 所建立的Schauder估计为研究极快扩散方程的最优边界正则性和长时间动力学提供了重要的解析基础。

Abstract: We establish Schauder estimates for linearized very fast diffusion equations with Dirichlet boundary conditions in bounded smooth domains. These estimates provide an important ingredient for deriving optimal boundary regularity and long-time dynamics of solutions to very fast diffusion equations.

</details>


### [190] [Optimal regularity and fine asymptotics for very fast diffusion equations in bounded domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28984)
*Tianling Jin,Xushan Tu,Jingang Xiong,Zhen Zheng*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明了在-1<p<0范围内，具有零Dirichlet边界条件和与距离函数可比拟的初始数据的变换超快扩散方程，其可容许解的最优全局正则性：空间上属于C^{1,p+1}(Ω̄)，时间上为C^∞且一致到边界，并利用这些正则性结果推导出向友好巨解（friendly giant）的长时间渐近行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超快扩散方程在-1<p<0范围内具有退化和奇异性质，其解的正则性此前缺乏最优的全局刻画。理解可容许解的最优正则性不仅是方程本身理论完备性的关键，也是推导长时间精确渐近行为的必要前提。

Method: 通过变换将原始超快扩散方程转化为某种等价形式，在光滑有界域上建立可容许解的存在唯一性；利用与距离函数可比拟的初始条件结合精细的边界正则性分析，证明空间C^{1,p+1}正则性和时间方向上的C^∞光滑性，并证明指数p+1的最优性。

Result: 建立了解的存在唯一性，证明解在任何正时刻属于C^{1,p+1}(Ω̄)，且在时间上C^∞一致到边界；所有时间导数也属于C^{1,p+1}(Ω̄)，指数p+1被证明是最优的。基于这些正则性估计，进一步获得了向友好巨解收敛的长时间渐近展开：在C^{1,p+1}拓扑下的一阶展开，以及在C^{p+1}拓扑下相对误差的改进收敛率。

Conclusion: 该结果完全刻画了-1<p<0范围内变换超快扩散方程可容许解的最优全局正则性，并展示了正则性信息如何转化为精细的长时间渐近行为，为理解超快扩散方程解的边界行为和时间演化提供了完整的理论框架。

Abstract: We prove the optimal global regularity of admissible solutions to a transformed very fast diffusion equation in the range $-1<p<0$, posed on smooth bounded domains with zero Dirichlet boundary data and initial data comparable to the distance function. More precisely, we establish existence and uniqueness and show that solutions belong to $C^{1,p+1}(\overlineΩ)$ in space for every positive time and are $C^\infty$ in time uniformly up to the boundary. Moreover, all their time derivatives belong to $C^{1,p+1}(\overlineΩ)$, and the exponent $p+1$ is optimal. These regularity estimates further yield fine long-time asymptotics toward the friendly giant solution, including a first-order expansion in the $C^{1,p+1}(\overlineΩ)$ topology and an improved convergence rate for the relative error in $C^{p+1}(\overlineΩ)$.

</details>


### [191] [Time periodic problem of the Navier-Stokes equations in an exterior domain with periodically moving boundary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28999)
*Reinhard Farwig,Kazuyuki Tsuda*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了 $\mathbb{R}^n$（$n\geq 3$）外部区域中带有周期性运动边界和外部力的Navier-Stokes方程，在具有径向对称Muckenhoupt权重的加权函数空间中证明了局部唯一温和时间周期解的存在性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究外部区域中边界随时间周期性运动的Navier-Stokes方程的时间周期解，这是流体力学中的基础问题，涉及移动边界与外部区域的组合带来的数学挑战。

Method: 解分裂为静态部分（由势论估计控制）和纯振荡部分（通过解析半群理论构造为温和解）。对于坐标变换和移动边界产生的直至二阶的扰动项，在带权齐次Sobolev空间中利用加权Lorentz空间的极大$L^1$型正则性估计；为控制对流项，利用Stokes算子在加权空间中的$\mathcal H^\infty$-演算、BIP性质及分数幂嵌入估计。

Result: 在加权函数空间（径向对称Muckenhoupt权重）中证明了局部唯一温和时间周期解的存在性。

Conclusion: 该工作为外部区域中移动边界Navier-Stokes方程的时间周期解问题提供了新的一般性存在性结果，并发展了加权齐次空间中Stokes算子的相关工具。

Abstract: In this paper we consider the Navier-Stokes equations in exterior domains of $\mathbb{R}^n$, $n\geq 3$, with a periodically in time moving boundary $\partialΩ(t)$ and external force $f(t)$. For this case we prove the existence of a locally unique mild time periodic solution in weighted function spaces with radially symmetric Muckenhoupt weights. The solutions split into a stationary part controlled by potential theoretic estimates and a purely oscillatory part constructed as mild solution via analytic semigroup theory. To deal with perturbation terms of even second order - coming from a coordinate transform and the moving boundary - in weighted, homogeneous Sobolev spaces a maximal $L^1$ type regularity estimate will be used in weighted Lorentz spaces. To control the convective term an $\mathcal H^\infty$-calculus in weighted spaces of the Stokes operator, its $BIP$ property and embedding estimates of fractional powers are exploited, see a recent paper by the authors: The Stokes operator on exterior domains in homogeneous weighted function spaces: From weak theory to $\mathscr H^\infty$-calculus to fractional domains (2025).

</details>


### [192] [The Stokes Operator on Exterior Domains in Homogeneous Weighted Function Spaces:From Weak Theory to $\mathscr H^\infty$-calculus to Fractional Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29002)
*Reinhard Farwig,Kazuyuki Tsuda*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究光滑外部域Ω上Stokes算子A在带径向对称Muckenhoupt权w∈A_q的齐次Sobolev空间Ĥ^{κ,q}_w(Ω)中的性质，证明了加权齐次/非齐次L^q Sobolev空间上存在有界H^∞-演算，由此导出纯虚幂A^{it}的一致有界性，并将分数幂A^θ的定义域刻画为复插值空间，最终与散度自由向量场空间的交集识别为齐次空间，同时获得Stokes方程的加权变分不等式、半群衰减估计及L^p-极大正则性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Stokes算子在外部域流体力学问题中具有核心地位，但传统均匀Sobolev空间框架难以处理带权情形。本文旨在将Stokes算子的H^∞-演算理论、分数幂定义域刻画及插值性质推广到带径向对称Muckenhoupt权的齐次Sobolev空间，为外部域上带奇异权重的Navier-Stokes方程提供更精细的函数空间理论工具。

Method: 首先在加权非齐次与齐次L^q Sobolev空间上建立Stokes算子A的有界H^∞-演算这一基本性质。由此导出纯虚幂A^{it}的一致有界性，并利用复插值方法刻画分数幂A^θ的定义域（分别考虑非齐次范数||u||_{L^q_w}+||A^θ u||_{L^q_w}与齐次范数||A^θ u||_{L^q_w}）。最终将齐次域D((-Δ_{q,w})^θ)识别为[L^q_w, D(-Δ_{q,w})]_θ与无散度向量场空间的交集。此外导出加权变分不等式、Stokes半群的加权L^q-L^r衰减估计及L^pσ,w上的L^p-极大正则性。

Result: 得到以下主要结果：(1) Stokes算子在加权齐次和非齐次Sobolev空间上具有有界H^∞-演算；(2) 证明了A^{it}的一致有界性；(3) 将分数幂定义域（带齐次或非齐次范数）刻画为复插值空间；(4) 识别齐次空间D((-Δ_{q,w})^θ)等于[L^q_w, D(-Δ_{q,w})]_θ与无散度向量场空间的交集；(5) 导出Stokes方程的加权变分不等式、半群的加权L^q-L^r衰减估计以及L^q_{σ,w}(Ω)上的L^p-极大正则性。

Conclusion: 本文成功将Stokes算子的H^∞-演算与插值理论从经典Sobolev空间推广到带Muckenhoupt权的齐次Sobolev空间框架，并建立了包括变分不等式、衰减估计和极大正则性在内的完整分析工具，为外部域上带权Navier-Stokes方程的研究奠定了坚实的函数空间基础。

Abstract: We consider the Stokes operator $A$ on smooth exterior domains $Ω$ of $\mathbb{R}^n$ in homogeneous Sobolev spaces $\widehat H^{κ,q}_w(Ω)$ with radially symmetric Muckenhoupt weights $w\in \mathscr A_q$. A fundamental property is the existence of a bounded $\mathscr H^\infty$-calculus of the Stokes operator on weighted nonhomogeneous and homogeneous $L^q$ Sobolev spaces. This property implies the existence of uniformly bounded purely imaginary powers $A^{it}$, $t\in\mathbb{R}$, and the characterization of domains of fractional powers $A^θ$ equipped with nonhomogeneous ($\| u\|_{L^q_w} + \|A^θu\|_{L^q_w}$) as well as homogeneous norm ($\|A^θu\|_{L^q_w}$) as complex interpolation spaces. The final aim is the identification with homogeneous spaces $\widehat{\mathcal D}((-Δ_{q,w})^θ) = [L^q_{w},\widehat{\mathcal D}(-Δ_{q,w})]_θ$ intersected by a space of solenoidal vector fields. Moreover, we obtain weighted variational inequalities for weak solutions of the Stokes equations, weighted $L^q$-$L^r$ decay estimates of the Stokes semigroup and $L^p$-maximal regularity on $L^q_{σ,w}(Ω)$.

</details>


### [193] [Absence of the Lavrentiev phenomenon for a general class of parabolic double phase problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29085)
*Youngchae Kim,Jehan Oh*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明了一类具有Orlicz增长的抛物型双相泛函不存在Lavrentiev现象，即任何有限能量映射都可以被光滑函数局部逼近而不损失能量，将双相理论从幂次增长推广到广义Young函数框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lavrentiev现象的存在与否是变分问题中的核心问题之一。此前关于抛物双相问题的研究仅限于幂次增长情形，而现实中的许多材料模型需要更一般的Orlicz增长。因此，将Lavrentiev现象的缺失性证明推广到Orlicz增长的抛物双相泛函是自然且重要的研究方向。

Method: 利用Young函数G和H满足Δ₂与∇₂条件且G≺H的结构，结合系数a(·)的连续性及G与H增长间隙、a(·)模连续性之间的平衡条件，通过构造适当的局部光滑逼近序列来证明有限能量映射可以被无能量损失地近似。

Result: 在G与H的增长间隙和a(·)的模连续性之间的适当平衡条件下，证明了一类Orlicz增长的抛物双相泛函不存在Lavrentiev现象，并将已知的抛物双相理论从幂次增长拓展到了广义Young函数框架，同时确定了问题自然的时空Orlicz能量类。

Conclusion: 该结果填补了抛物双相问题在Orlicz增长框架下的理论空白，为更广泛的非线性材料模型提供了变分分析的坚实基础，并指出了后续研究应关注的时空Orlicz能量空间。

Abstract: In this paper, we prove the absence of the Lavrentiev phenomenon for a general class of parabolic double phase functionals with Orlicz growth. The energy density is given by $$ G(|Dw|)+a(x,t)H(|Dw|), $$ where $G$ and $H$ are Young functions satisfying the $Δ_2$ and $\nabla_2$ conditions with $G\prec H$, and $a(\cdot)$ is a continuous nonnegative coefficient. Under suitable balance conditions between the growth gap of $G$ and $H$ and the modulus of continuity of $a(\cdot)$, we show that every finite-energy map can be approximated locally by smooth functions without loss of energy. The result extends the known parabolic double phase theory from power type growth to a broad Young function framework and identifies the natural space-time Orlicz energy class for the problem.

</details>


### [194] [A $C^2$-Perturbative Bernstein Theorem for Anisotropic Entire Minimal Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29117)
*Lu Chen,Jiali Lan*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该论文在维度1≤n≤7下证明了Φ-各向异性极小超曲面的Bernstein定理：当各向异性面积泛函的被积函数Φ在C^2范数下充分接近欧氏面积被积函数时，Φ-各向异性极小超曲面方程的所有整体光滑解u均为仿射函数。这解决了Mooney和Yang提出的C^2整体图版本的各向异性Bernstein问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Bernstein定理断言在全空间上满足极小曲面方程的整体解必为仿射函数。Mooney和Yang在2024年提出了各向异性版本的Bernstein问题，该问题在一般各向异性被积函数下是否成立尚不清楚。本文旨在解决这一C^2整体图版本的猜想，填补各向异性极小曲面理论中的空白。

Method: 证明采用紧性-刚性（compactness-rigidity）论证框架，结合Figalli在2017年建立的规则性（regularity）定理。基本思路是利用Φ在C^2意义下充分接近欧氏面积被积函数这一条件，通过紧性论证将各向异性情形归约到经典Bernstein定理的刚性结论。

Result: 在维度1≤n≤7内，证明了Φ-各向异性极小超曲面方程的所有整体光滑解u都是仿射函数，前提是Φ在C^2范数下充分接近欧氏面积被积函数。这一结果完全解决了Mooney和Yang提出的C^2整体图版本的各向异性Bernstein问题。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了各向异性极小曲面在欧氏面积被积函数C^2扰动下仍保持Bernstein性质，其维度限制1≤n≤7与经典Bernstein定理的最优维度一致。这一结果也为更一般各向异性Bernstein问题的研究提供了方法论的突破。

Abstract: We prove a Bernstein theorem for $Φ$-anisotropic minimal hypersurfaces in dimensions $1\leq n\leq 7$ that the only entire smooth solutions $u$ of $Φ$-anisotropic minimal hypersurfaces equation are affine functions provided the anisotropic area functional integrand $Φ$ is sufficiently $C^{2}$--close to the Euclidean area integrand. This settles the $C^2$ entire--graph version of anisotropic Bernstein problem posed by Mooney and Yang \cite{MooneyYang2024}, and the proof uses a compactness--rigidity argument combined with Figalli's regularity theorem established in \cite{Figalli2017}.

</details>


### [195] [uniqueness and asymptotic behavior of the stationary Navier-Stokes equations in a slab](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29138)
*Han Li,Jingwen Han*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了平板域中无滑移边界条件下定常Navier-Stokes方程的解的唯一性与渐近行为，在给定的外力和小Poiseuille流假设下，证明了H1解的存在性，并获得了远离边界处围绕Poiseuille流的点态衰减估计；当外力足够小时，解被证明是唯一的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定常Navier-Stokes方程在无界域（如平板域）中的解的存在性、唯一性和渐近行为是流体力学中的核心问题。平板域中的Poiseuille流作为经典解，其扰动解的长时间行为与唯一性对理解管道流动的稳定性具有重要意义。

Method: 证明的关键在于两个方面：一是对截断域中解的Dirichlet积分进行估计，利用截断技术处理无界域带来的困难；二是利用Stokes正则性估计来提升解的 regularity，从而完成存在性、衰减估计和唯一性的证明。

Result: 在给定的外力和小Poiseuille流假设下，证明了H1解的存在性；得到了解在无穷远处围绕Poiseuille流的点态衰减率；在外力充分小的条件下，证明了解的唯一性。

Conclusion: 该工作为平板域中定常Navier-Stokes方程的解提供了一个完整的存在性-唯一性-渐近行为的理论框架，所发展的截断域Dirichlet积分估计与Stokes正则性估计方法可推广至其他无界域问题。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the uniqueness and asymptotic behavior of the stationary Navier-Stokes equations in a slab domain with no-slip boundary conditions. Specifically, under the given external force and the small Poiseuille flow assumptions, we prove that there is an H1 solution, and obtain the pointwise decay around the Poiseuille flow at far field. Furthermore, if the force is small, the solution is shown to be unique. The key point of the proof is the estimate of the Dirichlet integral of the solutions in the truncated domains and the Stokes regularity estimates.

</details>


### [196] [Exact Rayleigh Reduction for Direct Detection of Turning Bifurcations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29150)
*Yavdat Il'yasov*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种精确的Rayleigh约化方法，用于非线性参数相关方程中转向分岔的直接构造与检测。通过沿一参数变换轨道构造广义Rayleigh泛函，使其成为真实解分支的精确参数律，从而将转向点识别转化为一维Rayleigh轮廓的非退化临界点问题。对于Kirchhoff方程，该方法导出了内部分岔的普适三分类判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义Rayleigh泛函通常仅提供解的必要标量约束，无法精确刻画解分支的参数行为。作者旨在建立一种既能直接构造转向分岔、又能精确检测其存在性的框架，并希望将其应用于Kirchhoff方程以获得可解析描述的全局分岔结构。

Method: 核心方法包括：(1) 引入精确Rayleigh约化，沿一参数变换轨道u=G_s(φ)构造广义Rayleigh泛函，使其成为精确参数律λ=R(G_s(φ))；(2) 将转向点识别为Rayleigh剖面的非退化临界点，利用分支切线自动生成状态线性化的核方向；(3) 在Fredholm、核简单性及参数横截性假设下证明检测点为简单折叠点；(4) 对Kirchhoff方程应用振幅缩放（有界域）和空间伸缩（R^N）构造显式精确分支及分岔值层级。

Result: 对广义Kirchhoff律M_b(A)=a+bA^θ，证明了分支几何由维度-齐次性指标θ(N-2)-2控制：当且仅当θ(N-2)>2时出现内点转向分岔。这一结果独立于具体Berestycki-Lions非线性，给出了普适的分岔三分类（无内部分岔/临界阈值/存在内部分岔），并生成了可数的转向值层级。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了几何变换对称性与参数化分岔理论之间的桥梁，将转向分岔的检测化归为一维Rayleigh剖面的临界点分析，既提供了理论判据又给出了可显式计算的构造方法，为Kirchhoff型方程的分岔分析提供了统一且不依赖具体非线性形式的普适框架。

Abstract: We introduce an exact Rayleigh reduction for the direct construction and detection of turning bifurcations in nonlinear parameter-dependent equations. A generalized Rayleigh functional ordinarily provides only a scalar necessary constraint on solutions. We show that, along a suitable one-parameter transformation orbit $u=\mathcal G_sφ$, it can instead become the exact parameter law $λ=\mathcal R(\mathcal G_sφ)$ of a genuine solution branch. Turning points are then found from nondegenerate critical points of this one-dimensional Rayleigh profile. The branch tangent automatically yields a kernel direction of the state linearization, and under the usual Fredholm, kernel-simplicity, and parameter-transversality assumptions the detected point is a simple fold.
  For Kirchhoff equations, amplitude scaling on bounded domains and spatial dilation on $\mathbb R^N$ yield explicit exact branches, global parameter thresholds, and countable hierarchies of turning values. For the generalized Kirchhoff law $M_b(A)=a+bA^θ$, the branch geometry is governed by the dimension--homogeneity index $θ(N-2)-2$: an interior turning bifurcation occurs exactly when $θ(N-2)>2$. This gives a universal bifurcation trichotomy that is independent of the particular Berestycki--Lions nonlinearity.

</details>


### [197] [Apriori Estimates and Nonexistence Results for Some Quasilinear Problems in a Half-Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29155)
*Evgeny Galakhov,Olga Salieva*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文针对半空间中的拟线性椭圆问题，建立了正解的先验估计，并利用这些估计及其他技术推导出相应Dirichlet问题的新不存在性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在半空间上研究拟线性椭圆问题正解的存在性与不存在性，是该领域的重要课题。已有结果尚不完善，需要建立更有效的估计方法以获得新的不存在性结论。

Method: 首先建立半空间中拟线性椭圆问题正解的先验（apriori）估计；然后结合先验估计与其他分析技术（如比较原理、能量方法等），推导Dirichlet问题解的不存在性结果。

Result: 获得了半空间中拟线性椭圆问题正解的先验估计，并据此推导出对应Dirichlet问题新的不存在性结论。

Conclusion: 通过先验估计与其他技术的结合，作者在半空间拟线性椭圆问题的正解研究中取得了新进展，获得了此前未知的不存在性结果。

Abstract: We obtain apriori estimates for positive solutions of some quasilinear elliptic problems in a half-space. Using these estimates and other techniques, we derive new nonexistence results for the respective Dirichlet problems.

</details>


### [198] [Two-point modulus for the logarithmic p-flux of the first Dirichlet eigenfunction of the p-Laplacian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29195)
*Rui Chen*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了有界凸域上p-Laplacian第一正Dirichlet特征函数的对数p-通量X_Ω的两点估计。作者证明了在区间和球上对所有p>1有锐利的两点估计（球上的径向模严格大于一维模），但在一维模在一般凸域上对所有p≠2均失效，分别通过边界平坦区域附近的边界行为（1<p<2）和薄矩形极限行为（p>2）构造反例。最后通过对称导数分解揭示了p=2、一维与径向对称性的特殊角色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当p=2时，log u的锐利凹度模是Andrews和Clutterbuck证明基本间隙猜想的关键。本文旨在探讨对p≠2是否存在类似的一维模（用对数p-通量替换∇log u），从而理解该模是否在非线性情形下仍然成立。

Method: （1）在区间和球上直接证明对所有p>1的锐利两点估计，并计算球上的精确径向模；（2）对1<p<2，利用边界平坦部分的边界行为构造反例，并通过光滑凸逼近保持反例；（3）对p>2，使用薄矩形区域推导u_ε(x,0)→(cosπx)^{2/p}的极限行为及重标度第一特征值的二阶展开；（4）将非线性对数通量的对称导数沿-log u水平集做分解，揭示切向变分如何将log-凹性与非线性通量的单调性分离。

Result: （1）区间和球上对所有p>1均存在锐利两点估计；但球上的锐利径向模严格大于对应的一维模。（2）在N≥2的凸域上，对所有p≠2一维模一般失效。（3）对称导数分解解释了p=2、一维和径向对称性在该问题中的特殊地位。

Conclusion: b>1，对数p-通量的两点估计确实存在，但一维模作为p=2情形的推广在p≠2时并不成立。该结果精确刻画了这一非线性推广的边界，并通过分解阐明了问题的结构根源。

Abstract: We study two-point estimates for the logarithmic \(p\)-flux \[ X_Ω:= |\nabla\log u|^{p-2}\nabla\log u, \] where \(u\) is the positive first Dirichlet eigenfunction of the \(p\)-Laplacian on a bounded convex domain \(Ω\). When \(p=2\), this reduces to \(\nabla\log u\), whose sharp modulus of concavity is a key ingredient in the proof of the fundamental gap conjecture by Andrews and Clutterbuck. We investigate whether an analogous one-dimensional modulus persists for \(p\neq2\). We first prove sharp two-point estimates on intervals and balls for every \(p>1\). On balls, the sharp radial modulus is strictly larger than the corresponding one-dimensional modulus. In contrast, in dimensions \(N\ge2\), the one-dimensional modulus fails in general on convex domains for every \(p\neq2\). For \(1<p<2\), the counterexample is obtained from the boundary behavior near a flat portion of the boundary and is preserved under smooth convex approximation. For \(p>2\), we use thin rectangles and derive the limiting behavior \(u_\varepsilon(x,0)\rightarrow(\cosπx)^{2/p}\), together with a second-order expansion of the rescaled first eigenvalue. Finally, we decompose the symmetric derivative of the nonlinear logarithmic flux relative to the level sets of \(-\log u\). The decomposition shows how the tangential variation of \(|\nabla\log u|\) separates log-concavity from monotonicity of the nonlinear flux, and explains the special roles of \(p=2\), one dimension, and radial symmetry.

</details>


### [199] [Stable $C^{7/9}$ cusp formation for the Novikov equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29236)
*Yunjoo Kim,Dowan Koo,Bongsuk Kwon,Wanyong Shim*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文在Novikov方程（三次非线性Camassa-Holm型方程）中建立了稳定的尖点形成机制，证明了存在一个光滑初始数据的开集，其首次梯度爆破产生具有$C^{7/9}$精确Hölder正则性的尖点。这一指数由非局部项与非线性代数结构之间的耦合决定，不同于Camassa-Holm和Hunter-Saxton方程中的$3/5$指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解非局部波破碎问题中尖点的精确正则性如何被确定，特别是区分非局部结构与非线性结构各自的作用；同时解决Novikov方程因其三次非线性特征导致朴素高频极限缺乏自相似主导流这一核心困难。

Method: 引入Galilei型变量变换（围绕非零背景），将三次非线性的高成本身转化为二次Hunter-Saxton型主导方程；利用其自相似轮廓确定$C^{7/9}$尖点，并在调制相似变量中对非局部项和三次余项进行微扰控制。

Result: 建立了Novikov方程首次梯度爆破产生稳定尖点的严格定理，确定尖点的Hölder指数为$7/9$，并证明了该指数不同于CH和HS方程中的$3/5$，展现了非局部波破碎中尖点正则性的多样性。

Conclusion: 在非局部波破碎问题中，尖点的精确正则性并非由非局部或非线性结构单独决定，而是由两者耦合选择；Novikov方程通过Galilei变换揭示的二次主导结构，产生了独特的$C^{7/9}$尖点。守恒的$H^1$-型量排除了Burgers型$C^{1/3}$尖点，但更精细的指数由方程具体结构决定。

Abstract: We establish stable cusp formation for the Novikov equation, a cubically nonlinear Camassa--Holm-type equation. We identify an open set of smooth initial data for which the first gradient blow-up produces a cusp with sharp Hölder regularity $C^{7/9}$. This result shows that, in nonlocal wave-breaking problems, the sharp regularity of the cusp is not determined by the nonlocal or nonlinear structure alone. While the conserved $H^1$-type quantity excludes the $C^{1/3}$ cusp associated with Burgers-type gradient blow-up, the precise Hölder exponent is selected by the coupling between the nonlocal term and the algebraic structure of the nonlinearity. In the Novikov equation, this coupling yields the exponent $7/9$, rather than the $3/5$ exponent known for the Camassa--Holm and Hunter--Saxton equations. The main difficulty is that the naive high-frequency limit retains the cubic character of the equation and therefore does not exhibit a self-similar leading flow. We overcome this by introducing a Galilean-type change of variables around a nonzero background, which reveals a quadratic Hunter--Saxton-type leading equation. Its self-similar profiles determine the $C^{7/9}$ cusp, while the nonlocal and cubic remainders are controlled perturbatively in modulated similarity variables.

</details>


### [200] [Global Existence of classical solutions to 3D nonlinear Klein-Gordon equations with low-regularity initial data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29250)
*Wei Xu,Yi Zhou*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究三维空间非线性Klein-Gordon方程Cauchy问题的整体存在性，在经典局部存在性的正则性框架内，通过高阶与低阶能量估计证明了全局解的存在。核心依赖Georgiev-Popivanov的降次技巧（将二次非线性化为三次项加可控项）以及新建立的无需尺度算子的尖锐Klainerman-Sobolev型不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性Klein-Gordon方程在三维空间的整体存在性通常需要较高的正则性假设，本文旨在将正则性要求降至经典局部存在性理论的框架内（即更低的Sobolev阶），从而缩小局部与整体存在性之间的正则性差距，这是非线性双曲型方程领域的经典且重要的问题。

Method: 采用高阶与低阶能量估计相结合的策略。第一，利用Georgiev与Popivanov的工作，将二次非线性项化简为三次项加上可由ghost能量控制的可控项，从而确保低阶估计的成立。第二，建立了一个不含尺度算子（scaling operator）的尖锐Klainerman-Sobolev型不等式，该不等式可提供直到二阶导数的时间衰减，随后通过分部积分技巧控制高阶估计中出现的导数损失项。

Result: 在三维空间维度，于经典局部存在的正则性框架内证明了非线性Klein-Gordon方程Cauchy问题的整体存在性。二阶以内的导数获得了足够的时间衰减，高阶导数损失项得到了有效控制。

Conclusion: 本文通过结合Georgiev-Popivanov降次方法与新建立的Klainerman-Sobolev型不等式（免去尺度算子），成功在较低正则性下完成了非线性Klein-Gordon方程三维Cauchy问题整体存在性的证明，拓展了该问题在正则性临界/次临界情形下的已知结果。

Abstract: This paper studies global existence for the Cauchy problem of nonlinear Klein-Gordon equations in three space dimensions, strictly within the regularity regime of classical local existence. We prove it via higher-order and lower-order energy estimates. The proof relies on two key ingredients. The first is due to the work of Georgiev and Popivanov, which reduces quadratic nonlinearities to cubic terms plus ghost-energy-controllable terms, securing lower-order estimates. The second is a sharp Klainerman-Sobolev-type inequality without the scaling operator, established herein, which yields enough time decay for derivatives up to second order; integration by parts then controls derivative loss terms in the higher-order estimates.

</details>


### [201] [Global small data radial symmetric solutions of 3D semilinear Euler-Poisson-Darboux equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29258)
*Qianqian Li,Qiao Xin,Huicheng Yin*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究三维半线性Euler-Poisson-Darboux方程的小数据解的整体存在性问题。针对临界指数p_crit(3,μ)=max{p_s(3+μ), p_f(3)}的猜想，作者在径向对称情形下，利用超几何Riemann表示和精巧的点态时空加权估计，填补了μ≥14/5且5/3<p≤1+2/μ范围内的证明空白，从而完整解决了μ≥14/5时径向对称解的整体存在性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维半线性Euler-Poisson-Darboux方程的小数据解的整体存在性在p>p_crit(3,μ)时仍是开放问题。已有工作[16]仅覆盖了p>max{5/3, 1+2/μ}且μ≥14/5的情形，剩余范围5/3<p≤1+2/μ且14/5≤μ<3尚未解决，需要填补这一空白。

Method: 利用超几何Riemann表示将解的积分表达式进行精细处理，并建立一系列精巧的点态时空加权估计（pointwise spacetime weighted estimates），在径向对称条件下证明小数据解的整体存在性。

Result: 在5/3<p≤1+2/μ且14/5≤μ<3的剩余参数范围内，证明了径向对称小数据解的整体存在性。结合[16]的工作，当μ≥14/5且p>p_crit(3,μ)=5/3时，径向对称小数据解的整体存在性问题被完全解决。

Conclusion: 对于径向对称情形且μ≥14/5，当p>5/3时，小数据解整体存在性猜想得到完整证明，即临界指数问题在径向对称框架下已闭合。

Abstract: For the 3D semilinear Euler-Poisson-Darboux equation $\square u+\fracμ{t}\partial_tu=|u|^p$, where $t\geq1$, $μ>0$ and $p>1$, it is conjectured that there is a critical exponent $p_{crit}(3,μ)=\max\{p_s(3+μ), p_f(3)\}$ with the Strauss exponent $p_s(3+μ)=\frac{μ+4+\sqrt{μ^2+16μ+32}}{2(μ+2)}$ and the Fujita exponent $p_f(3)=\frac{5}{3}$ such that when $p>p_{crit}(3,μ)$, the small data solution $u$ exists globally, otherwise, when $1<p\le p_{crit}(3,μ)$, the solution $u$ can blow up in finite time. It is pointed out that for $1<p\le p_{crit}(3,μ)$, the blowup of solution $u$ has been shown. However, it is still open for the global existence of small solution $u$ when $p>p_{crit}(3,μ)$. Note that $p_{crit}(3,μ)=p_s(3+μ)$ for $0<μ<\frac{14}{5}$ and $p_{crit}(3,μ)=p_f(3)$ for $μ\geq\frac{14}{5}$. In the recent paper [16], the authors have obtained the global small solution $u$ for $p>\max\{\frac{5}{3},1+\frac{2}μ\}$ and $μ\geq\frac{14}{5}$. In this paper, by utilizing the hypergeometric Riemann representation and establishing some delicate pointwise spacetime weighted estimates, we prove the global existence of small data radial solution $u$ in the remaining range of $\frac{5}{3}<p\leq1+\frac{2}μ$ and $\frac{14}{5}\leqμ<3$. Therefore, for the radially symmetric case and $μ\geq\frac{14}{5}$, the global existence problem of small data solution $u$ is solved when $p>p_{crit}(3,μ)=\frac{5}{3}$.

</details>


### [202] [Global recovery of Lorentzian principal geometry from the hyperbolic Dirichlet-to-Neumann map](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29332)
*Lu Chen,Yan Jiang,Hongyu Liu,Longyue Tao*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明了在有限时间区间上，Dirichlet-to-Neumann (D-to-N) 映射全局确定由波动算子主象征编码的洛伦兹度量，适用于空间维数n≥3且波速可乘性分离c(x,t)=a(x)b(t)的情形，条件为累积有效时间超过从边界到内部再返回的最大传播时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在解决波动方程反散射问题中的关键难题：如何从边界测量数据（Dirichlet-to-Neumann映射）全局恢复时空中的未知度量结构，包括未知的黎曼度量g(x)和波速c(x,t)，特别关注时间因子b(t)无需预先给定的情形。

Method: 采用基于波动算子主象征分析的方法，利用Dirichlet-to-Neumann映射在有限时间区间上的信息，结合乘性可分离波速的结构假设，通过边界保持的坐标变换和时间不变性来恢复度量。同时构造反例验证维度条件（n≥3）和可见性条件（累积时间超过最大往返时间）的最优性。

Result: 证明了在n≥3维且波速满足c(x,t)=a(x)b(t)的分离形式下，有限时间区间上的D-to-N映射全局确定整个观测柱体内的度量。数据恢复出未知的黎曼度量g(x)和波速c(x,t)，唯一性成立至边界固定且时间测量不变的坐标变换。此外，构造的例子表明维度和可见性条件对唯一性是必要条件。

Conclusion: 该结果为时空耦合波动方程的反演唯一性提供了重要进展，表明在适当的几何可见性条件下，时间因子的波速变化可由离散边界数据全局恢复，且所给条件在维度与能观性意义上不可放宽。

Abstract: We prove that the full Dirichlet-to-Neumann map on a finite time interval globally determines the Lorentzian metric \(\mathbf G_{g,c}=-c(x,t)^2\mathrm{d}t^2+g(x)\) encoded by the principal symbol of the wave operator. The result holds in spatial dimensions \(n\geq3\) for multiplicatively separable wave speeds \(c(x,t)=a(x)b(t)\), provided the accumulated effective time during the experiment exceeds the maximal travel time from the boundary to the interior and back. The data determine both the unknown Riemannian metric \(g(x)\) and the wave speed \(c(x,t)\) throughout the observation cylinder, up to a spatial change of coordinates that fixes the boundary and leaves the measured time unchanged. In particular, the temporal factor \(b\) is not prescribed but is recovered from the same measurements. We also construct several examples demonstrating that the dimensional and visibility conditions for uniqueness are sharp.

</details>


### [203] [A Liouville type rigidity result for entire solutions of the $k$-Hessian equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29392)
*Bin Wang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明了一个关于$k$-Hessian方程光滑整体解的Liouville型刚性结果，证明的关键工具是$k$-Hessian算子的一个凹性不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究$k$-Hessian方程整体解的刚性（rigidity）性质，即探究在何种条件下，光滑整体解必然取某种特殊形式（如常数解或线性解）。这类Liouville型结果在非线性偏微分方程理论中具有基础性意义。

Method: 利用$k$-Hessian算子的一个凹性（concavity）不等式作为核心工具，对$k$-Hessian方程的光滑整体解进行估计，从而推导出解的刚性结论。

Result: 建立了$k$-Hessian方程光滑整体解的Liouville型刚性定理，即满足一定条件下的光滑整体解具有刚性性质。

Conclusion: 该结果揭示了$k$-Hessian算子的凹性结构在推导解的整体刚性中的关键作用，为$k$-Hessian方程的进一步研究提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: In this note, we prove a Liouville type rigidity result for smooth entire solutions of the $k$-Hessian equation. The proof makes use of a concavity inequality for the $k$-Hessian operator.

</details>


### [204] [Global solutions to the Boussinesq equations and the planetary geostrophic approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29417)
*Ryan Budahazy,Xin Liu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究行星地转尺度下的三维Boussinesq方程，建立关于特征数的一致估计，获得无初始数据限制的全局弱解和唯一全局强解，并通过特征数趋于零严格证明行星地转近似，采用潮汐流-无潮汐流分解克服重力奇异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行星地转尺度下，浮力/重力驱动的流动接近地转平衡，这一尺度具有重要的地球物理意义。然而Boussinesq方程在这一尺度下存在由浮力/重力引起的奇异性问题，且此前求解需要限制初始数据。因此作者旨在建立无初始数据限制的全局解理论，并严格证明行星地转近似的有效性。

Method: 本文建立关于特征数（characteristic number）的一致估计（uniform estimates），在此基础上获得全局弱解和唯一全局强解。采用新颖的浮力流-无浮力流分解（buoyancy flow-free flow decomposition）来克服浮力/重力引起的奇异性。最后通过将特征数趋于零，利用已建立的紧致性（compactness）严格证明行星地转近似。

Result: 获得Boussinesq方程在行星地转尺度下的全局弱解（不受初始数据限制），获得唯一全局强解；成功将特征数趋于零，严格证明行星地转近似；证明了浮力流-无浮力流分解的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文为行星地转尺度下的Boussinesq方程建立了完整的全局适定性理论，并严格证明了行星地转近似的数学合理性，为理解大型水平空间尺度下浮力驱动流动的地转平衡行为提供了坚实的数学基础。

Abstract: We investigate the three dimensional Boussinesq equations in the planetary geostrophic scale, where the buoyancy/gravity driven flow is near geostrophic balance in the large horizontal spatial scale. By establishing the uniform (with respect to the characteristic number) estimates, we are able to obtain the global weak solutions and the unique global strong solutions to the Boussinesq equations without restriction on the initial data. Furthermore, sending the characteristic number to zero with the established compactness rigorously justifies the planetary geostrophic approximation of the Boussinesq equations. We employ the novel buoyancy flow-free flow decomposition to overcome the singularity caused by the buoyancy/gravity.

</details>


### [205] [A Riemannian Extension of a Quadrature Surface Free Boundary Problem: Stability and Optimality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29462)
*Ababacar Sadikhe Djite,Diaraf Seck*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了紧致光滑黎曼流形上的quadrature曲面自由边界问题，将其表述为涉及Laplace-Beltrami算子Dirichlet问题的形状优化问题。在适当的几何假设下，建立了问题类的紧致性、对应Dirichlet问题的稳定性（状态函数在H0^1中强收敛）、形状泛函的一阶最优性条件，以及在接触点处切向边界的第二基本形式和平均曲率的黎曼比较原理，将Euclidean框架严格推广到紧致黎曼流形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将quadrature曲面自由边界问题从Euclidean空间推广到紧致黎曼流形。该推广在数学上具有基础性和挑战性，因为黎曼流形的曲率、几何结构使得原本在平坦空间中成立的紧致性、稳定性等性质需要新的几何假设和分析工具来保证。

Method: (1) 将问题表述为形状优化问题，目标函数涉及Laplace-Beltrami算子的Dirichlet问题解；(2) 对容许类施加均匀几何假设以获得紧致性；(3) 证明对应Dirichlet问题的稳定性，包括状态函数在H0^1(M)中的强收敛；(4) 基于变分分析推导一阶最优性条件；(5) 建立关于第二基本形式和平均曲率的黎曼比较原理。

Result: 在均匀几何假设下，建立了容许类紧致性；证明了Dirichlet问题族的稳定性及状态在H0^1(M)中的强收敛性；导出了形状泛函的一阶最优性条件；建立了接触点处切向边界第二基本形式与平均曲率的黎曼比较原理。

Conclusion: 这些结果严格地将quadrature曲面自由边界问题框架从Euclidean设置推广到了紧致黎曼流形，为后续在流形上进行自由边界问题的分析与数值研究奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: We study a quadrature surface free boundary problem on a smooth compact finite-dimensional Riemannian manifold $(M,g)$. The problem is formulated as a shape optimization problem involving a Dirichlet problem for the Laplace--Beltrami operator, with a geometric condition on the free boundary.
  Under suitable uniform geometric assumptions on the admissible class, we establish its compactness and prove the stability of the corresponding Dirichlet problems, including strong convergence of the associated states in $H_0^1(M)$. We derive the first-order optimality condition for the associated shape functional. Finally, we establish a Riemannian comparison principle for the second fundamental forms and the mean curvatures of tangent boundaries at a contact point. These results provide a rigorous extension of the quadrature surface free boundary framework from the Euclidean setting to compact Riemannian manifolds.

</details>


### [206] [Interior $C^{2,α}$ Regularity for the Quadratic Hessian Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29484)
*Ruosi Chen,Xingchen Zhou,Ruixuan Zhu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明了在全正分支上，具有正C^α右端的二次Hessian方程容许解的内部C^{2,α}正则性，核心工具是定量大迹传播论证和自适应Dirichlet比较方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二次Hessian方程的正则性理论是非线性偏微分方程领域的重要问题。当右端函数仅为C^α（而非光滑）且解在全正分支上时，建立内部C^{2,α}估计之前尚无一般性结果，因此需要发展新的工具来突破这一困难。

Method: 本文采用两个关键技术：(1) 定量大迹传播论证（quantitative large-trace propagation argument），用于在解的正则性层级之间进行传播估计；(2) 自适应Dirichlet比较方法（adaptive Dirichlet comparison method），通过自适应构造合适的Dirichlet问题作为比较基准来获取解的更高阶正则性。

Result: 建立了二次Hessian方程在全正分支上、右端函数为正C^α时容许解的内部C^{2,α}正则性估计。

Conclusion: 该结果填补了二次Hessian方程在低正则右端情形下内部正则性理论的空白，所提出的定量大迹传播与自适应Dirichlet比较方法为后续更一般的Hessian方程正则性研究提供了新的分析框架。

Abstract: We establish interior $C^{2,α}$ regularity for admissible solutions of the quadratic Hessian equation with positive $C^α$ right-hand side on the full positive branch. The main ingredients are a quantitative large-trace propagation argument and an adaptive Dirichlet comparison method.

</details>


### [207] [Endpoint Mapping Properties of Wave Operators for Two-Dimensional Schrödinger Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30602)
*Han Cheng,Changxing Miao,Xiaohua Yao*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文建立了二维Schrödinger算子波算子W±(H,-Δ)在端点p=1和p=∞处的尖锐映射性质，完整刻画了波算子的L^p映射行为。当零点为正则点时，波算子不是L^1和L^∞的有界算子，但满足Calderón-Zygmund类型的替代估计；当零点具有s波共振时，波算子在两个端点空间上均有界，这与n≥3维的情况形成鲜明对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维Schrödinger算子波算子的非端点L^p有界性理论已建立，但端点p=1和p=∞的行为此前尚未完全理解。为了给出波算子L^p映射性质的完整分类，需要对端点处的行为进行精确刻画，并探究零能谱配置（正则点、s波共振、p波共振、零特征值）对端点行为的影响。

Method: 针对二维情形下不同的零能谱配置进行分类讨论：（1）零为正则点时，利用Calderón-Zygmund型估计证明L^1→L^{1,∞}、H^1→L^1、L^∞→BMO映射；（2）零为第一类阈值奇点（s波共振）时，证明波算子在L^1和L^∞上有界；（3）零为p波共振时，证明端点有界性不成立；（4）零特征值情形下，通过s波和p波共振的存在性以及零能量本征函数满足的显式二阶调和矩消关系，给出端点有界性的充分必要条件。

Result: 在二维情形下，当零为正则点时波算子不有界于L^1和L^∞，但满足Calderón-Zygmund型替代估计（L^1→L^{1,∞}、H^1→L^1、L^∞→BMO）；当零为s波共振时波算子在L^1和L^∞上均有界；p波共振同时阻碍L^1和L^∞有界性；零特征值情形下端点有界性取决于s波与p波共振的存在及零能量本征函数的二阶调和矩消条件。

Conclusion: 该研究完成了二维波算子L^p映射性质的完整分类。一个关键发现是：在二维中，s波共振反而改善了波算子的端点行为（使其在L^1和L^∞上有界），这与n≥3维中只有正则情形才有利的已知结果形成鲜明对照，揭示了维度二在阈值现象上的独特性。

Abstract: We establish sharp endpoint mapping properties for the wave operators $W_\pm(H,-Δ)$ of two-dimensional Schrödinger operators $H=-Δ+V$ with real-valued decaying potentials $V$. Together with the known non-endpoint $L^p$ theory, our results give a complete classification of the $L^p$ mapping properties of the two-dimensional wave operators, and reveal an unexpected reversal of the usual threshold paradigm at the endpoints $p=1$ and $p=\infty$.
  When zero is a regular point of $H$, the wave operators fail to be bounded on $L^1(\mathbb{R}^2)$ and on $L^\infty(\mathbb{R}^2)$, but they satisfy the atural substitute estimates of Calderón--Zygmund type: $$ L^1(\mathbb{R}^2)\longrightarrow L^{1,\infty}(\mathbb{R}^2),\ \ \ \mathcal{H}^1(\mathbb{R}^2)\longrightarrow L^1(\mathbb{R}^2),\ \ \ L^\infty(\mathbb{R}^2)\longrightarrow \mathrm{BMO}(\mathbb{R}^2). $$ When zero is instead a threshold singularity of the first kind---an s-wave resonance with no other threshold obstruction, the wave operators are bounded on both endpoint spaces $L^1(\mathbb{R}^2)$ and $L^\infty(\mathbb{R}^2)$. Thus, in dimension two, an s-wave resonance improves the endpoint behavior of the wave operators, in sharp contrast with dimensions $n\ge3$, where the only regular case is the favorable one.
  We also determine the endpoint behavior in the remaining zero-energy spectral configurations of $H$. A p-wave resonance obstructs both the $L^1$- and the $L^\infty$-boundedness of the wave operators, while in the zero-eigenvalue case we obtain necessary and sufficient conditions for endpoint boundedness, expressed in terms of the presence of s- and p-wave resonances and of explicit second-order harmonic moment cancellations satisfied by the zero-energy eigenfunctions.

</details>


### [208] [Non-time-decaying global classical solutions to nonlinear wave equations in 3D under the null condition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29500)
*Zexian Zhang,Yi Zhou*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的方法来证明三维空间中满足零条件（null condition）的非线性波动方程的整体适定性。与传统依赖时间衰减的方法不同，该方法仅基于空间衰减，在经典局部存在性理论的正则性框架下给出证明，并有望应用于爱因斯坦方程的研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的整体适定性证明依赖于时间衰减技术，而本文旨在提供一种完全不同的方法——仅基于空间衰减来证明整体适定性，为问题提供新视角，并为后续研究爱因斯坦方程开辟潜在应用途径。

Method: 作者给出了一种替代性证明方法，在三维空间维度中，假设角导数具有小性（smallness of the angular derivatives），在经典局部存在性理论的正则性框架下，利用空间衰减而非时间衰减来证明非线性波动方程在零条件下的整体适定性。

Result: 成功地在经典局部存在性理论的正则性范围内，仅通过空间衰减条件证明了满足零条件的三维非线性波动方程的整体适定性。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了不同于传统时间衰减途径的新视角，并为研究爱因斯坦方程提供了潜在的应用前景，作者计划在未来的工作中进一步探索这一方向。

Abstract: We present an alternative proof of the global well-posedness of nonlinear wave equations in three spatial dimensions under the null condition, in the regularity regime of the classical local existence theory, assuming smallness of the angular derivatives. Unlike previous methods, which rely on decay in time, our approach is based solely on spatial decay. This provides a new perspective on the problem and offers potential applications to the study of Einstein's equations, which we intend to explore in future work.

</details>


### [209] [Infinite time blow-up of solutions to the classical Keller-Segel model of chemotaxis in higher dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29522)
*Yūki Naito,Takasi Senba*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了R^N空间中抛物-椭圆趋化模型的最简单形式，证明了当N≥10时，初始数据低于Chandrasekhar奇异解情况下无限时间爆破存在的优化判据，并特别展示了N≥11与N=10两种情况判据的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对于趋化模型的无限时间爆破问题，以往研究尚未完全确立在初始数据低于临界奇异解时的优化存在判据，尤其在高维空间(N≥10)中维度对爆破行为的影响机制尚不清晰，需要系统刻画。

Method: 采用变换方程研究Cauchy问题，引入解的平均质量作为关键变量，并结合Liouville方程解的渐近展开技术来分析爆破行为。

Result: 建立了N≥10时无限时间爆破存在的优化判据，并揭示了N≥11与N=10两种情况在判据上的本质区别。

Conclusion: 该研究完善了高维趋化模型中临界解附近爆破行为的理论刻画，证明了维度奇异性（N=10作为临界维度）在无限时间爆破判据中的关键作用。

Abstract: We study the simplest parabolic-elliptic model of chemotaxis in ${\bf R^N}$, and show the optimal criteria for the existence of infinite time blow-up of solutions with the initial data below the Chandrasekhar singular solution when $N \geq 10$. In particular, we exhibit the differences of the criteria between the cases $N \geq 11$ and $N = 10$. Our argument is based on the study of the Cauchy problem for the transformed equation involving the averaged mass of the solution and the asymptotic expansions of solutions to the Liouville equation.

</details>


### [210] [Remarks on the two-dimensional Cahn-Hilliard equation forced by divergence of space-time white noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29534)
*Kazuo Yamazaki*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究二维Cahn-Hilliard方程在保守形式（Kawasaki动力学）下由空间-时间白噪声的散度驱动时的全局唯一解理论。尽管启发式论证表明解是一个分布导致非线性项中的乘积未定义，但本文证明了全局时间唯一解的存在性，证明中除标准重整化程序外，主要依靠确定性分析工具并利用方程的独特结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维Cahn-Hilliard方程在由时空白噪声散度驱动（即Kawasaki保守动力学）时，其解在经典意义下是分布而非函数，这使得方程非线性项中的乘积在数学上是未定义的（ill-defined）。标准的奇性随机偏微分方程理论（如正则结构或Paracontrolled calculus）通常需要复杂的重整化技术，本文旨在寻找一种更简洁的证明路径。

Method: 证明方法包括：(1) 最小程度的标准重整化程序来处理未定义乘积问题；(2) 应用确定性分析工具（而非依赖复杂的随机分析框架）；(3) 充分利用Cahn-Hilliard方程本身的结构特性（即保守形式以及Kawasaki动力学引入的特殊结构），这一利用是证明的关键。

Result: 成功证明了该二维随机Cahn-Hilliard方程在全局时间上的唯一解存在性理论。

Conclusion: 本文的结论表明，对于由保守型噪声驱动的二维Cahn-Hilliard方程，可以借助方程自身的独特结构（而无需引入过重的随机分析机器）配合最小限度的重整化技术，即可建立全局时间唯一解理论，为后续研究该类奇性随机偏微分方程提供了新的证明思路。

Abstract: We consider the two-dimensional Cahn-Hilliard equation forced by divergence of space-time white noise that represents Kawasaki dynamics in conservative form. The standard heuristic argument shows that the solution is a distribution and thus the product within the nonlinear term is ill-defined. We prove the global-in-time unique solution theory. Besides a minimum amount of the standard renormalization procedure to deal with ill-defined products, our proof consists of applications of deterministic analysis tools and taking advantage of the unique structure of the equation, which is crucial.

</details>


### [211] [Time-modulated refocusing in inhomogeneous medium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29542)
*Hongyu Liu,Wei Wu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文针对非均匀耗散介质中瞬时时间镜像无法产生平衡点聚焦的问题，提出了一种微局部选择的反向传播横磁分量的方法，通过短脉冲调制的薄壳实现波的回溯，并通过归一化方向能量设计物理上可实现的调制来均衡方向相关增益，最终实现焦点尺度的前导轮廓与方向衰减无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非均匀耗散介质中，不同射线经历不同的往返衰减，导致瞬时时间镜像虽能追溯射线路径却无法产生平衡的点聚焦。这一缺陷限制了时间镜像技术在损耗介质中的实际应用，因此需要在存在欧姆导电性和几何畸变的条件下实现聚焦。

Method: 本文采用Maxwell-Debye方程对非均匀Debye介质中薄壳的短脉冲调制作精确分解，隔离脉冲生成的返回场；利用半经典模式约简得到标量返回算子，其主符号将时间脉冲响应、壳调制和累积材料损耗分离。通过预调制测量和已知无损耗反向传播算子计算归一化方向能量，其倒数用于构造平滑、非负、物理上可容许的调制函数。

Result: 几何畸变在出射和返回分支间相互抵消，而物理衰减保留。归一化方向能量与返回符号具有相同的前导衰减因子，其倒数构造的调制可在相对O(h)误差内均衡选定源侧增益。该调制下，点源生成的场具有与方向衰减无关的焦点尺度前导轮廓，焦点窗口内最大位移不超过O(h^2)，空间半振幅面积为O(h^2)。

Conclusion: 通过不依赖完整衰减模型的归一化方向能量法，成功在非均匀耗散介质中实现了时间镜像的聚焦，将几何畸变与物理衰减的影响解耦，证明了只需预调制测量即可构造有效的增益均衡调制。

Abstract: In an inhomogeneous dissipative medium, different rays experience different round-trip attenuation, so an instantaneous time mirror may retrace rays without producing a balanced point focus. We solve this problem for a microlocally selected back-propagating transverse-magnetic component generated by a short pulsating modulation of a thin shell surrounding an inhomogeneous Debye medium with Ohmic conductivity. An exact decomposition of the Maxwell--Debye field isolates the pulse-generated return, and a semiclassical mode reduction yields a scalar returned operator whose principal symbol separates the temporal pulse response, shell modulation, and accumulated material loss. Our analysis shows that geometric distortion and amplitude attenuation are both involved in wave propagation, but the geometric distortion cancels between the outgoing and returning branches, whereas physical attenuation remains. A normalized directional energy, computed from pre-modulation measurements using the known lossless backward propagator, contains the same leading attenuation factor as the returned symbol. Its reciprocal defines a smooth, nonnegative, physically admissible modulation that equalizes the selected source-side gain up to a relative $O(h)$ error without requiring a complete attenuation model. With such a modulation, the field generated by a point source has a focal-scale leading profile independent of directional attenuation, with a unique maximum at the source and coherent return time. In every fixed focal window, the maximum is displaced by at most $O(h^2)$ in space and time, and the spatial half-amplitude area is $O(h^2)$.

</details>


### [212] [Critical Morrey Rigidity and Removable Singularities for Five-Dimensional Stationary Navier-Stokes Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29664)
*Yubo Chen,Wendong Wang,Xiao Wang,Guoxu Yang,Jianbo Yu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明了五维平稳Navier-Stokes方程的临界Morrey刚性定理：在R^5\{0}上的光滑解若满足速度立方Morrey范数的尺度不变有界条件（sup R^{-2}∫_{B_R}|u|^3 dx < ∞），则必恒为零（压力可差常数）。该结果将高维刚性理论中点态Type-I控制替换为仅依赖速度的尺度不变平均条件，允许空间集中。作为应用，得到五维可去奇点判别准则及四维相应的速度立方Morrey刚性定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维（≥4）平稳Navier-Stokes方程的刚性理论此前依赖点态Type-I控制，条件过强。本文旨在用更弱的、仅依赖速度且尺度不变的Morrey型平均条件替代点态控制，从而推广刚性定理，并为可去奇点准则提供更优的充分条件（以有界性替代小性假设）。

Method: 本文提出了一种不依赖点态压力估计和经典法向迹的"弱头压力机制"。具体步骤包括：(1) 从速度场重构正则压力；(2) 推导正头压力的重整化不等式；(3) 引入两个单调径向通量；(4) 利用环形能量估计、适当弱紧性以及膨胀/收缩极限（blow-up和blow-down）来确定端点通量并迫使刚性结论成立。

Result: 获得以下结果：(1) 五维临界Morrey刚性定理：满足立方速度Morrey条件的光滑解必为零；(2) 五维可去奇点准则：若适当弱解除一点外光滑，且尺度不变Dirichlet能量或立方速度Morrey量在该点附近有界，则奇点可去；(3) 四维相应的速度立方Morrey刚性定理，通过不同的有限能量论证证明。

Conclusion: 本文用弱平均条件替代了点态Type-I控制，建立了更一般的五维刚性理论，并将经典稳态正则性准则中的小性假设在孤立奇点类中替换为有界性，推动了高维Navier-Stokes方程奇点分析的发展。

Abstract: We prove a critical Morrey rigidity theorem for the five-dimensional stationary Navier--Stokes equations. More precisely, every smooth solution on $\mathbb R^5\setminus\{0\}$ satisfying \[ \sup_{R>0}R^{-2}\int_{B_R}|u|^3\,dx<\infty \] is identically zero, up to an additive constant in the pressure. This replaces the pointwise Type-I control in the known higher-dimensional rigidity theory by a velocity-only, scale-invariant averaged condition that allows spatial concentration. The proof develops a weak head-pressure mechanism that does not rely on pointwise pressure estimates or classical normal traces. We reconstruct a canonical pressure from the velocity, derive a renormalized inequality for the positive head pressure, and introduce two monotone radial fluxes. Annular energy estimates, suitable-weak compactness, and blow-up and blow-down limits are then used to identify the endpoint fluxes and force rigidity.
  As an application, we obtain a removable-singularity criterion in dimension five: if a suitable weak solution is smooth away from one point and either its scale-invariant Dirichlet energy or its cubic velocity Morrey quantity remains bounded near that point, then the singularity is removable. Thus, within the isolated-singularity class, the smallness assumption in the classical stationary regularity criterion is replaced by boundedness. We also prove the corresponding velocity-only cubic Morrey rigidity theorem in dimension four by a different finite-energy argument.

</details>


### [213] [Plasmon poles for the linearized Coulomb--Hartree--Fock equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29671)
*Yuya Dan*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究R³空间中带物理Coulomb相互作用（直接与交换通道，交换耦合参数η）的含时Hartree-Fock方程，在紧动量支集均匀平衡态附近的线性化问题。作者证明在交换通道存在时，plasma极点仍然存在、保持单重纯虚根并关于η实解析；每个极点给出精确无阻尼模式和闭式单位密度本征函数；密度Green函数分裂为无阻尼正弦波与剩余项，满足t^{-3/2}色散衰减和Strichartz估计；通过Ward型消去项证明交换对plasma能隙的一阶修正为零，并给出刚度修正闭式公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Nguyen和You已证无非交换项时存在低于生存阈值κ₀的两个纯虚plasma极点±iτ₀(|k|)，但现有非线性Hartree-Fock理论要求短程直接相互作用和光滑小交换核，无法覆盖当前的Coulomb设置。本文旨在填补这一空白，证明Coulomb交换通道下极点行为的鲁棒性，并量化交换效应对plasma色散的影响。

Method: 在紧支集动量带0<k₋≤|k|≤k₊<κ₀上，对小|η|无条件证明：极点保持单重纯虚根且关于η实解析；一阶位移τ_X分解为显式静态自能项与动态顶角修正；每个极点给出闭式单位密度本征函数；通过Ward型消去证明|τ_X(r)|≤Cr²（r→0），使plasma能隙一阶不变；用C⁹光滑Fermi球数值实验验证分析结果。

Result: （1）Coulomb交换下极点持续存在、单重纯虚、关于η实解析；（2）因果密度Green函数分裂为显式无阻尼正弦波与Laplace变换在极点附近全纯的剩余项；（3）plasma通道满足一致t^{-3/2}色散衰减与端点Strichartz估计；（4）Ward型消去给出自能与顶角项在r=0附近抵消至O(r²)；（5）数值验证自能与顶角项在计算范围内抵消至1.2%精度，交换在约0.7κ₀处最强烈地软化色散。

Conclusion: 交换相互作用对plasma极点的一阶位移具有严格的结构性质：自能与顶角修正通过Ward型恒等式精确抵消，使Coulomb交换不改变plasma能隙至一阶，仅产生刚度修正。数值结果与理论预见高度一致，确认了物理Coulomb交换下Hartree-Fock线性化理论的适用性与量化的色散软化效应。

Abstract: We study the time-dependent Hartree--Fock equation in $\R^3$ with physical Coulomb interactions in both direct and exchange channels, with exchange coupling $η$, linearized about homogeneous equilibria $γ_f=g(-i\nabla)$ having compact momentum support. Nguyen and You showed that, without exchange, two purely imaginary plasmon poles $\pm iτ_0(|k|)$ exist below a survival threshold $κ_0>0$. Existing nonlinear Hartree--Fock theory requires a short-range direct interaction and a smooth small exchange kernel, excluding the present Coulomb setting. On every compact band $0<k_-\le|k|\le k_+<κ_0$, we prove unconditionally for Coulomb exchange that, for small $|η|$, the poles persist, remain simple and purely imaginary, and depend real-analytically on $η$. Their first-order shift $τ_X$ splits into explicit static self-energy and dynamic vertex corrections. Each pole yields an exact undamped mode with a closed-form unit-density eigenfunction, embedded in the fiber generator's essential spectrum. The causal density Green function splits into an explicit undamped sine wave and a remainder whose Laplace transform is holomorphic near the poles, while the plasmon channel satisfies uniform $t^{-3/2}$ dispersive decay and endpoint Strichartz estimates. A first-order Ward-type cancellation gives $|τ_X(r)|\le Cr^2$ down to $r=0$; hence exchange leaves the plasma gap unchanged to first order, and we obtain a closed formula for the stiffness correction. Numerics for a $C^9$ smoothed Fermi ball support the analysis: self-energy and vertex terms cancel within $1.2\%$ on the computed range, and exchange softens the dispersion most strongly near $0.7\,κ_0$.

</details>


### [214] [Energy identity for Intrinsic Stationary Biharmonic Mappings into Homogeneous Spaces in Supercritical Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29673)
*Chang-Yu Guo,Yu-Ting Kang,Ming-Lun Liu,Wen-Juan Qi*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了超临界维度下到齐次空间的内蕴双调和映射的能量恒等式，将Hornung-Moser在临界维度上的结果推广到超临界情形，证明策略类似于Lin-Rivière的方法，关键工具是利用到齐次空间的内蕴双调和映射的守恒律来推导映射的更高正则性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的能量恒等式结果仅覆盖临界维度（Hornung-Moser, 2012），而超临界维度下的情况尚未解决，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用Lin-Rivière在2002年提出的证明策略，核心创新在于建立到齐次空间的内蕴双调和映射的守恒律，并利用该守恒律获得映射的高阶正则性。

Result: 在超临界维度下建立了内蕴双调和映射到齐次空间的能量恒等式，推广了Hornung-Moser在临界维度上的相应结果。

Conclusion: 本文成功将能量恒等式从临界维度推广到超临界维度，为超临界情形下调和映射/双调和映射的紧性和正则性理论提供了重要工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider energy identity for intrinsic stationary biharmonic maps into homogeneous spaces in supercritical dimensions, extending the corresponding result of Hornung-Moser [Anal. PDE. 2012] in critical dimension. The proof follows a similar strategy as that of Lin-Rivière [Duke Math. J. 2002]. A key ingredient is a conservation law for intrinsic biharmonic maps into homogeneous spaces, which allow us to derive higher regularity of the map.

</details>


### [215] [Existence of Periodic Solutions to Steady Viscous Burgers Equation with a General Force](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29704)
*Yuhan Cao,Quansen Jiu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文针对带外力的粘性稳态Burgers方程，利用形式渐近展开构造周期解，从一般解u₀（满足无粘稳态Burgers方程u₀u₀x=f）出发，证明了当粘度足够小时存在C²周期解，其与有限阶渐近展开的误差为O(ε^{n+1})，相比Jauslin-Kreiss-Moser的结果提供了一种新方法，且收敛阶更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者前期工作从特定的u₀=-(2+cos x)出发构造了周期解，本文旨在将该结果推广到满足无粘稳态Burgers方程的一般u₀和外力f，并希望相比已有方法（如Jauslin-Kreiss-Moser的结果）提供更优的收敛速率和新的构造途径。

Method: 基于形式展开u^ε=u₀+εu₁+⋯+εⁿuₙ+⋯构造渐近解，利用关于粘度的均匀有界性条件，通过严格的解析验证证明存在ε₀>0（依赖n），使得对任意0<ε<ε₀，粘性稳态Burgers方程存在C²周期解，且满足与展开式的误差界|u^ε−Σ|≤Cε^{n+1}，其中常数C依赖n但不依赖ε。

Result: 证明了对于一般满足u₀u₀x=f的u₀和f，存在ε₀>0，使得当0<ε<ε₀时粘性稳态Burgers方程存在C²周期解，且与n阶渐近展开的误差为Cε^{n+1}量级。该解在粘度趋于零时收敛到无粘方程的解，且收敛阶可达高阶。

Conclusion: 本文将前期基于特定解的工作推广到一般情形，建立了从无粘解出发构造粘性稳态Burgers方程周期解的一般框架，提供了比Jauslin-Kreiss-Moser方法更新的构造途径，并获得了更高阶的收敛速率。

Abstract: In this paper, we will construct periodic solutions to the viscous steady Burgers equation with an external force $f(x)$, based on the following formal expansion $u^\varepsilon(x)=u_0(x)+\varepsilon u_1(x)+\cdots+\varepsilon^n u_n(x)+\cdots$, where $\varepsilon \ge 0$ represents the viscosity and $u_0(x)$ is a solution to non-viscous steady Burgers equation with the external force $f(x)$. We will focus on the solutions which are uniformly bounded with respect to the viscosity. In our previous work, starting from $u_0=-(2+\cos x)$, the authors constructed the periodic solutions to the viscous steady Burgers equation with the external force $f=u_0(x)u_{0x}=-2\sin x-\sin x\cos x$. In this paper, we will extend the main result obtained in our previous work and construct the solutions starting from general $u_0$ and $f$ satisfying the non-viscous steady Burgers equation $u_0(x)u_{0x}=f$. It will be shown that there exists a $\varepsilon_0>0$, which depends on $n$, such that for any $0<\varepsilon<\varepsilon_0$, the viscous steady Burgers equation with the external force $f$ has a periodic solution $u^\varepsilon(x) \in C^2([0,2π])$, satisfying $|u^\varepsilon(x)-u_0(x)-\varepsilon u_1(x)-\cdots-\varepsilon^n u_n(x)| \leq C\varepsilon^{n+1}$, where $C>0$ is a constant which depends on $n$, but is independent of $\varepsilon$. Compared with Jauslin-Kreiss-Moser's result, we present a new approach to construct periodic solutions to the viscous steady Burgers with a general external force. The constructed solutions will tend to the ones of the non-viscous Burgers equation with a sharper convergence rate (up to higher order) when the viscosity vanishes.

</details>


### [216] [A Uniqueness theorem for the Vortex-Wave system with non-constant vorticity near the point vortex](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29721)
*David Meyer*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了涡旋波系统（即包含点涡和有界涡量的二维不可压缩Euler方程）的唯一性问题。证明了当初始涡量和点涡位置满足特定的Hölder型约束条件（α>2），且涡量属于L∞时，即使速度场不是log-Lipschitz的，解仍然在全局时间上唯一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维Euler方程中，当涡量包含点涡和L∞有界部分时，速度场往往缺乏足够的正则性（如log-Lipschitz），导致经典唯一性理论失效。作者旨在突破这一正则性障碍，在更弱的速度场条件下建立唯一性结果。

Method: 利用初始涡量在点涡位置处满足的Hölder型约束条件（指数α>2），结合L∞范数假设，通过精细的解析估计在更宽泛的时空正则性框架下证明解的全局唯一性。

Result: 在初始涡量与点涡位置满足|w0(x)-w0(X0)|=O(|x-X0|^α)（α>2）且涡量属于L∞的条件下，涡旋波系统的解是全局时间唯一的，即使速度场不具有log-Lipschitz连续性。

Conclusion: 该结果表明，涡旋波系统的唯一性可以在比log-Lipschitz速度更弱的条件下成立，关键在于点涡位置处涡量的正则性约束条件。这一发现拓展了二维Euler方程唯一性理论的适用范围。

Abstract: We study the uniqueness of the vortex wave system, i.e.\ the 2D incompressible Euler equations with vorticity consisting of a point vortex and a bounded part. We show that if the initial vorticity and point vortex location $(w_0,X_0)$ satisfy a constraint of the type $|w_0(x)-w_0(X_0)|=O(|x-X_0|^α)$ for $α> 2$ and the vorticity is in $L^\infty$, then solutions are unique, globally in time, even though the velocity is not log-Lipschitz.

</details>


### [217] [Revisiting Fazly-Wei-Xu pointwise estimates for the fourth-order Hénon equation via Bernstein's technique](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30625)
*Quôc Anh Ngô,Trung Nguyen*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文针对四阶Hénon方程的有界正解，提出了一个新的点态估计证明方法。作者采用基于最大值原理的Bernstein技术，取代了原论文中Fazly、Wei和Xu使用的Moser迭代论证。新方法不仅复制了原始点态估计结果，还将其推广到临界指数情形、去除了解的有界性假设、放宽到任意σ≥0，并证明了系数2/(n-4)是最优的（尖锐的），从而首次回答了原文中提出的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fazly, Wei和Xu在2015年的论文中建立了四阶Hénon方程解的点态估计，但其证明依赖于复杂的Moser迭代论证，且存在若干限制：σ仅隐式地接近0、p需严格大于临界指数、解需有界、系数的尖锐性未验证。本文旨在提供一个更简洁的替代证明，同时克服这些限制并回答原文遗留的系数最优性问题。

Method: 本文采用Bernstein技术，结合最大值原理，通过巧妙地选取辅助函数来推导所需的点态估计。这一方法避免了Moser迭代的复杂性，并且通过辅助函数的灵活选取，能够同时处理临界指数情形(p=(n+4+2σ)/(n-4))、任意σ≥0以及无界解的情形。作者还通过构造性分析证明系数2/(n-4)的尖锐性。

Result: 作者成功建立了与原文相同的点态估计，但适用范围更广：对任意σ≥0成立、包含临界指数p=(n+4+2σ)/(n-4)、且不要求解有界。此外，证明系数2/(n-4)是尖锐的，这首次回答了Fazly-Wei-Xu论文中提出的问题。该估计还导出了R^n上具有正标量曲率和Q曲率的共形度量存在性的有趣推论。

Conclusion: 本文通过Bernstein技术与最大值原理提供了一个比Moser迭代更简单且具构造性的证明方法，不仅推广了原始点态估计的适用范围，还回答了系数最优性的开放问题，并在共形几何（正标量曲率和Q曲率）方面产生了有意义的推论。

Abstract: In a remarkable work published in Analysis and PDE 8 (2015) 1541-1563, M. Fazly, J. Wei, and X. Xu establish the following pointwise estimate \[-Δu \geq \frac 2{n-4} \frac{|\nabla u|^2}u + \sqrt{\frac 2{p+1- \frac 8{n(n-4)}}} |x|^{\frac σ2} u^\frac{p+1}2 \quad \text{in } \mathbf R^n\] for any bounded, positive, $C^4$-solution $u$ to the the fourth-order Hénon equation \[Δ^2 u = |x|^σu^p \quad \text{in } \mathbf R^n\] with $n \geq 5$, $σ\geq 0 $ but implicitly near $0$, and $p>(n+4+2σ)/(n-4)$. Their argument relies on a sophisticated iteration argument in the fashion of the standard Moser proof. In this paper, we provide an alternative approach which relies on the Bernstein technique via the maximum principle. The heart of our argument is a suitable choice of an auxiliary function allowing us not only achieving the same pointwise estimate, which still holds even for $p = (n+4+2σ)/(n-4)$, but also relaxing the boundedness of solutions. In addition, the pointwise inequality actually holds for any $σ\geq 0$. This estimate leads to an interesting consequence of the existence of conformal metric of $\mathbf R^n$ having positive scalar and $Q$ curvatures. We also show that the coefficient $2/(n-4)$ is sharp, hence providing the first answer to the question in the op. cit. paper. Our proof appears to be simpler and constructive.

</details>


### [218] [Refined decay estimates for global solutions to the rotating MHD equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29794)
*Mikihiro Fujii,Yang Li*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究三维全空间上带科里奥利力的不可压缩磁流体动力学（MHD）方程的柯西问题，针对快速旋转条件下临界Sobolev空间中大初值全局解的衰减估计问题，通过精细的色散估计给出了速度场和磁场改进的衰减率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带科里奥利力的MHD方程在大初值下已证明在快速旋转条件下临界Sobolev空间$\dot{H}^{1/2}(\mathbb{R}^3)$中存在唯一全局解，但相应的衰减估计尚未得到充分研究。已有结果仅在次临界Sobolev框架$H^s(\mathbb{R}^3)$（$1/2<s<3/2$）中由Kim获得，其衰减率有待改进。

Method: 利用精细的色散估计（dispersive estimates）技术，对速度场和磁场分别建立改进的衰减率估计，并在临界Sobolev空间框架下进行分析。

Result: 获得了速度场和磁场的改进衰减估计，其衰减率显著优于Kim（2022）在次临界Sobolev框架$H^s(\mathbb{R}^3)$（$1/2<s<3/2$）中得到的结果。

Conclusion: 在临界Sobolev空间中建立了带科里奥利力MHD方程大初值全局解的改进衰减估计，推进了该问题的已知结果。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider the Cauchy problem for the incompressible magnetohydrodynamic equations with the Coriolis force in the three-dimensional whole space. For large initial data, it is known that the Cauchy problem admits a unique global solution in critical Sobolev space $\dot{H}^{1/2}(\mathbb{R}^3)$ provided that the speed of rotation is fast enough. By using delicate dispersive estimates, we show refined decay estimates of the velocity field and magnetic field. These decay rates significantly improve the ones obtained by Kim [\emph{J. Differential Equations.}, 2022] in the subcritical Sobolev framework $H^s(\mathbb{R}^3)$ with $1/2<s<3/2$.

</details>


### [219] [Ultracontractivity of heat semigroups with non-local Robin boundary conditions via two-sided bounds for a positive eigenfunction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30763)
*Christoph Schwerdt*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究有界Lipschitz域Ω⊂ℝ^d(d>2)上，在非局部Robin边界条件下热半群的超压缩性。在边界算子B被正算子C所控制且C1∈L^∞(∂Ω)的假设下，证明了相应一致椭圆算子生成的半群是超压缩的，其从L^2(Ω)到L^∞(Ω)的范数具有短时阶t^{-d/4}。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非局部Robin边界条件下研究热半群的性质，特别是当边界算子B可能破坏半群的正性时，探究半群是否仍具有超压缩性。这一问题的核心难点在于边界条件的非局部性和非正性可能使传统的分析工具失效，需要寻找新的论证路径。

Method: 主要方法分为三个步骤：(1) 构造一个控制椭圆算子的正特征函数φ，满足0<δ≤φ≤M几乎处处成立；上界通过对幂截断和Sobolev指数的迭代获得，下界通过与Neumann半群的比较获得。(2) 利用这两侧估计得到比较半群的L^∞界。(3) 结合Nash不等式和对偶性，最终导出超压缩性。

Result: 证明了在给定假设下，满足非局部Robin边界条件的一致椭圆算子生成的热半群是超压缩的，其从L^2(Ω)到L^∞(Ω)的算子范数在短时间内的阶为t^{-d/4}。

Conclusion: 本文在允许边界算子破坏半群正性的情况下，通过构造正面有界特征函数这一关键技术步骤，建立了超压缩性结果。该结果拓展了非局部Robin边界条件下热半群理论的分析框架。

Abstract: We study heat semigroups on bounded Lipschitz domains $Ω\subset \mathbb{R}^{d}$ with dimension $d>2$ under non-local Robin boundary conditions. The boundary operator $B \in \mathcal{L}( \mathrm{L}^{2}(\partialΩ))$ is allowed to destroy the positivity of the semigroup. We assume that there exists a positive operator $C \in \mathcal{L}(\mathrm{L}^{2}(\partialΩ))$ such that $$ |Bu| \leq C|u| \quad\text{for every }u\in \mathrm{L}^{2}(\partialΩ), \qquad C{\bf 1}\in \mathrm{L}^{\infty}(\partialΩ). $$ Under this assumption, we prove that the semigroup generated by the corresponding uniformly elliptic operator is ultracontractive. More precisely, its norm from $\mathrm{L}^{2}(Ω)$ to $\mathrm{L}^{\infty}(Ω)$ has short-time order $t^{-d/4}$. The main step is the construction of a positive eigenfunction $φ$ of a dominating elliptic operator such that $$ 0 \ < \ δ\ \leq \ φ\ \leq \ M $$ almost everywhere in $Ω$. The upper bound is obtained by power truncations and an iteration of Sobolev exponents. The lower bound follows by comparison with the Neumann semigroup. The two-sided estimate gives an $\mathrm{L}^{\infty}$-bound for the comparison semigroup. Nash's inequality and duality then yield ultracontractivity.

</details>


### [220] [Boundary Harnack inequalities for Kolmogorov equations in asymptotically cylindrical Lipschitz domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29813)
*Kaj Nyström*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文建立了Kolmogorov方程在非特征内蕴Lipschitz图域上的边界Harnack不等式，突破了此前要求定义函数独立于高阶变量y_m的限制，通过引入以C^{0,1/3}_{y_m}缺陷刻画的定量渐近圆柱性，在扰动框架下证明了正解商的局部可比性、内蕴Hölder连续性以及对数商的Hölder估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前关于Kolmogorov方程边界Harnack不等式的理论要求图域的定义函数不依赖于高阶变量y_m，这一对称性限制过于严格。本文旨在通过用定量渐近圆柱性替代完全对称性，将边界Harnack理论推广到更广泛的非特征内蕴Lipschitz图域。

Method: 证明结合了局部边界估计与全局刚性论证。关键在于前向和后向参考值的均匀平衡；若在塌缩尺度上平衡失败，则通过最大尺度选择和二次重定标构造圆柱无界域上的非负多项式增长Dirichlet解。排除该构型依赖于边界能量与均值估计、多项式增长解空间的有限维性以及y_m方向的平移不变性，最终通过有限维平移表示、零点传播、特征纤维解析延拓和Tikhonov唯一性论证迫使极限解恒为零。

Result: 获得了非负解的正解商的局部可比性、内蕴Hölder连续性以及对数商的Hölder估计。条件只需y_m缺陷的尺度不变C^{0,1/3}模充分小；对C^{0,α}_{y_m}图（α>1/3）在一切足够小尺度上成立，且在临界圆柱模消失时也成立。

Conclusion: 指数1/3是最佳的，因为y_m具有齐次度三。该结果通过定量扰动框架成功将边界Harnack理论推广至非y_m-独立的一般Lipschitz图域，为Kolmogorov型退化抛物算子的边界行为研究提供了新工具。

Abstract: We establish boundary Harnack inequalities for non-negative solutions of the Kolmogorov equation \[
  \mathcal Ku=Δ_xu+x\cdot\nabla_yu-\partial_tu=0 \] in non-characteristic intrinsic Lipschitz graph domains whose defining functions may depend on the higher-order variable \(y_m\). Earlier theory required independence of \(y_m\). We replace this symmetry by quantitative asymptotic cylindricality, measured by a scale-invariant \(C^{0,1/3}_{y_m}\) defect. The perturbative comparison theorem applies whenever this defect is sufficiently small, and hence at all sufficiently small scales for \(C^{0,α}_{y_m}\) graphs with \(α>1/3\), as well as under a vanishing critical cylindrical modulus. We obtain local comparability and intrinsic Hölder continuity of quotients of positive solutions, together with a Hölder estimate for their logarithmic quotient.
  The proof combines local boundary estimates, valid without \(y_m\)-independence, with a global rigidity argument for cylindrical blow-up limits. The central issue is uniform balance of forward and backward reference values. If this balance fails at collapsing scales, maximal-scale selection and a second rescaling produce a non-negative polynomial-growth Dirichlet solution in an unbounded cylindrical domain that vanishes at the backward reference point but not at the forward one. We exclude this configuration using boundary-energy and mean-value estimates, finite-dimensionality of spaces of polynomial-growth solutions, and translation invariance in \(y_m\). The resulting finite-dimensional translation representation yields real-analytic dependence on \(y_m\). Propagation of zeros, analytic continuation along invariant fibres, and a Tikhonov uniqueness argument then force the limiting solution to vanish identically. The exponent \(1/3\) is critical because \(y_m\) has homogeneous degree three.

</details>


### [221] [Uniform-in-time relative entropy estimates for Kac's approximation of the Landau equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29815)
*Xuanrui Feng,Chenguang Liu,Zhenfu Wang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明了Kac粒子近似对三维空间均匀Landau方程（Maxwell分子情形）的均匀时间定量相对熵估计，通过相对熵方法与对数导数估计获得了阶为N^{-1/2}的有限时间归一化熵界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Landau方程是等离子体物理和动力学理论中的核心模型，其粒子近似的收敛性（传播混沌）是严格从微观到宏观推导的关键。已有的结果多为有限时间或缺乏均匀时间（uniform-in-time）的定量估计，本文旨在建立不随时间退化的收敛率。

Method: 采用相对熵方法结合对数导数估计；利用显式协方差恒等式以及一种新的对偶方法推导粒子系统的大数定律估计；最终通过将有限时间估计与代数松弛（向平衡态收敛）估计插值，得到均匀时间的传播混沌。

Result: 获得了均匀时间的定量相对熵估计，有限时间归一化熵界为N^{-1/2}的阶，并由此建立了均匀时间的传播混沌。

Conclusion: 本文为Kac粒子系统近似Landau方程提供了首个均匀时间的定量收敛率结果，对理解粒子系统的长时间行为及传播混沌提供了新的数学工具（对偶方法）。

Abstract: We prove uniform-in-time quantitative relative entropy estimates for Kac's particle approximation of the three-dimensional spatially homogeneous Landau equation for Maxwellian molecules. Using the relative entropy method and logarithmic derivative estimates, we obtain a finite-time normalized entropy bound of order $N^{-1/2}$. The proof relies on explicit covariance identities and a new law-of-large-numbers estimate for the particle system using a new duality method. The uniform-in-time propagation of chaos is derived from an interpolation with an algebraic relaxation estimate toward equilibrium.

</details>


### [222] [Polynomial mixing for stochastic viscous conservation law equation on the whole line](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29824)
*Peng Gao,Liying Li*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了全实直线上受白噪声外力驱动、具有任意小阻尼和粘性系数的粘性守恒律方程的长时间行为，建立了两种设置下的多项式混合速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究随机粘性守恒律方程的长时间行为（混合速率），是理解此类随机偏微分方程遍历性和统计性质的关键，具有重要理论意义。

Method: 基于arXiv:2408.00592中发展的抽象多项式耦合准则，结合Foiaş--Prodi估计来证明多项式混合速率。两个设置分别涉及：超二次通量配超粘性耗散（非线性增长由超粘性阶数控制，外力在一般L2基上充分非退化）；以及二次通量配标准二阶耗散（外力在某个无条件基上非退化）。

Result: 在两种设置下均建立了多项式混合速率，且两种设置都涵盖经典Burgers型非线性。

Conclusion: 该工作扩展了随机粘性守恒律方程在任意小阻尼和粘性参数下的混合性结论，通过抽象耦合准则与Foiaş--Prodi估计提供了统一的证明框架。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the long-time behavior of the viscous conservation law equation on the whole real line, subject to a white-in-time force and arbitrarily small damping and viscosity constants. We establish polynomial mixing rates in two settings: (a) a general super-quadratic flux with hyperviscous dissipation, where the growth of the nonlinearity is controlled by the order of hyperviscosity, and the force is sufficiently non-degenerate on a generic L2-basis; and (b) a general quadratic flux with standard second-order dissipation, and the force is non-degenerate on some unconditional basis. Both settings include the classical Burgers-type nonlinearity. The proof is based on an abstract polynomial coupling criterion developed in arXiv:2408.00592 and a Foiaş--Prodi estimate.

</details>


### [223] [Revisiting Picard's proof of the Picard--Weber--Weck selection theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31054)
*Yi-Sheng Lim,Timo Sprekeler,Marcus Waurick*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文在欧几里得空间中，对弱Lipschitz域上的Maxwell紧致性给出了独立且自洽的证明，绕过了微分形式、Gaffney不等式（光滑域）、Calderón延拓定理和PDE正则性理论等常用技术手段，并顺带利用Fourier基的L²完备性独立证明了经典立方体Gaffney估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重新审视Picard开创性论文[13]中的论证逻辑，寻求一个不依赖多重复杂分析工具（微分形式、Gaffney不等式、Calderón延拓定理、正则性理论）的独立证明，以简化Maxwell紧致性证明的依赖路径，使证明更自足和易于验证。

Method: 采用纯欧几里得框架，直接对弱Lipschitz域建立Maxwell紧致性证明。通过绕开微分形式设定、光滑域上的Gaffney不等式、Calderón延拓定理及PDE正则性理论等技术工具，仅借助Fourier基的L²完备性，对立方体情形给出Gaffney估计的独立证明。

Result: 获得了Maxwell紧致性在弱Lipschitz域上的独立自洽证明，减少了对外部高级定理的依赖；同时作为副产品，利用Fourier基的L²完备性给出了立方体上经典Gaffney估计的独立证明。

Conclusion: 该工作为Maxwell紧致性提供了更简洁、自足的证明路径，降低了该结果对多种复杂分析工具的依赖，使证明更易于被理解和推广到更一般的设定中。

Abstract: Revisiting the rationale provided by Picard in the seminal paper [13], we provide an independent, self-contained proof of the Maxwell compactness property for weak Lipschitz domains in the Euclidean setting, detouring technical complications like the differential forms setting, Gaffney's inequality for smooth domains, Calderon's extension theorem or regularity theory for PDEs, or systems thereof, on smooth domains that are used in this context. As a by-product we provide an independent proof of the classical Gaffney estimate for cubes using only $L^2$-completeness of the Fourier bases.

</details>


### [224] [Proof of the planar Khavinson-Shapiro conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29909)
*Feng Shao,Weicheng Zhan*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该论文证明了一个关于平面区域边界性质的猜想（Khavinson-Shapiro猜想）:如果定义在二维实数空间上的所有多项式在区域边界上与某个调和多项式一致，则该区域的边界必为椭圆，且该区域为椭圆的内部。（本文结果限于满足特定拓扑条件的平面有界区域，即边界由有限条两两不相交的Jordan曲线组成。）


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Khavinson-Shapiro猜想是调和分析与逼近论中的一个重要猜想，它刻画了调和多项式逼近与椭圆边界之间的深刻联系。该猜想在一般平面区域上尚未完全解决，本文在其一个重要情形（边界为有限条两两不相交Jordan曲线的平面有界区域）下证明了该猜想。

Method: 本文的方法涉及复分析与位势理论的工具。通过将调和多项式与解析函数联系起来，利用边界条件将多项式在边界上与调和多项式的一致性转化为对边界几何结构的约束，最终利用椭圆函数的性质或类似的分析手段来刻画边界形状。

Result: 证明了在边界为有限条两两不相交Jordan曲线的平面有界区域上，Khavinson-Shapiro猜想成立：若所有多项式在区域边界上与调和多项式一致，则边界必为椭圆，区域为椭圆内部。

Conclusion: 该结果将Khavinson-Shapiro猜想的重要情形从连通边界推广到更一般的拓扑结构（有限条Jordan曲线组成的边界），进一步验证了该猜想的正确性，为最终完全解决该猜想提供了重要的理论支撑。

Abstract: Let $Ω\subset\mathbb{R}^2$ be a bounded domain whose boundary is a finite union of pairwise disjoint Jordan curves. We prove the planar Khavinson--Shapiro conjecture in this setting: if every polynomial on $\mathbb{R}^2$ agrees on $\partialΩ$ with a harmonic polynomial, then $\partialΩ$ is an ellipse and $Ω$ is its bounded interior.

</details>


### [225] [A partial data coefficient identification inverse problem for a semilinear damped wave operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29918)
*Mandeep Kumar,Parveen Kumar,Manmohan Vashisth*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究有界域中半线性阻尼波动算子的系数识别反问题，证明可从部分Dirichlet-to-Neumann映射中唯一恢复阻尼系数、零阶线性项和幂型非线性系数，边界邻域系数已知而Neumann数据仅在小开子集上给定时仍成立唯一性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半线性波动方程的非线性系数识别是反问题领域的核心挑战，尤其是当边界数据部分缺失时；现有结果多为全边界数据情形，本文旨在突破边界数据受限条件下的唯一性难题。

Method: 以唯一延拓原理为基础，结合Fourier分析与高阶线性化技术，从部分Dirichlet-to-Neumann映射中提取不同阶的系数信息，逐项恢复阻尼系数、线性项和非线性系数。

Result: 证明了在系数于边界邻域已知、Neumann数据仅在边界任意小开子集上施加的条件下，阻尼系数、零阶线性项及幂型非线性系数均可被唯一确定。

Conclusion: 本文首次在半线性阻尼波动方程框架下，将部分数据的唯一性恢复结果推广至任意小边界开子集的场景，拓展了反问题唯一性理论的应用范围。

Abstract: This manuscript deals with a coefficient identification inverse problem for a semilinear damped wave operator in a bounded domain of $\mathbb{R}^{1+d}\ (d\geq 2)$. We establish the unique recovery of the damping coefficient, zeroth-order linear term, and the coefficient of the power-type nonlinearity from the partial Dirichlet-to-Neumann map. We investigate the corresponding uniqueness problem under the assumption that the coefficients are known in a neighborhood of the boundary, while the Neumann boundary data are prescribed only on an arbitrarily small open subset of the boundary. The analysis is largely based on the unique continuation principle, Fourier Analysis and the higher-order linearization technique.

</details>


### [226] [Failure of a BM-type inequality for the Gaussian torsional rigidity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29941)
*Xuan Hien Nguyen,Alina Stancu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该论文证明Ornstein–Uhlenbeck算子的高斯扭转刚度Tg不满足任何指数α>0的Brunn–Minkowski型不等式，即使对光滑、正曲率、中心对称的凸体也如此，从而否定了Marín Sola和Salerno提出的猜想1.4和问题(Q)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Marín Sola和Salerno提出了关于高斯扭转刚度的Brunn–Minkowski型不等式的猜想（猜想1.4），并询问该不等式是否对某个指数α成立。该论文旨在验证或否决这一猜想。

Method: 作者对单位球Ω₀在Minkowski扰动下建立了Tg的一阶下界。选择具有小扭转和大平均宽度的扰动体Ω₁时，Tg((1-t)Ω₀+tΩ₁)在一阶意义下增加。由于Minkowski组合的扭转刚度大于两个端点，因此任何指数α都无法修复不等式。

Result: 对任意指数α>0和任意维数n≥2，Brunn–Minkowski型不等式均不成立，即使限制在光滑、正曲率、中心对称的凸体上也失败，从而以否定方式回答了猜想1.4和问题(Q)。

Conclusion: 高斯扭转刚度不满足任何形式的Brunn–Minkowski型不等式，这与经典扭转刚度的行为形成鲜明对比，揭示了Ornstein–Uhlenbeck算子背景下该量的本质差异。

Abstract: Let $u$ be the torsion function for the Ornstein--Uhlenbeck operator on a bounded domain $Ω\subset \R^n$, i.e., the solution of $Δu - x \cdot \nabla u=-1$ in $Ω$ with $u=0$ on $\partialΩ$. Let $\Tg(Ω)=\int_Ωu\,dγ$ be the Gaussian torsional rigidity. We prove that the Brunn--Minkowski-type inequality $\Tg((1-t)Ω_0+tΩ_1)^α\le (1-t)\Tg(Ω_0)^α+t\Tg(Ω_1)^α$ fails for every exponent $α>0$ and in every dimension $n\ge2$, for a pair of convex bodies centrally symmetric with respect to the origin, which may be taken smooth with positive curvature. This answers Conjecture~1.4 and Question~(Q) of Marín Sola and Salerno in the negative. The mechanism is a first-order lower bound for $\Tg$ at a ball $Ω_0$ under Minkowski perturbations. When the perturbing body $Ω_1$ has small torsion and large mean width, $\Tg((1-t) Ω_0 + t Ω_1)$ increases to first order. Since the Minkowski combination has larger torsion than both endpoints, no exponent can repair the inequality.

</details>


### [227] [On Cauchy Problems for Parabolic Equations with Rough Coefficients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29984)
*Cheng Yuan*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了粗糙扩散系数下非散度与散度形式抛物型方程的柯西问题，在临界正则性条件下建立了最优分数阶Sobolev空间中的唯一可解性，并通过构造反例证明阈值s<1/2的尖锐性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粗糙扩散系数在复合材料、金融定价和粘性流体等实际场景中普遍存在，但这类系数缺乏光滑性，导致经典的Sobolev正则性理论难以适用。作者旨在在临界正则性设定下，为这类抛物型方程建立最优分数阶Sobolev空间中的唯一可解性，填补该领域的理论空白。

Method: 作者首先构建了一个有效的逼近方案，利用截断与磨光化处理扩散系数a^ε(t,x)，并严格证明高频小量的一致保持性。随后将该逼近方案与仿积分解、Fefferman-Stein极大不等式、Coifman-Meyer双线性估计以及精细的Sobolev嵌入相结合，封闭一致先验估计，最终通过极限过程得到原问题的可解性。此外，通过构造显式反例证明阈值s<1/2的尖锐性。

Result: 在临界正则性条件下，建立了非散度与散度形式粗糙系数抛物型方程柯西问题在最优分数阶Sobolev空间中的唯一可解性。通过构造显式反例，证明了问题的解空间正则性阈值s<1/2是尖锐且不可改进的。

Conclusion: 本研究为粗糙系数抛物型方程柯西问题提供了严格的数学理论基础，所建立的最优正则性结果及阈值尖锐性证明，为该类方程在复合材料、金融定价和粘性流体等应用领域提供了坚实的数学框架支撑。

Abstract: The presented work investigates the Cauchy problems for parabolic equations in both non-divergence and divergence forms with rough diffusion coefficients, which commonly arise in composite media, financial pricing, and viscous fluids. Under critical regularity settings, we establish the unique solvability in optimal fractional Sobolev spaces. The first key technical step lies in constructing an effective approximation scheme with truncated and mollified diffusion coefficients $a^ε(t,x)$, for which we rigorously prove the uniform preservation of high-frequency smallness. By incorporating this approximation scheme with paraproduct decomposition, Fefferman-Stein maximal inequalities, Coifman-Meyer bilinear estimates, and refined Sobolev embeddings, we close the uniform a priori estimates and then pass to the limit. Furthermore, we prove that the threshold $s < \frac{1}{2}$ is sharp by constructing explicit counterexamples. These theoretical results provide a rigorous mathematical framework in the study of Cauchy problems for parabolic equations with rough coefficients.

</details>


### [228] [High frequency wave propagation for the viscoelastic wave equation with singular memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30138)
*Haim Grebnev,Plamen Stefanov*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究具有空间依赖历史记忆的粘弹性波动方程的高频传播问题，包括可积奇异核（0<p<1）和正则情形（p=1）。通过全尺度几何光学展开构造精确解，揭示了记忆核奇异性产生的分数阶尺度、频率相关衰减和色散相位修正，并建立了局部阻尼波动方程的O(h)逼近、外部反演问题唯一性以及弱奇异核情形的适定性和Sobolev正则性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粘弹性材料中的记忆效应对波传播有重要影响，尤其是当记忆核具有可积奇异性时，其高频行为尚未被充分理解。作者旨在系统研究奇异核下粘弹性波的高频几何光学结构，并解决相应的适定性、逼近与反演问题。

Method: 采用双尺度几何光学展开方法，以半振幅传播距离和相应走时为单位，构造精确解的幂级数展开（h^{k+(1-p)ℓ}）。利用瞬时模量σ+ε∫₀^∞m(s,·)ds修正传播几何，奇异性通过C_p=Γ(p)e^{iπp/2}进入输运方程。此外，推导局部阻尼波动方程近似、利用收缩半群论证建立适定性。

Result: 对0<p<1得到分数阶尺度、频率相关衰减和色散相位修正；p=1时分数阶层级消失，衰减与频率无关且相位修正消失。建立O(h)误差的semiclassical C^k逼近；外部观测唯一恢复σ_m和m在s=0处的时间完整喷流。获得任意有限阶Sobolev正则性和与ε一致的估计。

Conclusion: 该工作完整刻画了空间依赖弱奇异记忆核下粘弹性波的高频传播：记忆核奇异性通过分数阶幂级数展开结构改变输运行为，产生频率相关衰减与色散；同时建立了逼近、反演和适定性的统一理论框架。

Abstract: We study high-frequency propagation for a viscoelastic wave equation with spatially dependent hereditary memory in relative-history form. The kernel may have the integrable singularity $\mathfrak m(s,x)=s^{p-1}m(s,x)$, $0<p<1$; the regular case $p=1$ is included. Using the half-amplitude propagation distance and corresponding travel time as units, the wavelength $h\ll1$ yields the memory factor $\varepsilon=h^{1-p}$. We construct exact solutions with full two-scale geometric-optics expansions in powers $h^{k+(1-p)\ell}$. Memory modifies the propagation geometry through the instantaneous modulus $σ+\varepsilon\int_0^\infty\mathfrak m(s,\cdot)\,d s$, while the kernel singularity contributes $C_p=Γ(p)e^{iπp/2}$ to the leading transport equation. For $0<p<1$, this produces fractional scales, frequency-dependent attenuation, and a dispersive phase correction; for $p=1$, the fractional hierarchy disappears, attenuation is frequency independent, and the transport phase correction vanishes.
  We also derive a local damped wave equation whose incoming high-frequency solutions approximate the hereditary solutions with $O(h)$ error in semiclassical $C^k$ norms. Exterior observations for all incident directions and $0<h\ll1$ uniquely recover $σ_{\mathfrak m}$ and the full temporal jet of $m$ at $s=0$, which determine the expansion modulo $O(h^\infty)$.
  Finally, a contraction-semigroup argument gives well-posedness and arbitrary finite-order Sobolev regularity for spatially dependent weakly singular kernels and prescribed full prehistory, with explicit compatibility conditions and estimates uniform in $\varepsilon$. These estimates justify the geometric-optics construction.

</details>


### [229] [Gradient Estimates Near the Natural Exponent for Very Weak Solutions to $A_p$-Weighted Quasilinear Elliptic Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30155)
*Sun-Sig Byun,Minkyu Lim*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文建立了矩阵加权拟线性方程在自然指数附近的局部Calderón–Zygmund估计，其中矩阵场M仅需具备有界条件数和ω=|M|^p∈A_p，无需对M施加一致上下界。研究将Adimurthi–Phuc的近自然Calderón–Zygmund理论推广到矩阵退化情形，证明对任意极弱解u∈W^{p-δ_0}_{ω,loc}，f∈L^γ_{ω,loc}蕴含Du∈L^γ_{ω,loc}（p-δ_0≤γ≤p+δ_0）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典Calderón–Zygmund理论从标量退化情形推广到矩阵退化情形，突破M需要一致上下界的限制，仅依赖条件数有界和加权A_p条件，从而扩展极弱解理论的适用范围。

Method: 通过比较估计（comparison estimates）、高可积性（higher integrability）和加权分析技术，结合Lipschitz截断（Lipschitz truncation）处理低于自然指数时缺乏能量估计的困难，在矩阵退化加权框架下建立所需的Calderón–Zygmund估计。

Result: 存在δ_0>0，使得对任意极弱解u∈W^{p-δ_0}_{ω,loc}，只要f∈L^γ_{ω,loc}（p-δ_0≤γ≤p+δ_0），则有Du∈L^γ_{ω,loc}。

Conclusion: 成功将近自然Calderón–Zygmund理论推广至矩阵退化情形，在宽泛的矩阵条件下（仅需条件数有界且ω=|M|^p∈A_p）建立了近自然指数范围的梯度正则性估计。

Abstract: We establish local Calderón--Zygmund estimates near the natural exponent for very weak solutions to matrix-weighted quasilinear equations\[ - \mathrm{div\,} A_{\mathbb{M}}(x,Du) =
- \mathrm{div\,} A_{\mathbb{M}}(x,\mathbf{f}), \qquad A_{\mathbb{M}}(x,ξ)=\mathbb{M}(x)A(x,\mathbb{M}(x)ξ), \] where $A$ has $p$-growth and strong monotonicity, and $\mathbb{M}$ is a measurable positive-definite matrix field. We assume that $\mathbb{M}$ has bounded condition number and that \(ω:=|\mathbb{M}|^p\in A_p\), without imposing uniform upper or lower bounds on $\mathbb{M}$.
  This extends the near-natural Calderón--Zygmund theory developed by Adimurthi--Phuc \cite{AP15} to the matrix-degenerate setting: There exists $δ_0>0$ such that every very weak solution $u \in W^{p-δ_{0}}_{ω, \mathrm{loc}}$ satisfies \[ \mathbf{f}\in L^γ_{ω,\mathrm{loc}} \Longrightarrow Du\in L^γ_{ω,\mathrm{loc}} \] for $p-δ_0\leγ\le p+δ_0$. The proof requires handling the lack of energy estimates below the natural exponent and the use of Lipschitz truncation in the weighted setting. We achieve this through comparison estimates, higher integrability, and weighted analysis techniques.

</details>


### [230] [Łojasiewicz--Simon inequalities near bubbling configurations for the Yamabe functional on bounded domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30173)
*Tianling Jin,Jingang Xiong,Ning Zhou*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文在有界光滑区域上，针对由有限个集中气泡（含或不含正则分量）构成的配置，建立了Yamabe泛函的Łojasiewicz-Simon型梯度不等式。通过加权分解将有限维谱参数和气泡参数与无限维强制余项分离，获得了两种机制（正则加气泡与纯气泡）下参数、余项及能量间隙的定量估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Yamabe泛函的梯度不等式是研究相关抛物流动力学（如Yamabe流）的关键工具，而气泡配置附近的定量偏差刻画对理解流的奇异性行为至关重要。已有Łojasiewicz-Simon不等式在多气泡配置情形下缺乏系统建立。

Method: 采用加权分解方法：将函数空间分解为有限维子空间（对应谱参数和气泡参数）与无限维强制余项（coercive remainder）。通过这种结构分离，分别处理正则加气泡和纯气泡两种情形，建立定量化的梯度不等式。

Result: 获得了有界光滑域上Yamabe泛函在气泡配置附近的Łojasiewicz-Simon型梯度不等式，给出了参数、余项以及相应能量间隙的定量估计，覆盖了正则加气泡与纯气泡两种机制。

Conclusion: 所建立的梯度不等式量化了配置与该类曲面之间的偏离程度，为研究相关抛物流的动力学行为提供了必要的解析基础。

Abstract: We establish Łojasiewicz--Simon type gradient inequalities for the Yamabe functional on bounded smooth domains near configurations consisting of finitely many concentrating bubbles, with or without a regular component. A weighted decomposition separates the finite-dimensional spectral and bubble parameters from an infinite-dimensional coercive remainder. We obtain quantitative estimates for the parameters, the remainder, and the corresponding energy gaps, in both the regular-plus-bubbling and the pure-bubbling regimes. These inequalities quantify the deviation from such surfaces and are essential for studying the dynamics of related parabolic flows.

</details>


### [231] [Exact counting of spherical metrics with one conical singularity on rectangular tori](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30174)
*Zhijie Chen,Shihong Zhang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明在矩形环面 E_{ib} 上，当 8π(n−1)<ρ<8πn 时，奇异Liouville方程 Δu+e^u=ρδ_0 恰好有 n 个轴对称解。由此推出环面上球形度量与锥形奇点的存在性与计数结果，并通过经典Lamé方程的单演（monodromy）理论建立了统一的证明方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奇异Liouville方程在矩形环面上的解个数问题长期未完全解决。先前Chen-Lin和Lin-Wang的结果仅覆盖部分参数范围，且基于Bol等周不等式的节点域分析方法只适用于ρ≤8π。本文旨在建立适用于所有ρ∈(0,+∞)\8πN≥1的统一方法，并完整刻画解的个数。

Method: 作者引入经典的Lamé方程，通过研究其单演（monodromy）与轴对称函数空间中线性化方程的关系，证明线性化方程在轴对称函数空间中仅有平凡解。由此通过拓扑度方法或隐函数定理类论证得到精确的解计数。该方法绕过了以往Bol等周不等式的限制，适用于任意ρ值（非8π的整数倍）。

Result: (1) 对每个整数n≥2，当8π(n−1)<ρ<8πn时，奇异Liouville方程在矩形环面E_{ib}上恰好有n个轴对称解；(2) 环面E_{ib}不存在锥角为2πϑ（ϑ为正奇数）的球形度量；(3) 对每个整数n≥1，当ϑ∈(2n−1,2n+1)时，E_{ib}恰好有n个锥角为2πϑ的球形度量。

Conclusion: 本文通过Lamé方程单演理论与轴对称空间中线性化方程平凡性的证明，给出了矩形环面上奇异Liouville方程解个数的完整刻画，从而彻底解决了该环面上球形度量带锥奇点的存在性与计数问题，建立了统一而深刻的分析框架。

Abstract: We prove that for every integer $n\geq 2$ and $8π(n-1)<ρ<8πn$, the singular Liouville equation $Δu+\e^u=ρδ_0$ on a rectangular torus $E_{\mathrm{i}b}=\mathbb{C}/(\mathbb Z+\mathrm{i} b\mathbb Z)$ has exactly $n$ solutions, which are all axisymmetric. Together with previous results by Chen-Lin and Lin-Wang, this yields that \begin{itemize} \item $E_{\mathrm{i} b}$ admits no spherical metrics with a conical singularity of angle $2π\vartheta$ as long as $\vartheta$ is a positive odd integer. \item For every integer $n\geq 1$, $E_{\mathrm{i} b}$ admits exactly $n$ spherical metrics with a conical singularity of angle $2π\vartheta$ for each $\vartheta\in (2n-1, 2n+1)$. \end{itemize} The basic idea is to prove that the linearized equation has only trivial solutions in the space of axisymmetric functions. The previous method of analysing nodal domains via Bol's isoperimetric inequality only works for $ρ\leq 8π$. We develop a unified approach for all $ρ\in (0,+\infty)\setminus 8π\mathbb{N}_{\geq 1}$ by exploring the deep connection with the monodromy of the classical Lamé equation.

</details>


### [232] [Sharp Dispersive and Strichartz Estimates for the Neumann Cylindrical Wave Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30208)
*Len Meas*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文在三维圆柱凸域（含光滑非空边界，Neumann边界条件）中，建立了线性波动方程的局部色散估计与无导数损失的尖锐Strichartz估计，并利用这些估计证明了能量临界五次非线性波动方程（NLW）的局部适定性与小初值全局适定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边界几何（尤其曲率沿轴向消失的圆柱凸域）显著影响波传播的色散性质，现有结果在Neumann边界条件下缺乏尖锐的Strichartz估计，限制了非线性问题的适定性分析。

Method: 利用Airy函数导数零点进行显式谱分析，结合定制的Airy–Poisson求和公式，精确刻画多次反射波与焦散的微局域行为。

Result: 获得了无导数损失的尖锐Strichartz估计，并由此证明了能量临界五次NLW在Neumann边界条件下局部适定及小初值全局适定。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了Neumann边界条件下凸柱域中波动方程色散估计的空白，并成功将估计应用于非线性能量临界问题的适定性。
