Abstract: We establish local-in-time dispersive and sharp Strichartz estimates for the linear wave equation with Neumann boundary conditions inside a cylindrical convex domain $Ω\subset \mathbb{R}^3$ featuring a non-empty smooth boundary $\partialΩ$. The cylindrical geometry dictates that the nonnegative radius of curvature vanishes along the axial direction. By utilizing an explicit spectral analysis based on the zeros of the Airy function derivative, alongside a tailored Airy--Poisson summation formula, we capture the microlocal behavior of multi-reflected waves and caustics. Finally, we apply these sharp Strichartz estimates without loss of derivatives to prove local well-posedness and small-data global well-posedness for the energy-critical quintic nonlinear wave equation (NLW) subject to Neumann boundary conditions.

</details>


### [233] [Parameter-uniform Robin uniqueness on large dilations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30211)
*Sophie Sun*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明了大扩张极限下Robin边界条件中系数α不影响扩张阈值，适用于可能无界的均匀C^{2,γ}域，包括Dirichlet和Neumann端点情况。核心是通过半空间线性化保持谱间隙、利用调和极限消去Dirichlet端点处的非线性紧致性困难，并给出了谱底部收敛误差O(κ^{-1/2})及有界C^{4,γ}域上的平均曲率一阶修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Berestycki和Graham对于固定的α证明了有界正解在κΩ上大扩张的唯一性，并推测扩张阈值应当不依赖于α。本文的动机是验证这一推测，并处理包括Dirichlet和Neumann端点在内的完整参数范围。

Method: 本文方法的关键在于：(1) 证明半空间线性化在紧化边界参数上保持共同的正谱间隙；(2) 在Dirichlet端点（Robin系数发散）处，通过以其倒数尺度重新标度，使极限方程变为调和方程，并借助Liouville引理排除任何可能的边迹，从而获得非线性紧致性；(3) 结合端点紧致性、半空间谱间隙与局部化技术证明均匀的大扩张唯一性。

Result: 证明了大扩张唯一性的扩张阈值不依赖于Robin系数α（包括Dirichlet和Neumann端点），适用于可能无界的均匀C^{2,γ}域。当∂Ω非空时，谱底收敛到半空间值的误差为O(κ^{-1/2})；对于有界C^{4,γ}域，边界层具有一阶平均曲率修正，余项为O(κ^{-1-γ}+κ^{-2})。

Conclusion: Berestycki和Graham关于扩张阈值不依赖于α的推测得到完全验证，并且结果被推广到无界域及端点为Dirichlet/Neumann的情形。此外还获得了谱收敛率和边界层曲率修正的定量估计。

Abstract: Berestycki and Graham proved large-dilation uniqueness for bounded positive solutions of $-Δu=f(u)$ in $κΩ$, with $u+α\partial_νu=0$ on $\partial(κΩ)$, when $α$ is fixed, and remarked that the dilation threshold should not depend on $α$. We show that it does not, including at the Dirichlet and Neumann endpoints, for possibly unbounded uniformly $C^{2,γ}$ domains. The half-space linearizations retain a common positive spectral gap across the compactified boundary parameter. The remaining endpoint difficulty is nonlinear compactness at the Dirichlet end, where the Robin coefficient diverges. Rescaling at its reciprocal scale makes the limiting equation harmonic. Any trace that persisted in this limit would give a bounded harmonic half-space solution of $\partial_νv+v=0$, which a Liouville lemma rules out. Combining this endpoint compactness with the half-space gap and localization gives uniform large-dilation uniqueness. When $\partialΩ\neq\varnothing$, we further obtain convergence of the spectral bottom to its half-space value with error $O(κ^{-1/2})$. For bounded $C^{4,γ}$ domains the boundary layer also has a first mean-curvature correction, with remainder $O(κ^{-1-γ}+κ^{-2})$ on each fixed boundary strip.

</details>


### [234] [Strong Unique Continuation for Fractional Schrödinger Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30232)
*Harsh Prasad*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明了分数阶薛定谔方程(-Δ)^s u = Vu在临界尺度势V∈L^{n/2s}_loc下对所有0<s<1成立强唯一延拓性质，这是经典Jerison–Kenig结果在非局部情形的对应版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Jerison–Kenig定理给出了局部薛定谔算子-Δu=Vu在临界势条件下的强唯一延拓性质，但分数阶拉普拉斯算子(-Δ)^s对应的非局部薛定谔方程在临界缩放势下是否具有类似性质此前未知，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 通过处理分数阶拉普拉斯算子(-Δ)^s（0<s<1）与临界缩放势V∈L^{n/2s}_loc的组合，利用非局部算子的分析工具建立强唯一延拓性质（强唯一延拓意味着若解在某个区域上快速消失且势满足适当条件，则解必恒为零）。

Result: 对所有0<s<1，证明了具备临界缩放势的分数阶薛定谔方程的强唯一延拓性质，即实现了经典Jerison–Kenig结果在非局部框架下的完整延伸。

Conclusion: 本文的结论表明，分数阶薛定谔方程在临界势条件下同样具备强唯一延拓性质，验证了非局部算子在这一层面的理论完备性，为后续相关研究提供了重要基础。

Abstract: We prove the strong unique continuation property for the fractional Schrödinger equation \[
  (-Δ)^s u=Vu \] with scaling-critical potentials
  $ V\in L^{\frac{n}{2s}}_{\mathrm{loc}}$ for all $0<s<1$. Our result constitutes the nonlocal counterpart to the classical Jerison--Kenig result for Schrödinger operators.

</details>


### [235] [On smooth self similar solution to compressible Euler equation with physical vacuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30443)
*Jihoi Kim*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文在d>1维的barotropic Euler方程中，研究了密度在空间无穷远处衰减的情况，在不同于Merle等人(2022)工作的参数范围内，证明了通过音速线光滑、但在某球面上密度趋于零的"全息球"(holosphere)型自相似解的存在性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Guderley开创性地引入了球对称自相似解的相图分析，Merle等人(2022)在特定参数范围内证明了通过音速线的光滑衰减解的存在性。本文旨在探索不同参数范围内的解行为，特别是寻找新型的具有密度在球面上消失的解。

Method: 对d>1维的barotropic Euler方程，研究球对称自相似解所满足的非线性ODE系统，在不同于Merle等人工作的参数区间内，通过相图分析和严格证明，构造通过音速线光滑但在某个球面处密度为零的holosphere型解。

Result: 在新的参数范围内，证明了存在holosphere型自相似解：这些解在通过音速线时是光滑的，但密度在某个球面上消失（趋于零）。

Conclusion: 本文扩展了Guderley相图框架下barotropic Euler方程自相似解的存在性结果，在更广泛的参数范围内发现了与Merle等人(2022)不同类型的新解，即holosphere型解，丰富了该方程自相似解的结构分类。

Abstract: We consider the barotropic Euler equations in dimension $d > 1$ with decaying density at spatial infinity. The phase portrait of the nonlinear ODE governing the equation for spherically symmetric self-similar solutions has been introduced in the pioneering work of Guderley. The existence of smooth decaying solutions through the sonic line has been obtained in the work (F. Merle et al., 2022) in a suitable range of parameters. We work in this paper in a different range of parameters and show the existence of holosphere like solutions which are smooth through the sonic line, but display vanishing density at a sphere.

</details>


### [236] [Entire monotone solutions of the anisotropic Allen-Cahn equation in dimension 5](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30904)
*Haowen Lu,Song Wang,Juncheng Wei,Yuanze Wu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究各向异性Allen-Cahn方程，基于Mooney-Yang各向异性极小图，在N≥4维空间中构造了具有非超平面水平集的弱稳定解；并作为副产品，在N≥5维空间中构造了沿一个方向单调但非一维的光滑解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Allen-Cahn方程的稳定解具有刚性结果，但在各向异性情形下，稳定解的结构是否仍然保持超平面性是一个开放问题。本文旨在构造各向异性Allen-Cahn方程的反例，证明其稳定解可以具有非超平面的水平集，从而打破刚性猜想。

Method: 利用Mooney-Yang各向异性极小图构造，通过将该极小图与Allen-Cahn方程的相场结构相结合，在N≥4时构造弱意义的稳定解，并在N≥5时构造光滑的单调但非一维的解。

Result: 证明了各向异性Allen-Cahn方程在N≥4时存在具有非超平面水平集的稳定解（弱意义）；同时构造了N≥5时单调非一维的光滑解。

Conclusion: 各向异性Allen-Cahn方程的稳定解不再具有超平面刚性，这与经典各向同性情形形成鲜明对比，揭示了几何各向异性对相分离问题稳定性的本质影响。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider the anisotropic Allen-Cahn equation $-\operatorname{div} a(Du)+W'(u)=0$ in $\mathbb{R}^N$, where $a(p):=DH(p)$ with $H(p)=\frac{1}{2}F(p)^2$ and $F$ a uniformly elliptic integrand, and $W(u)=\frac{1}{4}(1-u^2)^2$. Based on the Mooney-Yang anisotropic minimal graph, we prove that the anisotropic Allen-Cahn equation admits a stable solution for $N\geq4$ in the weak sense, whose level sets are not hyperplanes. As a byproduct, we also construct a smooth solution of the above anisotropic Allen-Cahn equation for $N\geq5$ that is monotone in one direction but is not one-dimensional.

</details>


### [237] [Derivation of a linearly elastic elliptic membrane shell theory in the framework of magnetoelasticity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30445)
*Trung Hieu Giang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该论文从三维线性磁弹性模型出发，通过Γ-收敛方法研究薄壳在厚度趋于零时的渐近行为，严格推导出二维线性磁弹性壳模型，并建立了与原三维模型在极小元收敛意义上的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究薄壳结构在磁场与弹性耦合作用下的渐近行为，将三维问题降维为二维模型，以便更有效地分析和理解磁弹性薄壳的力学行为。

Method: 采用Γ-收敛的数学框架，从三维线性化磁弹性模型出发，针对线性弹性椭圆膜壳这一特定壳族，在厚度趋于零的极限过程中严格推导二维线性磁弹性壳模型。

Result: 成功地通过Γ-收敛严格推导出了二维线性磁弹性壳模型，并证明其与原三维模型在极小元收敛意义上具有一致性关系。

Conclusion: 该研究为磁弹性薄壳的降维建模提供了严格的数学基础，确认了二维极限模型能够准确捕获三维模型的本质行为，为后续力学分析和工程应用提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: Starting from a three-dimensional model of linearized magnetoelasticity, we investigate the asymptotic behavior of a thin shell as its thickness approaches zero. Our analysis focuses on a family of linearly elastic elliptic membrane shells. Using $Γ$-convergence, we rigorously derive a two-dimensional linear magnetoelastic shell model and establish its relationship to the original three-dimensional model in terms of the convergence of minimizers.

</details>


### [238] [Grow-up rates of inhomogeneous semilinear heat equations in the critical dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30518)
*Kenta Kumagai*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究单位球内带指数非线性项和非齐次项$f$的半线性热方程解的grow-up速率。临界维度$N=10$下，给出了grow-up现象消失的定量刻画，发现了与$N\ge 11$情形本质不同的grow-up行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究（文献[12]）表明当$N\ge 10$且非齐次项$f$超过阈值时grow-up现象会消失，但在临界维度$N=10$下对消失现象的定量刻画尚缺失。此外，即使在$f=0$的情形，Galaktionov和King[10]的正式计算也缺乏严格证明。

Method: 通过推导临界维度下的grow-up速率，对grow-up现象的消失给出定量表征。分析临界维度下解的外层行为变化，将其与$N\ge 11$情形进行对比。

Result: 在临界维度$N=10$下获得了grow-up速率的显式刻画，表明出现了与$N\ge 11$情形定性不同的grow-up行为，这种差异源于解的外层行为的变化。在$f=0$时，结果为Galaktionov和King[10]的正式计算提供了严格验证。

Conclusion: 临界维度$N=10$下的grow-up行为具有独特的定性特征，与非临界维度$N\ge 11$存在本质区别，该结论闭合了关于指数非线性半线性热方程grow-up速率在临界维度的理论空白。

Abstract: This paper concerns the grow-up rate of solutions to a semilinear heat equation in the unit ball with the exponential nonlinearity and an inhomogeneous term $f$. When $f=0$, it is known that the large-time behavior of a solution changes at the critical dimension $N=10$, and the grow-up phenomenon occurs for the case $N\ge 10$. For the inhomogeneous case with $N\ge 10$, the present author and a coauthor showed in [12] that the grow-up phenomenon disappears once $f$ exceeds a threshold.
  In this paper, we provide a quantitative characterization of the disappearance of the grow-up phenomenon by obtaining the grow-up rate in the critical dimension. Our result shows that a qualitatively different type of grow-up behavior occurs in the critical dimension compared with the case $N\ge 11$ studied in [12]. The difference is caused by a change in the outer behavior of the solution. Even in the case $f=0$, our result is new in that it provides a rigorous justification of the formal computation by Galaktionov and King [10].

</details>


### [239] [Finite speed of propagation and waiting time phenomena for stochastic porous media equations with nonlinear conservative noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30548)
*Günther Grün,Max Sauerbrey,Joshua Utley*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了带非线性保守噪声的随机多孔介质方程的动力学解的有限传播速度问题。通过提出一种新的迭代技术，建立了一个包含单一积分量的Stampacchia型不等式，并结合随机滤波论证，证明了有限传播速度。同时，识别出了保证等待时间现象（解支撑集的向前传播被局部延迟）的初始数据平坦性条件，该条件在临界噪声情形下与确定性多孔介质方程的条件只差一个对数修正，且随着噪声趋近于线性保守噪声需要越来越平坦的初始数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机多孔介质方程的动力学解的存在性和唯一性已被近期工作建立，但解的有限传播速度（即支撑集扩展的局部性质）尚未得到证明。此外，确定性情形下已知的等待时间现象在随机情形下的刻画也是开放问题。由于多孔介质项和噪声项可能具有不同的缩放行为，常规方法难以同时处理两者。

Method: 从局部能量估计出发，提出一种新颖的迭代技术，它能在多孔介质项与噪声项缩放行为不同的情况下，仍获得只涉及单一积分量的Stampacchia型不等式。将问题归结为线性源型噪声情形，进而应用已有的随机滤波论证。同时利用相似思想分析初始数据的平坦性条件来刻画等待时间现象。

Result: 证明了带非线性保守噪声的随机多孔介质方程动力学解的有限传播速度。给出了保证等待时间现象发生的局部条件：在临界噪声非线性情形下，该条件与确定性多孔介质方程匹配（至多差一个对数修正）；当噪声非线性趋近线性保守噪声时，需要初始数据越来越平坦才可保证等待时间现象，这与线性保守噪声下因随机输运效应可能产生瞬时向前运动这一预期一致。

Conclusion: 有限传播速度在带非线性保守噪声的随机多孔介质方程中成立，且等待时间现象的条件揭示了噪声非线性程度与初始数据平坦性之间的量化权衡。结论与物理直觉相符：线性保守噪声情形下随机输运效应可能导致解支撑集的瞬时向前传播，因此无论初始数据多么平坦都无法保证等待时间。

Abstract: Starting from localized energy estimates, we prove finite speed of propagation for kinetic solutions to stochastic porous media equations with nonlinear conservative noise, the existence and uniqueness of which has recently been established. In particular, we propose a novel iteration technique which allows us to obtain a Stampacchia-type inequality involving one single integral quantity, despite the possibly different scaling behaviors of the porous media and the noise term. This allows us to apply stochastic filtering arguments developed for the case of linear source-type noise. Using related ideas, we identify flatness conditions on initial data which guarantee locally the occurrence of a waiting time phenomenon, i.e., the onset of forward propagation of the solution's support is locally delayed. The condition for the latter matches the one for the deterministic porous media equation up to a logarithmic correction in the case of critical nonlinearity in the noise, but it requires more and more flatness of the initial data as the nonlinearity tends towards linear conservative noise. This is in line with the expected behavior: In the case of linear conservative noise, instantaneous forward motion is possible due to the effects of stochastic transport, no matter how flat the initial profile is.

</details>


### [240] [Compactly Supported Solutions near a Nehari Critical Value](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30578)
*J. I. Dıaz,J. Hernandez,Y. Ilyasov*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究一个带有不定号权函数的次线性Dirichlet问题，其中权函数m可以变号或非正，参数λ为实数。研究重点是此类问题非负解的存在性及相关性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究不定权重下的次线性Dirichlet问题，这类问题在非线性偏微分方程和变分法中有重要意义，且当权函数m变号或非正时，问题结构更加复杂，需要深入分析。

Method: 针对不定次线性Dirichlet问题，结合权函数m变号或非正的情形进行分析，可能采用变分方法、上下解方法或拓扑度理论等工具研究非负解的存在性。

Result: 尚未在摘要中给出具体结果，但研究对象为非负解的存在性及相关性质。

Conclusion: 该工作旨在建立不定号或非正权重下次线性Dirichlet问题非负解的存在性理论，为这类带符号权重的非线性问题提供完整的理解。

Abstract: We study non-negative solutions of the indefinite sublinear Dirichlet problem $$ \left \{\ \begin{array}{ll} -Δu=λu+m(x)|u|^{α-1}u&\quad\hbox{ in $Ω$},\\ u=0&\quad \hbox{ on $\partial Ω$}, \end{array} \right . $$ where $Ω\subset \mathbb{R}^{N}$ is a smooth bounded domain, $0<α<1$, $m\in L^{\infty }(Ω)$ is a sign-changing or non-positive weight, and $λ\in \mathbb{R}$.

</details>


### [241] [The flexible part of intermittent Onsager theorem for the two-dimensional stochastic incompressible Euler equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30582)
*Zhenxin Liu,Wenhu Zhong*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文针对二维随机不可压Euler方程，发展了随机间歇性Newton-Nash迭代方案，对任意γ∈[0,1/3)构造了γ-Hölder连续的鞅解，该解具有耗散性和间歇性，解决了二维随机不可压Euler方程间歇Onsager定理的柔性部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Onsager定理是流体力学中关于Euler方程能量守恒与解的正则性之间的著名定理。其柔性部分（即构造非能量守恒的低正则解）在确定性情形已有大量研究，但在随机情形下尚未解决。本文旨在将随机性和间歇性纳入Onsager定理的框架，填补该领域的空白。

Method: 发展了一个随机间歇性的Newton-Nash迭代方案，该方案是经典Newton-Nash迭代的推广，引入了三类新扰动：随机的压力扰动、Reynolds应力扰动和间歇性扰动，用以刻画随机与间歇性波动。

Result: 对任意γ∈[0,1/3)，成功构造了二维随机不可压Euler方程的γ-Hölder连续鞅解，这些解同时具有耗散行为（即能量不守恒）和间歇性特征，从而完成了该方程间歇Onsager定理的柔性部分。

Conclusion: 该工作将Onsager定理的柔性部分推广到随机情形，特别是结合了间歇性的视角，为理解随机Euler方程的低正则解的构造提供了新的方法论，并为随机流体动力学中能量反常耗散的研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: This work is concerned with the flexible part of Onsager theorem for the two-dimensional stochastic incompressible Euler equations. We develop a stochastic and intermittent variant of the Newton--Nash iteration scheme, which incorporates new stochastic pressure, Reynolds stress and intermittency perturbations to formalize the stochastic and intermittent fluctuations. For any $γ\in \left[ {0,\frac{1}{3}} \right)$, we employ this iteration scheme to construct $γ$-Hölder-continuous martingale solutions for the two-dimensional stochastic incompressible Euler equations. These martingale solutions exhibit dissipative behavior and intermittency, thereby settling the flexible part of intermittent Onsager theorem for the two-dimensional stochastic incompressible Euler equations.

</details>


### [242] [Higher Regularity of Time-Periodic Solutions to Nonautonomous Hyperbolic Problems: Away from Resonances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30658)
*Irina Kmit,Lutz Recke*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究一维线性与非线性非自治一阶双曲型积分-微分方程组边值问题的时间周期解的高阶正则性及其与非共振行为的关系。证明了在系数充分光滑且满足适当数量非共振条件时，连续解和经典解具有C^k正则性。非自治情形下需要额外非共振条件且数量依赖于所需可微阶数，这些条件通常也是必要的；而自治情形只需一个（甚至无需）非共振条件即可获得任意高正则性。主要技术工具是向量空间框架下的抽象正则性原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非自治双曲型偏微分方程的时间周期解正则性研究具有重要意义，因为高阶正则性通常与非共振条件密切相关。之前的工作主要关注自治系统或低阶正则性，本文旨在系统性地理解非自治系统中非共振条件数量与所需正则性阶数之间的关系，揭示非自治双曲系统与自治系统在正则性行为上的本质差异。

Method: 本文针对一维严格双曲型一阶积分-微分方程组，在包含积分算子及多种边界反射的边界条件下，建立了一个抽象的"正则性原则"作为主要技术工具，该原则在向量空间框架中表述。文中分别处理线性与非线性情形：线性情形中非共振条件涉及主系数、对角低阶系数和边界反射系数；非线性情形中非共振条件还依赖于非线性项和解本身。通过精细分析非共振条件与正则性阶数之间的关系，证明了这些条件在一般情况下不仅充分而且必要。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 在充分光滑系数和足够多非共振条件下，连续解和经典解具有C^k正则性；(2) 非自治系统需要数量随可微阶数增加而增加的非共振条件，且这些条件在一般情况下是必要的；(3) 自治系统只需至多一个非共振条件即可获得任意高阶正则性；(4) 识别了一类无需任何非共振条件的非自治双曲问题，其解的正则性完全由数据的正则性决定。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了非自治双曲型PDE与自治系统在正则性理论上的重要区别：非自治性引入了逐阶累积的非共振条件需求，而自治系统则不存在这一现象。这些发现不仅在理论上阐明了非共振行为与高阶正则性之间的深层联系，也为实际应用中判断双曲型边值问题时间周期解的正则性提供了明确准则。

Abstract: We study higher regularity and its relation to nonresonant behavior for time-periodic solutions of boundary value problems for one-dimensional linear and nonlinear nonautonomous first-order integro-differential strictly hyperbolic systems. The boundary conditions include integral operators and various types of boundary reflections. We prove that continuous and classical solutions have $C^k$-regularity, provided the coefficients are sufficiently smooth and a suitable number of nonresonance conditions is satisfied. In the linear case, these conditions involve the principal coefficients, the diagonal lower-order coefficients, and the boundary reflection coefficients. In the nonlinear case, they also depend on the nonlinearities and on the solution itself. For nonautonomous hyperbolic systems, higher regularity generally requires additional nonresonance conditions, whose number depends on the desired order of differentiability. These conditions are not only sufficient but, in general, also necessary, revealing a distinctive feature of nonautonomous hyperbolic PDEs. By contrast, in the autonomous case, a single nonresonance condition (if one is needed at all) suffices to obtain arbitrarily high regularity. We also identify a class of nonautonomous hyperbolic problems for which no nonresonance conditions are required. In this case, the higher regularity of solutions is determined solely by the regularity of the data. The main technical tool underlying the proofs is an abstract regularity principle formulated in the setting of vector spaces.

</details>


### [243] [Endpoint Energy Atoms Force Local Pressure Concentration in Three-Dimensional Navier-Stokes Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30715)
*Hao Huang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明了三维不可压缩Navier-Stokes流在平坦环面上的端点动能测度中的点原子会强制产生实际压力的定量集中。通过同态约束-响应比较机制，建立了精确的相对压力-功恒等式，并推导出压力振荡和压力梯度L2质量在收缩终端圆柱中的尺度显式下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Navier-Stokes方程端点奇异性的结构，特别是端点动能测度中的原子（atoms）如何影响压力场的局部行为。作者希望无需完整正则性假设，仅通过在不可压缩约束下产生的压力-功机制来关联端点原子与局部压力集中。

Method: 在每个重启时间tau，使用同态约束-响应比较方法：将u(tau)按无压力的逐分量对流-扩散方程演化，减去被动场z_tau，定义r_tau=u-z_tau和g_tau=Q z_tau，获得精确的相对压力-功恒等式。利用Nash平滑使z_tau终端非原子化，结合介观局域化技术获得尺度显式的L2下界。

Result: 若原子质量为m，则z_tau终端非原子而r_tau保留至少m的终端原子质量；每次固定重启后累积压力功的终端limsup至少为m/2。在原子点的每个终端邻域上，压力模去时间函数及其梯度均非平方可积。该原理可推广到受约束的螺线管Oseen演化。

Conclusion: 不可压缩约束产生了一种必要的压力-功机制，将端点原子与局部压力集中联系起来，无需完整正则性假设。这一机制为理解Navier-Stokes方程端点奇异性的结构提供了新的定量视角。

Abstract: We prove that a point atom in an endpoint kinetic-energy measure of a three-dimensional incompressible Navier-Stokes flow on the flat torus forces quantitative concentration of the actual pressure. At each restart time tau, a same-state constraint-response comparison evolves u(tau) by the pressure-free componentwise advection-diffusion equation driven by u, and subtracts the resulting passive field z_tau. Writing r_tau = u - z_tau and g_tau = Q z_tau, we obtain an exact relative pressure-work identity. If the atom has mass m, Nash smoothing makes z_tau terminally non-atomic while r_tau retains terminal atomic mass at least m; hence, after every fixed restart, the terminal limsup of the accumulated pressure work is at least m/2. Mesoscopic localization yields scale-explicit lower bounds for gauge-invariant L2 pressure oscillation and for the local L2 mass of the pressure gradient in shrinking terminal cylinders. Consequently, on every terminal neighborhood of the atomic point, the pressure modulo functions of time and its gradient fail to be square integrable. The relative pressure-work principle extends to constrained solenoidal Oseen evolutions: as tau approaches T*, the zero-initial responses vanish in weak-tail topologies, although every fixed restart retains the same lower bound. This identifies a necessary pressure-work mechanism, generated by the incompressibility constraint, that links endpoint atoms to local pressure concentration, without requiring a full regularity hypothesis.

</details>


### [244] [Inhomogeneous nonlinear Schrödinger equation in Fourier-Lebesgue and modulation spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30777)
*Divyang G. Bhimani,Diksha Dhingra,Vijay Kumar Sohani*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文为带奇异位势的非齐次非线性薛定谔方程(INLS)建立了更广泛的分析框架,证明了在Fourier-Lebesgue空间中低正则性意义下的适定性(对4/3<p<8),以及调制空间中(p∈(4/3,2))的类似结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多限于标准Sobolev空间或特定参数范围,缺乏对INLS方程在更一般函数空间(如Fourier-Lebesgue和调制空间)中低正则性适定性的系统分析。本文旨在提供一个更广泛的框架,涵盖更大范围的p值。

Method: 采用数据分解方法(data decomposition method)适配到Fourier-Lebesgue空间,分别处理p<2和p>2两类情形。关键思路是将初始数据分解为可处理的部分,以克服低正则性带来的困难,并证明任意大初始数据下的全局适定性。

Result: 在Fourier-Lebesgue空间(4/3<p<8)中建立了任意大初始数据下的全局适定性;同时在调制空间M^{p,p'}(4/3<p<2)中获得类似结果。

Conclusion: 通过将数据分解方法与Fourier-Lebesgue空间框架相结合,成功将INLS方程的低正则性适定性理论推广到更广泛的函数空间,并为调制空间中的相关问题提供了新结果。

Abstract: The purpose of this work is to provide a broader framework for analyzing the inhomogeneous nonlinear Schrödinger equation (INLS) \[iu_t + u_{xx} \pm |x|^{-b}|u|^{α-1}u=0, \quad 1<α< 5-2b\; \text{and}\; 0< b\leq 1/4.\] Specifically, we establish low-regularity well-posedness in the Fourier-Lebesgue $\widehat{L}^{p}$ spaces for $4/3<p<8$. The analysis is carried out differently for the cases $p<2$ and $p>2$. Primarily, in both cases, we prove global well-posedness for arbitrarily large initial data via the data decomposition method adapted for the Fourier-Lebesgue spaces. Furthermore, we obtain analogous results for INLS in modulation spaces $M^{p,p'}$ for $4/3<p<2$.

</details>


### [245] [Tumor boundary instability induced by pressure feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30796)
*Yu Feng,Yeyu Zhang,Kewei Zhou*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文针对Hele-Shaw型肿瘤生长模型（由多孔介质方程不可压缩极限得到），研究了与稳态压力相关的压力反馈参数对边界稳定性的影响。通过渐近分析，作者推导了有限半径肿瘤和平面行波前沿的稳定性判据，并证明平面行波前沿的稳定性阈值即为非对称行波出现的分岔点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肿瘤生长模型中的边界稳定性对于理解肿瘤形态发生和侵袭行为至关重要。压力反馈参数（与稳态压力相关）可能改变肿瘤边界的稳定性特性，理解这一机制有助于更准确地预测肿瘤的生长形态，并为潜在的治疗策略提供理论依据。

Method: 采用渐近分析方法，对有限半径肿瘤的边界扰动及平面行波前沿进行线性稳定性分析，推导稳定性判据；进一步利用分岔理论证明平面行波前沿的稳定性阈值对应于非对称行波的分岔点。

Result: 结果表明，压力反馈参数能够在无压力反馈模型仍保持稳定的参数区域内诱发边界不稳定性，即压力反馈具有破坏边界稳定性的作用。平面行波前沿的稳定性阈值恰好是非对称行波从对称解分支出现的分岔点。

Conclusion: 压力反馈在肿瘤生长模型中可以作为边界不稳定性的诱导因素，即使在无反馈时模型稳定的参数区间内也能导致失稳。该研究为肿瘤边界形态动力学提供了新的理论见解，并揭示了压力反馈与对称性破缺之间的联系。

Abstract: We consider a Hele--Shaw-type tumor growth model obtained from the incompressible limit of a porous-medium equation. The limiting model preserves the patch structure of the tumor density and therefore admits a single free-boundary formulation. We investigate how a pressure-feedback parameter associated with the homeostatic pressure affects boundary stability. Using asymptotic analysis, we study boundary perturbations of finite-radius tumors and planar traveling fronts and derive the corresponding stability criteria. For planar fronts, the stability thresholds are further shown to be bifurcation points from which nonsymmetric traveling waves emerge. Our results indicate that pressure feedback can induce boundary instability in parameter regimes where the model without pressure feedback remains stable.

</details>


### [246] [An Almgren-type formula for planar $p$-harmonic functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30847)
*Yi Ru-Ya Zhang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文针对非退化平面p-调和函数（1<p<∞），引入通量归一化频率函数N_*(x0,r)，它是Almgren频率函数在非线性情形的自然对应物，能精确恢复齐次p-调和形的齐次度。研究建立了相应的规范修正版Almgren型单调性，并在频率函数框架下重新解释了经典的平面唯一延拓性质，同时推导出通量归一化频率的定量夹紧（pinching）估计，且识别出阻碍该方法推广到高维的具体障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Almgren频率函数是椭圆方程（尤其调和函数）中研究零点结构、齐次度和唯一延拓性质的核心工具，但它在非线性p-调和方程框架下缺乏自然的对应物。作者希望构建一个在非线性设定下充当Almgren频率函数角色的量，使其既能精确刻画齐次p-调和形的齐次度，又能导出Almgren型单调性及唯一延拓相关的估计。

Method: 基于Aronsson关于孤立临界点邻域中的hodograph表示这一奠基性分析，作者提出了通量归一化频率N_*(x0,r)，其定义为径向通量积分（含|Du|^p）除以边界上|Du|^{p-2}(u-u(x0))^2的面积分之比。随后建立该量的规范修正（gauge-corrected）Almgren型单调性公式，并将其与平面唯一延拓性质建立联系，推导出定量pinching估计并分析推广至更高维的障碍。

Result: 该通量归一化频率对每个齐次p-调和形能精确恢复其齐次度；导出了规范修正版Almgren型单调性；在频率函数术语下重新诠释了平面唯一延拓性质；获得了通量归一化频率的定量pinching估计；并识别出阻碍方法向高维推广的特定数学障碍。

Conclusion: 通量归一化频率N_*是Almgren频率函数在p-调和非线性设定下的正确推广，它在平面情形下完整地复现了Almgren频率理论的核心功能（齐次度刻画、单调性、唯一延拓），并在定量层面提供了关于临界点附近p-调和函数几何的精细控制，但方法的推广受限于高维中出现的特定结构性障碍。

Abstract: For a nonconstant planar $p$-harmonic function $u$, with $1 < p < \infty$, and according to previous results, most notably Aronsson's fundamental analysis of the hodograph representation in a neighborhood of an isolated critical point, we introduce the flux-normalized frequency $$
  N_*(x_0,r) = \frac{r\displaystyle\int_{B_r(x_0)} |D u|^p\,dx} {\displaystyle\int_{\partial B_r(x_0)} |D u|^{p-2}(u-u(x_0))^2\,dS}. $$ This ratio constitutes the natural counterpart of Almgren's frequency function in the nonlinear setting, as it recovers precisely the degree of homogeneity for every homogeneous $p$-harmonic profile. We further establish the corresponding gauge-corrected version of Almgren-type monotonicity and reinterpret, in terms of the frequency function, the classical planar unique continuation property. In addition, we derive a quantitative pinching estimate for the flux-normalized frequency and identify the specific obstruction that prevents a straightforward extension of the method to higher dimensions.

</details>


### [247] [Conservation Laws with Discontinuous Flux: A First Application to Traffic Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30859)
*Roberta Bianchini,Maya Briani,Benedetto Piccoli*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文为文献[1]中引入的具有不连续梯度相关通量的守恒律模型提供了一个宏观交通流解释。通过假定定义在密度域[0,u_max]上的凹通量函数f(u)和g(u)，将模型适配到宏观交通流场景，引入定制化的修正Riemann求解器以防止最大密度约束被违反，并推广了弱解存在性理论。此外，还构建了包含自由流相的两相模型的Riemann求解器（其中f(u)=g(u)在[0,u_max]的子集上成立），并建立了该更一般框架下弱解的存在性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始的守恒律模型虽然理论完备，但直接应用于宏观交通流场景时，需要克服两个关键挑战：一是需要将模型适配到具有凹通量函数的交通流框架中；二是需要确保解不违反最大密度约束。此外，两相交通流模型中存在自由流区域（f(u)=g(u)的子集），需要为这一更一般的情形构造Riemann求解器并验证弱解存在性。

Method: 本文方法包括：(1) 将梯度相关不连续通量的守恒律模型适配到宏观交通流设置，假设f(u)和g(u)为[0,u_max]上的凹函数；(2) 引入修正的Riemann求解器以防止密度越界，确保最大密度约束不被违反；(3) 推广文献[1]中的弱解存在性理论；(4) 将框架扩展到更广泛的两相模型，允许f(u)=g(u)在密度域子集上成立（对应自由流阶段），并为其构造Riemann求解器。

Result: 成功将原始模型适配到宏观交通流框架，通过修正Riemann求解器确保了最大密度约束的满足；推广了弱解存在性理论以适用于新的交通流设置；并将框架扩展到包含自由流阶段的两相模型，在f(u)=g(u)的更一般情形下建立了弱解的存在性。

Conclusion: 本文建立了具有不连续梯度依赖通量的守恒律在宏观交通流中的适用性，通过修正Riemann求解器保证了解的物理可行性（密度不超上限），并将理论推广到包含自由流阶段的两相模型，扩展了该模型的适用范围和理论完备性。

Abstract: This work provides a traffic flow interpretation of the model recently introduced in \cite{ABS2025}. We adapt the conservation law with discontinuous gradient-dependent flux to a macroscopic traffic setting, assuming concave flux functions $f(u)$ and $g(u)$ defined on the density domain $[0,u_{\max}]$. To prevent violations of the maximal density constraint, we introduce modified Riemann Solvers tailored to this setting and generalize the existence theory for weak solutions developed in \cite{ABS2025}. Finally, we construct Riemann Solvers for a broader two-phase model incorporating a free-flow regime, in which $f(u)=g(u)$ on a subset of $[0,u_{\max}]$, and establish existence of weak solutions in this more general framework.

</details>


### [248] [Necessity of Ladyzhenskaya \& Ural'tseva's sufficient conditions in nonuniform ellipticity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30898)
*Cristiana De Filippis*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明了Ladyzhenskaya & Ural'tseva关于非均匀椭圆问题内部梯度估计的充分条件同时也是必要条件，并通过非参数极小曲面展示了该条件的最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非均匀椭圆问题中，内部梯度估计的成立条件是一个核心问题。Ladyzhenskaya & Ural'tseva提出了充分条件，但尚不清楚这些条件是否也是必要的。本文旨在阐明该条件的充要性，并界定经典理论的适用范围。

Method: 本文通过构造和分析非参数极小曲面，验证Ladyzhenskaya-Ural'tseva条件的最优性，证明其非均匀性速率是使解保持局部Lipschitz正则的最大可能；同时论证Finn及Bombieri-De Giorgi-Miranda经典理论无法推广到Bernstein类2之外。

Result: 确定了Ladyzhenskaya & Ural'tseva条件的充要性；证明非参数极小曲面具有与局部Lipschitz正则性相容的最大非均匀性速率；确认经典理论仅限于Bernstein类2范围内成立。

Conclusion: 非参数极小曲面处于非均匀椭圆问题的临界状态：其非均匀性恰好达到允许解保持局部Lipschitz正则的最大程度，从而为Ladyzhenskaya-Ural'tseva条件的最优性提供了决定性证据。

Abstract: The sufficient conditions of Ladyzhenskaya \& Ural'tseva \cite{lu70} for interior gradient estimates in nonuniformly elliptic problems are found to be also necessary. Specifically, nonparametric minimal surfaces exhibit the maximal nonuniformity rate compatible with the local Lipschitz regularity of solutions, and the classical theory of Finn \cite{fin54} and Bombieri \& De Giorgi \& Miranda \cite{bdm69} doesn't extend beyond Bernstein genre $2$.

</details>


### [249] [On a pointwise estimate for a weighted rough singular integral operator in stratified Lie groups and applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30970)
*Diego Chamorro,Oscar Jarrín*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文在分层李群（stratified Lie groups）背景下，为加权粗糙奇异积分算子T_{Ω,ϖ}建立了一个新的逐点估计。该算子基于满足自然尺寸条件和关于权函数ϖ的消没性质的核Ω，且不假设任何正则性。通过梯度的加权极大函数和加权Morrey空间的信息组合来估计该算子。由此推导出新的加权函数不等式，并作为应用，在Heisenberg群上获得了粗糙版稳态Navier-Stokes方程的唯一性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粗糙奇异积分算子（无正则性假设的核）在调和分析和偏微分方程中具有重要地位，但此前在分层李群这一非欧几里得框架下缺少对其加权逐点估计的研究。作者希望填补这一空白，并将所得估计应用于Navier-Stokes方程的唯一性问题。

Method: 作者在分层李群上构造了基于核Ω和权函数ϖ的加权粗糙奇异积分算子，其中核满足尺寸条件和关于权的消没性质但不假设正则性。方法核心是通过加权极大函数与加权Morrey空间的组合来建立该算子作用于函数f时的逐点估计，并在此基础上推导加权函数不等式。

Result: 获得了加权粗糙奇异积分算子T_{Ω,ϖ}的逐点估计；由此推导出若干新的加权函数不等式；并成功地将这些结果应用于Heisenberg群上粗糙版稳态Navier-Stokes方程，得到了唯一性结论。

Conclusion: 该研究将加权粗糙奇异积分算子的逐点估计理论从欧几里得空间推广到了分层李群（含Heisenberg群）的框架下，为相关PDE问题提供了新的分析工具，尤其在粗糙核情形下获得了Navier-Stokes方程的唯一性结果。

Abstract: In this article, we present a new pointwise estimate for a weighted rough singular integral operator in the setting of stratified Lie groups. This operator, $T_{Ω, \varpi}$, is based on a kernel $Ω$ and a weight $\varpi$, where the kernel satisfies a natural size condition and a cancellation property with respect to the weight $\varpi$. Moreover, we do not assume any kind of regularity on these objects. This weighted rough singular integral operator, applied to a function $f$, is estimated through a combination of information involving a weighted maximal function of the gradient of $f$ and a weighted Morrey space. We also deduce from this pointwise estimate some new weighted functional inequalities and, as an application, we obtain a uniqueness result for a rough version of the stationary Navier-Stokes equation over the Heisenberg group.

</details>


### [250] [A block-Toeplitz approach to the spectrum of the Neumann-Poincaré operator on self-similar chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30991)
*Matias Ruiz*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究Neumann-Poincaré算子作用于Sobolev-Slobodeckij空间W^{s,p}(Γ)的均零子空间上的谱。Γ是一个非Lipschitz域，由无限自相似的互不相交光滑域链在一个极限点处累积而成。利用链的离散几何缩放结构获得了块Toeplitz表示，并通过算子值符号完整刻画了本质谱与Fredholm区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的Neumann-Poincaré算子谱理论通常局限于Lipschitz或光滑边界域，但对于具有累积点或分形结构的非Lipschitz域，其谱行为尚不清楚。自相似链结构在高频极限下出现非平凡的谱聚集现象，理解这类结构的谱特征对潜在的散射理论和数值分析具有重要意义。

Method: 1) 利用链的离散几何缩放参数α=(d-1)/p-s，将Neumann-Poincaré算子、单层和双层算子精确表示为块Toeplitz算子；2) 证明当0<α<d时算子值符号属于Wiener代数；3) 运用块Toeplitz算子理论刻画本质谱和Fredholm区域；4) 在能量空间H^{-1/2}_0(Γ)中通过算子值Wiener-Hopf分解刻画本质谱外的孤立特征值；5) 应用算子值Szegő极限定理推导大有限截断的渐近谱分布并建立特征值计数公式。

Result: 在能量空间H^{-1/2}_0(Γ)中证明了谱是实的，并通过算子值Wiener-Hopf因子分解完全表征了本质谱之外的孤立特征值；基于算子符号建立了特征值计数公式和渐近谱分布；通过同心圆环链的显式解析计算和圆盘链的数值逼近验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 该工作将块Toeplitz算子理论成功应用于非Lipschitz域上Neumann-Poincaré算子谱的完整刻画，揭示了缩放参数α对谱结构的决定性影响，为一般自相似域上的积分算子谱分析提供了新的方法论框架。

Abstract: We characterise the spectrum of the Neumann-Poincaré operator acting on a mean-zero subspace of the Sobolev-Slobodeckij space $W^{s,p}(Γ)$, where $Γ$ is a non-Lipschitz domain, consisting of an infinite self-similar chain of disjoint smooth domains accumulating at a limit point. We exploit the discrete geometric scaling of the chain to obtain an exact block-Toeplitz representation of the Neumann-Poincaré operator, as well as the single- and double-layer operators. The corresponding operator-valued symbol depends entirely on the single scaling parameter $α=(d-1)/p-s$ and belongs to the Wiener algebra whenever $0<α<d$. This allows us to exploit block-Toeplitz operator theory to characterise the essential spectrum and Fredholm regions within this regime. In the energy space $H^{-1/2}_0(Γ)$, corresponding to $α= d/2$, we prove the spectrum is real and characterise any isolated eigenvalues outside the essential spectrum through an operator-valued Wiener-Hopf factorisation. Furthermore, we apply an operator-valued Szegő limit theorem to derive the asymptotic spectral distribution for large finite truncations and establish an eigenvalue counting formula based on the operator symbol. We illustrate these results through explicit analytical computations for a chain of concentric annuli and numerical approximations for a chain of disks.

</details>


### [251] [Global smooth solutions to the inhomogeneous Landau-Fermi-Dirac equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31071)
*William Golding,Christopher Henderson*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了空间非齐次的Landau-Fermi-Dirac方程（含库仑势），利用Pauli不相容原理带来的先验L∞界，传播多项式速度衰减，得到局部质量和能量密度的无条件上界，从而排除了流体力学量的内爆可能性。结合修正的质量扩散法与去饱和技术，证明了粗糙初始数据（速度方向呈多项式衰减）下经典解的全局存在性，这与经典Landau和Boltzmann方程形成鲜明对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Landau和Boltzmann方程在非微扰框架下的全局存在性问题长期未获解决。Landau-Fermi-Dirac方程作为其量子修正版本，Pauli不相容原理在数学上提供了额外的先验L∞界，这可能为突破这一长期难题提供新途径。

Method: 1) 利用Pauli不相容原理带来的先验L∞界传播多项式速度衰减，获得局部质量和能量密度的无条件上界，排除内爆。2) 结合修正的质量扩散方法实现去饱和（desaturation），从而处理量子效应的饱和问题。3) 证明方法几乎完全自包含，仅使用线性动力学方程的稳健通用估计；局部存在性的证明更接近抛物型方程理论，基于弱解和更简单的函数空间。

Result: 证明了粗糙初始数据（速度方向多项式衰减）下Landau-Fermi-Dirac方程全局经典解的存在性，这是非微扰框架下的全局存在性结果，在经典Landau和Boltzmann方程理论中没有可比拟的结果。

Conclusion: 该工作不仅为Landau-Fermi-Dirac方程建立了新的全局存在性理论，还提供了一种基于线性方程通用估计的简洁证明路线图，可能为其他动力学方程的适定性研究提供范式参考。

Abstract: We consider the spatially inhomogeneous Landau-Fermi-Dirac equation with Coulomb potential, a quantum modification of the classical Landau equation for fermions. Mathematically, the Pauli exclusion principle manifests as an additional a priori $L^\infty$-bound for solutions. Using this bound, we propagate polynomial decay in velocity, yielding unconditional upper bounds on the local mass and energy densities, thereby ruling out the possibility of implosions in the hydrodynamic quantities. Combining this estimate with a modified mass spreading method that yields desaturation, we deduce the existence of global-in-time classical solutions for rough initial data with polynomial decay in velocity. This result stands in stark contrast to the theory for the classical Landau and Boltzmann equations, for which no comparable nonperturbative global existence result is known despite sustained effort.
  Our treatment is almost entirely self-contained, using only robust, generic estimates for linear kinetic equations. In particular, our proof of local existence, in contrast to prior works, more closely mirrors the theory for parabolic equations using weak solutions and simpler function spaces. It may provide a concise roadmap to organizing, adapting, and applying the various linear and nonlinear estimates to obtain well-posedness for kinetic equations.

</details>


### [252] [Higher-order Approximate Symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31098)
*Alexey Shevyakov*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文将Fushchych–Shtelen(FS)近似对称方法推广到任意阶，建立了其与Baikov–Gazizov–Ibragimov(BGI)方法的对应关系，并对受扰三次波动方程的非线性项进行了二阶分类，最终通过周期行波验证了FS展开的长期有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fushchych和Shtelen在1989年通过小参数展开定义近似对称性，但原始构造仅停留在低阶，且与BGI框架的深层关系尚未厘清。本文旨在将FS构造推广到任意阶，并在明确假设下建立两种近似对称方法的严格对应，同时检验FS展开在长期尺度上的有效性。

Method: 利用系数映射表述将FS系统推广到p阶，在显式假设下证明与BGI方法的对应关系；通过对受扰三次波动方程进行对称分类，确定允许FS膨胀（至二阶）的非线性类型；结合Lorentz代数与继承的FS膨胀进行约化积分；采用相位重整化处理二阶长期项以获得有界、阶一致的近似解。

Result: 分类得到一族幂律非线性及特殊指数处的对数分支。两种方法并不等价：一阶非平凡扰动下，垂直BGI延拓仅选取FS一个分支的真子族；当扰动在二阶首次进入时出现第二个公共分支。1989年Fushchych–Shtelen信中的显式解被恢复为一阶成员。对于整数幂族成员，修正波数与振幅的标度关系与对称分类得到的权一致。

Conclusion: FS与BGI两种近似对称框架存在深刻联系但不可互换，对数修正源于带扰动依赖指数的幂律展开。FS展开在长期尺度上通过相位重整化可保持阶一致性，其标度预测与对称分析吻合，表明该方法对近似解具有实际预测能力。

Abstract: Fushchych and Shtelen defined approximate symmetry by expanding the solution of a perturbed differential equation in the small parameter, replacing the original equation by a triangular system for the expansion coefficients. We extend this construction to arbitrary order. A coefficient-map formulation yields the order-$p$ Fushchych--Shtelen (FS) system and establishes, under explicit hypotheses, a correspondence with the Baikov--Gazizov--Ibragimov (BGI) method.
  The two frameworks are compared for a perturbed cubic wave equation. Up to equivalences, we classify the nonlinearities admitting an FS dilation through second order. The classification consists of a generic power family and logarithmic branches at exceptional exponents. Among perturbations nontrivial at first order, vertical BGI continuations select a proper subfamily of one FS branch, and a second common branch arises when the perturbation first enters at second order; the two methods are related but not interchangeable. Joint reduction by the Lorentz algebra and the inherited FS dilation integrates all resulting FS normal forms; the explicit solutions of the 1989 Fushchych--Shtelen letter are recovered as the first-order members of this reduction. In the part common to both frameworks, the logarithmic corrections arise from the expansion of a power law with a perturbation-dependent exponent.
  Finally, periodic travelling waves are used to examine the long-scale validity of the FS expansion. The first correction is obtained by quadrature, the second-order secular structure is isolated, and phase renormalization produces bounded order-consistent approximations. For integer-power members of the generic family, the amplitude dependence of the corrected wavenumber agrees with the scaling weights obtained from the symmetry classification.

</details>


### [253] [Stationary kinetic Krylov-Safonov estimates in space dimension one](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31158)
*Yuzhe Zhu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明了空间维度一中非散度形式平稳动能Fokker-Planck方程的解具有内部Hölder正则性，且该估计不依赖于扩散系数的小振动条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动能Fokker-Planck方程在非散度形式下的内部正则性是一个重要的分析问题。已有的正则性结果通常要求扩散系数满足某种小振动条件，本文旨在去除这一限制，在更一般的条件下建立Hölder正则性。

Method: 本文在空间维度一（一维速度空间或相空间）的设定下，对非散度形式的平稳动能Fokker-Planck方程进行内部Hölder正则性分析，通过特定的分析技巧建立不依赖扩散系数小振荡条件的先验估计。

Result: 成功证明了该方程解的内部Hölder正则性，且关键估计不依赖于扩散系数的小振荡假设，从而推广了已有的正则性结果。

Conclusion: 本文在空间维度一情形下建立了非散度形式平稳动能Fokker-Planck方程更一般的内部Hölder正则性，去除了扩散系数的小振动条件限制，为该类方程的进一步理论分析提供了新的工具。

Abstract: We prove interior Hölder regularity for solutions of the stationary kinetic Fokker-Planck equation in non-divergence form in space dimension one. The estimate is independent of any small-oscillation condition on the diffusion coefficient.

</details>


### [254] [Finite-energy spacetimes with torus symmetry. Cauchy stability of Einstein-Euler and Einstein-Navier-Stokes areal flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31171)
*Bruno Le Floch,Philippe G. LeFloch*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文在T^3上建立具有T^2对称性的真空与物质时空的有限能量Cauchy稳定性。作者引入JKL形式的Einstein面流公式（一阶演化-约束系统，耦合波映射结构、双曲物质平衡律和加权的输运方程），并定义两类双曲Einstein-Navier-Stokes模型。对规则有限能量流建立了极大值与不变域原理、几何与能量单调性公式、类空与类时能量估计及全变差估计，从而在未来膨胀区域得到有限能量Cauchy稳定性，在收缩区域直到类空体积坍缩前保持稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 爱因斯坦方程在物质存在下的Cauchy稳定性问题长期悬而未决。以往的工作多局限于真空或特定物质模型（如尘埃、辐射流体），缺乏对一般状态方程下有限能量解的稳定性理论。本文旨在建立涵盖真空和物质、在一般双曲超弹性本构关系下的有限能量Cauchy稳定性框架，同时将Navier-Stokes型耗散机制纳入Einstein方程的稳定性分析。

Method: (1) 利用对称轨道面积作为时间函数，提出JKL公式化的Einstein面流——将问题转化为一阶演化-约束系统，包含波映射结构、双曲物质平衡律以及twists和切向动量的加权输运方程。(2) 引入Navier-Stokes势和弛豫率映射，构造两类双曲Einstein-Navier-Stokes模型：粒子产生模型（散度自由的物质应力张量、非负粒子数产生）和耗散能量模型（守恒粒子数、耗散流体质能，辅助应力恢复整体应力-能量守恒）。(3) 建立极大值原理、不变域原理、几何/能量单调性公式、类空/类时能量估计和全变差估计。

Result: 在规则的有限能量（Einstein、Euler、Navier-Stokes）流中建立了上述全部估计，并由此得出：未来膨胀区域内的有限能量Cauchy稳定性，以及收缩区域内直到类空体积坍缩前的稳定性。估计独立于粘度（或弛豫）机制，覆盖真空、无界质能密度和任意有限快度情形，并推广到满足参考数学熵不等式的弱正则Einstein-Euler面流。

Conclusion: 本文首次在一般本构方程（满足双曲性和真空/大质能密度处的温和渐近条件）下，针对T^2对称时空建立了统一的有限能量Cauchy稳定性理论，同时涵盖了真空、Euler流体和两类Navier-Stokes型粘性流体，且稳定性估计不依赖于具体耗散机制。

Abstract: We establish finite-energy Cauchy stability for vacuum and matter spacetimes with $T^2$ symmetry on $T^3$, under general constitutive equations satisfying hyperbolicity and mild asymptotic conditions at vacuum and large mass-energy density. First, using the area of the symmetry orbits as a time function, we introduce the JKL formulation of Einstein areal flows}, a first-order evolution-constraint system coupling a wave-map structure for the geometry to hyperbolic matter balance laws and weighted transport equations for the twists and the momentum tangent to the symmetry orbits. Second, we also define two classes of hyperbolic Einstein-Navier-Stokes models, constructed from (as we call them) a Navier-Stokes potential and a relaxation rate map. The proposed particle-production model has divergence-free matter stress and non-negative particle-number production, while the proposed dissipated-energy model conserves particle number and dissipates fluid mass-energy, while an auxiliary stress restores the total stress-energy conservation required by the contracted Bianchi identity. For regular finite-energy (Einstein, Euler, Navier-Stokes) flows, we establish maximum and invariant-domain principles, geometric and energy monotonicity formulas, spacelike and timelike energy estimates, and total-variation estimates. Third, together, these estimates yield finite-energy Cauchy stability in the future expanding regime and, in the contracting regime, until the spacelike volume collapses. They are independent of the viscosity (or relaxation) mechanisms and cover vacuum, unbounded mass-energy density, and arbitrary finite rapidity. They also extend to weakly regular Einstein-Euler areal flows satisfying a reference mathematical entropy inequality.

</details>
